### [1] [Identifying changing partial differential equations using Sampled Local WeakIdent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12479)
*Wenbo Hao,Mengyi Tang,Sung Ha Kang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出SLW-Ident框架，用于从单一数据集识别变化的控制方程（PDE），通过局部采样补丁识别方程、利用残差误差发现方程转换边界，并能处理常数与变系数PDE，且对噪声数据鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于有限元逼近的方法在表示变化系数时存在局限，而局部识别方法虽然计算高效但容易对局部扰动敏感。因此需要一种既能保持局部识别优势又具备全局信息、稳定性的方程识别框架。

Method: SLW-Ident包含以下步骤：(1) 在整个给定域内采样多个补丁；(2) 对每个采样补丁独立识别方程；(3) 利用这些方程的残差误差来发现不同方程之间的转换边界；(4) 将识别方程支撑集不变的区域定义为一个方程区域；(5) 在每个方程区域内选取出现频率最高的方程作为识别结果，并可在区域内求解常数或变系数PDE。方法由不确定性量化理论支撑，给出优势支撑选择的统计误差与补丁数量之间的关系。

Result: 通过多种数值实验，SLW-Ident能够准确恢复每个方程的区域以及变化的PDE控制方程，包括带变系数的情形，即使在含噪声的数据下也能保持较高的识别精度。

Conclusion: SLW-Ident成功结合了局部识别的效率与全局信息的稳定性，为从数据中发现分段或变化的控制方程提供了一种可靠且计算高效的框架，并具备理论保证。

Abstract: We propose Sampled Local WeakIdent (SLW-Ident), a framework for identifying changing governing equations from a single set of given data. Different from a typical approach of using finite element based approximation to represent varying coefficients, this paper explores a local approach in identification of differential equations. First, we present the power of Local WeakIdent which gives good local identification and is also computationally efficient with a small patch size, yet it can be sensitive to local perturbations. We propose SLW-Ident which stabilizes the identification process and also incorporates global information: we first sample patches in the whole given domain, identify equations for each sampled patch, then use residual error of these equations to find the transitions between different equations. We refer to a region where the support of the identified equation does not change to be a region of one equation. Within each region of one equation, we pick the most frequently identified equation as the identified equation, and find constant as well as varying coefficient PDEs within each region of one equation. This is justified by an uncertainty quantification theory that gives the relation between statistical error of dominant support selection and the number of patches. We provide various numerical experiments showing that SLW-Ident accurately recovers the regions of one equation and the governing equations for changing PDEs with varying coefficients even with noisy given data.

</details>


### [2] [BTF-PINN: Enforcing Dirichlet Boundary Conditions Without Boundary Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12823)
*Wenyu Dong,Shuo Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出BTF-PINN（免边界训练物理信息神经网络），一种仅依赖内部区域的策略来求解齐次Dirichlet边值问题，无需边界训练、边界惩罚或边界相容参数化。核心思想是将本质边界条件嵌入新设计的无边界损失函数中，并在理论上证明了其与原始边值问题的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 齐次Dirichlet边值问题是非插值方法求解Dirichlet问题的核心难点。传统PINN方法通常需要边界惩罚或边界参数化来处理边界条件，这些方法可能在边界处精度不足或增加训练复杂度。作者希望设计一种不需要任何边界训练的纯内部区域策略。

Method: BTF-PINN的关键在于：1）设计了一种不含边界项的新型变分损失函数，将本质边界条件隐式嵌入其中；2）证明了该内部区域变分公式与原边值问题的等价性；3）建立了残差权重的尖锐阈值条件；4）基于泛函的强制性（coercivity）发展了一致性收敛分析。

Result: 通过高维问题、不规则几何和各项异性椭圆方程上的数值实验验证了BTF-PINN的有效性。与标准边界惩罚PINN的对比表明，BTF-PINN在边界迹精度（boundary trace accuracy）上表现更优。

Conclusion: BTF-PINN作为一种完全免边界训练的方法，在理论上具有严谨的等价性和收敛性保证，在实际中能够获得比传统边界惩罚PINN更高的边界精度，为求解齐次Dirichlet边值问题提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: The homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problem captures the core difficulty of solving Dirichlet problems with non-interpolatory methods. We propose BTF-PINN (Boundary-Training-Free Physics-Informed Neural Network), an interior-only strategy for solving homogeneous Dirichlet boundary value problems that requires no boundary training, boundary penalties, or boundary-conforming parametrizations. The key idea is to embed the essential boundary condition into a newly designed boundary-free loss function. We prove the equivalence between the proposed interior-only variational formulation and the original boundary value problem, establish a sharp threshold condition for the residual weight, and develop a convergence analysis based on the coercivity of the functional. Numerical experiments on high-dimensional problems, irregular geometries, and anisotropic elliptic equations demonstrate the effectiveness of BTF-PINN. Comparisons with standard boundary-penalty PINNs further show that BTF-PINN achieves superior boundary trace accuracy.

</details>


### [3] [Finite element approximation of the linearized Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12833)
*Ali Mesforush,Stig Larsson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究线性化Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程的空间离散（有限元）和时间离散（后向Euler）的强收敛估计，在Wiener过程协方差算子的合适假设下，基于解析半群框架给出误差界，结果可理解为对非线性Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程温和解中随机卷积项的逼近分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程是描述相分离动力学的随机偏微分方程，其数值逼近的收敛性分析具有重要意义。研究者希望在线性化情形下建立严格的强收敛估计，为非线性方程的全离散逼近提供理论基础。

Method: 采用标准有限元方法对空间变量进行离散，使用后向Euler格式进行时间离散；整个分析建立在解析半群框架下，主要工作集中于推导对应的确定性Cahn-Hilliard方程的详细误差界，并在此基础上引入随机项的处理。

Result: 在Wiener过程协方差算子的适当假设下，证明了空间（有限元）和时间（后向Euler）离散化的强收敛估计；所获得的误差界可被解释为对非线性方程温和解中随机卷积项逼近的结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为线性化Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程的全离散数值逼近提供了严格的强收敛性理论保证，其分析框架和误差界对理解非线性Cahn-Hilliard-Cook方程的随机卷积逼近具有直接的推广意义。

Abstract: The linearized Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation is
  discretized in the spatial variables by a standard finite element
  method. Strong convergence estimates are proved under suitable
  assumptions on the covariance operator of the Wiener process,
  which is driving the equation. The backward Euler time stepping is
  also studied. The analysis is set in a framework based on analytic
  semigroups. The main effort is spent on proving detailed error
  bounds for the corresponding deterministic Cahn-Hilliard
  equation. The results should be interpreted as results on
  approximation of the stochastic convolution, which is a part of
  the mild solution of the nonlinear Cahn-Hilliard-Cook equation.

</details>


### [4] [A robust and optimal method for a sixth-order elliptic singular perturbation problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12886)
*Mingqing Chen,Jianguo Huang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对具有小参数0<ι<1的梯度弹性Kirchhoff板（GEKP）模型，提出了一种鲁棒的非协调有限元方法。该方法在离散空间中采用H³三次有限元，而在双线性形式的低阶项和右端项中采用插值映射到H²非协调有限元空间。该数值方法对参数ι具有鲁棒性，无需任何稳定化项即可一致地以h阶收敛。数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度弹性Kirchhoff板模型包含小参数ι，普通有限元方法可能对该参数缺乏鲁棒性，导致收敛性依赖于ι。因此需要设计一种对该参数不敏感、收敛阶均匀的数值方法。

Method: 提出一种鲁棒非协调有限元方法：离散空间采用H³三次有限元；在双线性形式的低阶项及右端项中，通过插值映射将函数映射到H²非协调有限元空间。该方法无需添加稳定化项即可保证关于参数ι的鲁棒性和O(h)的一致收敛。

Result: 所提出的数值方法对参数ι具有鲁棒性，且无需任何稳定化项即可达到关于网格尺寸h的一阶一致收敛。数值实验验证了理论分析结果的正确性。

Conclusion: 该非协调有限元方法为解决带有小参数的梯度弹性Kirchhoff板模型提供了一种鲁棒且高效的数值方案，避免了稳定化项带来的额外复杂度，并保证了均匀的最优阶收敛。

Abstract: In this paper we propose a robust nonconforming finite method for a gradient-elastic Kirchhoff plate (GEKP) model with a small parameter $0<ι<1$. While the method employs an $H^3$ cubic finite element for the discrete space, it employs an interpolation mapping into an $H^2$ nonconforming finite element space in both the lower-order terms of the bilinear form and the right-hand side. Notably, the numerical method is robust with respect to the parameter $ι$ and uniformly convergent to order $h$ without any stabilized terms. Finally, some numerical experiments are performed to verify the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [5] [A Counterexample to Robust Second-Order Convergence of the Strang Projector-Splitting Integrator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12893)
*Shiheng Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明经典的Strang投影分裂积分器在标准假设下只能保证一阶收敛，无法稳健地升级为二阶。通过构造3×3矩阵微分方程的具体反例，展示了局部误差中不可消除的h²项，其机制是与小奇异值相关的因子方向快速旋转，破坏了对称Strang组合的相消性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数值实践中Strang投影分裂积分器通常表现出二阶收敛，但现有误差分析在小奇异值趋近于零时只能证明一阶。作者旨在揭示这一差距是否是理论上的本质缺陷（即无法被改进），而非分析技术上的不足。这关系到该方法鲁棒性的理论边界。

Method: 构造3×3矩阵微分方程的反例。这些方程在时间上是C²光滑、在矩阵变量上光滑，初始数据秩为2，并满足均匀有界性、Lipschitz、切向缺陷和正则性界。然后分析精确子流动Strang方法在一个由四个Strang步组成的周期强迫循环中的局部误差，证明其存在非零的h²项，并通过循环重复排除全局二阶界的可能性。

Result: 构造的反例表明，精确子流动Strang方法在标准假设下局部误差存在非零的h²项。其机制是小奇异值对应因子方向的快速旋转：一阶一致性得以保持，但变化的投影空间阻止了对称Strang组合预期的相消效应。因此，在标准假设下无法建立稳健的二阶收敛界。

Conclusion: 经典投影分裂方法的稳健一阶结果在给定假设下不可升级为稳健二阶。这一差距是内禀的，不是由分析不精细造成的。该结果明确了该方法在当前理论框架下的收敛阶本质上限。

Abstract: The classical Strang projector-splitting integrator is widely observed to converge with order two, whereas the error analysis that remains uniform as the smallest singular value retained in the low-rank approximation tends to zero proves only order one. We show that this gap is intrinsic under the standard assumptions. We construct $3\times3$ matrix differential equations that are $C^2$ in time and smooth in the matrix variable, with rank-two initial data that satisfy uniform boundedness, Lipschitz, tangency-defect, and regularity bounds. Nevertheless, the exact-subflow Strang method has a nonzero $h^2$ term in the local error over one periodic forcing cycle consisting of four Strang steps. Repetition of that cycle rules out a global second-order bound whose constant and stepsize threshold are independent of the retained singular values. The mechanism is a rapid rotation of the factor directions associated with the small singular value: first-order consistency is preserved, but the changing projection spaces prevent the cancellation normally expected from a symmetric Strang composition. Hence the robust first-order result cannot, under these assumptions alone, be upgraded to robust second order for the classical projector-splitting method.

</details>


### [6] [Analysis of Error Propagation in Autoencoder-Based Reduced-Order Neural Ordinary Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13132)
*Jingyi Zhang,Gwanghyun Jo*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种路径积分恒等式，将神经ODE降阶模型的长期外推误差分解为局部误差注入与潜在动力学放大两部分，并通过多步Jacobian范数据此区分不同传播机制。在Burgers和Gray-Scott系统上发现两种不同的误差演化模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经ODE降阶模型虽然在局部预测精度上表现相当，但其长时间外推行为可能差异显著。现有方法缺乏对这种差异的系统性分析工具，需要一种数学框架来区分局部误差注入与误差在潜在动力学中的放大效应。

Method: 开发路径积分恒等式，将局部误差注入与潜在动力学中的误差放大分离；利用多步Jacobian范数量化传输敏感性，用于区分不同的误差传播机制。

Result: 在Burgers系统中，预测误差保持有界，主要与持续性的局部误差相关；在Gray-Scott系统中，外推期间存在显著的误差放大，此时Jacobian范数更适合作为敏感性诊断指标，而非物理预测精度的直接指示。

Conclusion: 神经ODE降阶模型的长时外推误差行为取决于系统动力学特性：不同系统表现出截然不同的误差传播模式，Jacobian范数在不同场景下的解释意义也不同。

Abstract: Neural ODE reduced-order models often achieve comparable local prediction accuracy, yet their long-horizon extrapolation behavior can differ substantially. To analyze this discrepancy, we develop a path-integral identity that separates local discrepancy injection from amplification in the learned latent dynamics. The associated multi-step Jacobian norms quantify transport sensitivity and distinguish different propagation regimes. Experiments on the Burgers and Gray--Scott systems exhibit two distinct patterns of error evolution. In Burgers systems, prediction errors remain bounded and are primarily associated with persistent local discrepancies. In contrast, Gray--Scott systems exhibit pronounced amplification during extrapolation, where Jacobian norms serve as sensitivity diagnostics rather than direct indicators of physical prediction accuracy.

</details>


### [7] [Performance Evaluation of an Adaptive Quadrature and a Double Exponential Formula Using Arbitrary-Precision Floating-Point Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13187)
*Tomonori Kouya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文实现了基于GNU MPFR库的AQE11D（延展的9点牛顿-柯特斯自适应规则）和Takahasi-Mori双指数（DE）公式，并在Kahaner的21个测试问题上进行了评估。AQE11D在所有21个问题上在$10^{-50}$和$10^{-100}$两种绝对容差下均达到目标精度，但在强端点奇异性（如$1/\sqrt{x}$）下所需函数评估次数约为DE公式的$7\times10^4$倍；DE公式在两种容差下收敛于18个问题，展示了其对抗端点奇异性的优势，但在积分区间内部有奇异性的问题上会失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高精度数值积分场景中，不同的积分算法对端点奇异性和内部奇异性有不同的处理能力。作者旨在使用任意精度算术系统性地比较AQE11D和DE公式在极端容差（$10^{-50}$和$10^{-100}$）下的性能表现，从而揭示两种方法在不同奇异性类型下的相对优势和局限。

Method: 使用GNU Multiple Precision Floating-Point Reliable Library（MPFR）实现任意精度算术，实现了两种数值积分方法：(1) AQE11D——Ninomiya的自适应9点牛顿-柯特斯规则扩展了高阶规则序列；(2) Takahasi和Mori的双指数（DE）公式。在绝对容差$10^{-50}$和$10^{-100}$下，对Kahaner的21个标准测试问题进行评估，比较收敛性和函数评估次数。

Result: AQE11D在两种容差下均成功达到所有21个测试问题的目标精度。但在处理强端点奇异性（如$1/\sqrt{x}$）时，在$10^{-100}$容差下需要约$5.4\times10^7$次函数评估，约为DE公式的$7\times10^4$倍。DE公式在两种容差下分别在18个问题上收敛，表现出对端点奇异性的强大处理能力，但在积分区间内部存在奇异性的问题上无法收敛。

Conclusion: AQE11D具有普适性和可靠性，能处理所有21个测试问题，但面对端点奇异性时效率极低；DE公式在端点奇异性问题上效率远高于AQE11D，但其应用范围受限，无法处理积分区间内部含有奇异性的问题。两种方法各有优劣，互补性强。

Abstract: Using arbitrary-precision arithmetic provided by the GNU Multiple Precision Floating-Point Reliable Library, we implement AQE11D---that is, Ninomiya's adaptive 9-point Newton--Cotes rule extended with a sequence of higher-order rules---and Takahasi and Moris' double exponential (DE) formula. We evaluate them for Kahaner's 21 test problems. For both absolute tolerances $10^{-50}$ and $10^{-100}$, AQE11D attains target accuracy on all 21 problems; however, for strong endpoint singularity such as $1/\sqrt{x}$, it requires about $5.4\times10^{7}$ function evaluations at $10^{-100}$, roughly $7\times10^{4}$ times as many as the DE formula. The formula converges on 18 problems at both tolerances, demonstrating its strength against endpoint singularities but also its failure, as it stands, on problems with a singularity inside the integration interval.

</details>


### [8] [Stochastic resistive Hall--MHD with current fluctuations: martingale weak solutions via a convergent structure-preserving finite element method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13407)
*Agus L. Soenjaya*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究有界凸多面体域上带非线性能量守恒边界条件的随机电阻霍尔磁流体动力学（Hall-MHD）系统，该系统由动量方程中的乘法高斯强迫和感应方程中源自广义欧姆定律的电阻与霍尔部分随机扰动的旋度型噪声驱动。作者基于兼容离散 de Rham 复形发展了全离散、线性隐式、保结构的混合有限元方法，精确保持磁高斯定律，并证明了离散解子序列收敛到有限能量弱鞅解，从而获得带旋度型噪声的随机 Hall-MHD 系统的构造性存在性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机 Hall-MHD 系统在等离子体物理和磁约束聚变等领域具有重要应用，但由于其强非线性、霍尔项和随机噪声的耦合，其数学分析和数值方法研究充满挑战。现有工作多限于光滑域或简单噪声结构，尚未有在凸多面体域上结合非线性边界条件与结构型噪声的构造性存在性和保结构数值方法的研究。

Method: 基于兼容离散 de Rham 复形构建线性隐式混合有限元方法，对动量方程采用乘法高斯噪声，对感应方程采用旋度型结构噪声以反映广义欧姆定律中的电阻与霍尔项随机扰动；该方法精确保持磁高斯定律，并通过紧性论证和鞅方法证明离散解的子序列收敛性。

Result: 证明了全离散格式的解存在子序列弱收敛到有限能量弱鞅解，给出随机 Hall-MHD 系统在带旋度型噪声情形下的构造性存在性结果；数值实验验证了随机动力学行为、磁散度精确消失性质以及方法的收敛性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为凸多面体域上带非线性边界条件的随机 Hall-MHD 系统（含旋度型噪声）提供了构造性存在性证明，同时建立了能精确保持磁高斯定律的保结构数值框架，为后续更复杂的随机磁流体问题的分析与计算奠定基础。

Abstract: We study the stochastic resistive Hall--magnetohydrodynamic (Hall--MHD) system on bounded convex polyhedral domains, subject to nonlinear perfectly conducting boundary conditions. The system is driven by multiplicative Gaussian forcing in the momentum equation together with structured curl-type noise in the induction equation arising from stochastic perturbations of the resistive and Hall parts in the generalised Ohm law. We develop a fully discrete, linearly implicit, structure-preserving mixed finite element method based on a compatible discrete de Rham complex, which preserves the magnetic Gauss law exactly. We prove subsequential convergence of the discrete solutions to a finite-energy weak martingale solution, thereby obtaining a constructive existence result for the stochastic Hall--MHD system with curl-type noise. Numerical experiments illustrate the stochastic dynamics, exact divergence preservation, and convergence of the method.

</details>

Abstract: Pre-trained black-box predictive functions encode knowledge distilled from massive datasets and extensive computation. However, when the available input features differ from those the black box expects, direct use is infeasible. We introduce a method for transferring predictive knowledge from the black box to a new, heterogeneous input space. Our approach decomposes the target regression function into a transferable component, which the black box can inform, and a non-transferable component, which captures information unique to the new space. We propose a two-step neural network procedure, estimating the transferable component from abundant unlabeled feature pairs that bridge the two input spaces and the non-transferable component from limited labels. We derive prediction risk bounds that improve on those of a non-transfer alternative when the non-transferable component is small or smooth, and the procedure adapts to either case. Under additional conditions, the worst-case risk of our estimator is of strictly smaller polynomial order than the minimax risk of estimation from the labeled data alone. We extend the framework to multiple black boxes, each on its own input space, and show that aggregation can reduce prediction error relative to the best single black box. Simulated and real data demonstrate the practical value of the method.

*Yuanyu Li,Jintao Xu,Zijiang Liu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Chen Xie,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出SSPO（结构感知相似性加权偏好优化）方法，通过差异加权leave-one-out基线来解决神经组合优化(NCO)训练中并行解采样组信息利用不充分的问题，在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上取得一致提升，且已在京东生产环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NCO方法依赖并行解采样进行训练，但未能充分利用共生采样解组中的丰富信息。基于偏好优化的方法锚定单一最优解，丢弃了其他解的细粒度质量与结构信号（梯度信号极化问题）；而基于均值基线的方法对所有解均匀加权，使结构近似相同的解充斥基线造成冗余信息并保持高梯度方差（基线冗余问题）。

Method: SSPO通过对所有B个采样解进行联合评分，采用差异加权leave-one-out基线：结构上不同的解获得更高权重，以单一机制同时解决梯度信号极化和基线冗余两个问题。其基线使用零参数、问题自适应的解嵌入，由编码器已有的节点表示构建。

Result: 在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上，SSPO较此前最优锚定基线和均匀权重基线均取得一致改进。与均匀RLOO在TSP和EFL上的直接对比证实，结构感知加权是改进的主要驱动力。SSPO训练的EFL策略已在京东商城的生产设施选址系统中部署，验证了其在大规模场景下的实际可行性。

Conclusion: 结构感知的相似性加权机制有效解决了NCO训练中的两类失败模式，不仅提供了优于现有基线的性能增益，也通过实际生产部署证明了该方法在实际工业场景中的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) relies on parallel solution sampling for training, yet existing methods fail to fully exploit the rich information latent in a co-sampled solution group. Preference-optimization methods anchor on the single best solution and discard fine-grained quality and structural signal from all other peers-a failure we term gradient signal polarization. Mean-based baselines instead weight peers uniformly, so structurally near-identical peers flood the baseline with redundant information and keep gradient variance high-a failure we term baseline redundancy. We propose SSPO (Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization), which scores all $B$ sampled solutions jointly through a dissimilarity-weighted leave-one-out baseline: structurally distinct peers receive higher weight, resolving both failures in a single mechanism. The baseline uses zero-parameter, problem-adaptive solution embeddings built from the encoder's existing node representations. Experiments on TSP, EFL, and JSP benchmarks show consistent gains over prior best-anchor and uniform-weight baselines. A direct comparison against uniform RLOO on TSP and EFL confirms that structure-aware weighting is the primary driver of improvement. The SSPO-trained EFL policy has been deployed in a production facility-location system at JD$\mathord{.}$com, confirming practical viability at scale.

</details>

<details>

Method: 1) 在生成模型假设下，分别推导同步和异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，证明迭代平均值弱收敛于重标度布朗运动；2) 基于随机缩放技术构造渐近枢轴统计量，利用整条QTD路径的信息进行推断；3) 该统计量可在线上计算，无需存储全部迭代轨迹。

Result: 建立了同步与异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，并提出了渐近枢轴的在线推断统计量，大幅降低了内存需求，实现了分布强化学习中的高效统计推断。

Conclusion: 该工作为分布强化学习中的QTD方法提供了严格的统计推断理论基础，并提出了一种内存高效的在线推断方案，兼顾了理论有效性与计算可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to perform statistical inference for quantile temporal difference learning (QTD) in distributional reinforcement learning. Assuming access to a generative model, we first establish functional central limit theorems for both synchronous and asynchronous QTD, which show that the averaged iterates of QTD converge weakly to a rescaled Brownian motion. We next provide online inference methods. Based on random scaling, the inference procedure constructs an asymptotically pivotal statistic for inference by using the information along the whole QTD path. Meanwhile, the proposed statistic can be computed online without storing the entire trajectory of QTD iterates. This substantially reduces the memory requirement and enables efficient statistical inference in distributional reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [12] [Statistical Properties of Robust Learning under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13133)
*Zhiyi Li,Xiaojie Mao,Yunbei Xu,Ruohan Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究分布偏移下DRO（分布鲁棒优化）和RS（鲁棒满足性）两种鲁棒学习框架在目标环境中的有限样本泛化误差界，推导了避免Wasserstein经验集中维度灾难的误差上界，提出信息导向的超参数校准方法，并应用于网络批量问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布偏移下的鲁棒学习分析通常只在源环境或对抗性最坏情况（模糊集）下建立保证，缺乏在目标（偏移后）环境中的有限样本泛化保证，且DRO与RS两种方法缺乏系统性比较。

Method: 首先推导DRO和RS在目标分布下的有限样本泛化误差界，显式刻画鲁棒超参数带来的正则化惩罚与偏移敏感度降低之间的权衡，并避免Wasserstein经验集中的维度灾难；其次在部分偏移信息（如偏移幅度或方向）可用时，提出信息导向的超参数校准方法，并在相同信息下比较两种方法；最后将框架应用于网络批量问题。

Result: 在部分信息场景下，DRO和RS表现出互补的理论和实证行为；在目标环境中获得了避免维度灾难的有限样本泛化误差界；网络批量问题中揭示了鲁棒策略对需求分布正向偏移的响应机理。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了分布偏移下鲁棒学习方法统计性质理解的空白，为比较DRO与RS提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Distributional shifts arise when the target deployment environment differs from the source environment that generated the training data. Robust learning frameworks such as Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) and Robust Satisficing (RS) aim to address this challenge, yet their finite-sample guarantees under such shifts, and their systematic comparison, remain underexplored: existing analyses typically establish guarantees either in the source environment or for adversarial worst-case performance over an ambiguity set. This paper instead studies generalization error in the target environment---the excess loss under the shifted target distribution. Our contributions are threefold. First, we derive finite-sample generalization error bounds in the shifted target environment for both DRO and RS. These bounds explicitly characterize the trade-off between reduced sensitivity to shift and the regularization penalty induced by each method's robustness hyperparameter, and they avoid the curse of dimensionality associated with Wasserstein empirical concentration. Second, when partial shift information such as shift magnitude or direction is available, we propose information-directed hyperparameter calibrations and compare the two methods given the same information. Under these calibrations, and in the partial-information regimes we study, DRO and RS exhibit complementary theoretical and empirical behavior. Finally, we apply the framework to a network lot-sizing problem, using it to interpret how robust policies respond to positive shifts in the demand distribution. Together, these results fill a gap in understanding the statistical properties of robust learning methods under distributional shifts and provide a principled basis for comparing DRO and RS.

</details>


### [13] [High-dimensional networks and mean squared error for possibly misspecified models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13171)
*Lourens Waldorp*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究了高维（参数远多于观测）情境下的网络邻域选择问题。作者证明在最小描述长度（MDL）原则下，无论真实模型是线性还是非线性，都能在保证低假阳性率的前提下正确或保守地估计每个节点的邻域连接。论文将岭参数对均方误差的影响与机器学习中的"双重下降"（double descent）现象联系起来，指出多数邻域选择方法（Lasso、AIC、BIC）会导致伪边（高假阳性率），而MDL通过将模型空间体积纳入惩罚项，在高维条件下能实现正确的邻域选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着网络分析中包含的变量越来越多，出现高维（参数远多于观测）情境。传统邻域选择方法（如Lasso、AIC、BIC）在高维条件下会产生大量伪边（高假阳性率），导致网络结构估计失真。研究者希望通过新的理论方法实现在高维条件下保守（低假阳性率）的邻域估计。

Method: 本文通过理论分析和证明探讨高维条件下邻域选择问题。考察线性与非线性真实模型两种情形，分析岭回归参数对均方误差的影响，并与机器学习中的"双重下降"现象建立联系。核心方法是采用最小描述长度（MDL）原则，将模型空间体积纳入惩罚项，与传统的Lasso、AIC、BIC等选择方法进行对比分析。

Result: 证明了在高维设定下，最小描述长度（MDL）方法在两种情形（模型正确假设为线性和错误假设为线性）下都能实现正确或更小的邻域选择（即低假阳性率）。相比之下，Lasso、AIC、BIC等主流邻域选择方法会产生伪边（高假阳性率）。同时揭示了岭参数对均方误差的影响导致低测试方差，进而产生包含大量边的邻域，这与双重下降现象相关联。

Conclusion: 在高维网络邻域选择中，惩罚项需要纳入模型空间的体积维度。MDL原则可以克服传统方法的不足，在模型设定正确或错误的两种情况下都能实现保守（低假阳性率）的邻域估计，为高维网络分析的邻域选择提供了理论保障。

Abstract: To avoid missing important variables and their connections in networks, more and more variables are included in network analysis. Here we show that in a setting with many more parameters than observations (high-dimensional) it is possible to get a conservative (i.e., low false positive rate) estimate of the neighbourhood for each node (which connections are in the network). A neighbourhood is often estimated with a linear model, and this leads to two interesting cases: (i) If the true model is linear, then neighbourhood selection work reasonably well, and (ii) if the true model is nonlinear, then neighbourhood selection requires a penalty for the high dimensions. Here we show the impact of the ridge parameter on the mean squared error, and how this leads to low test variance and hence to neighbourhoods with large numbers of edges. We connect these insights with results from machine learning, where the so-called double descent (when more parameters are included than observations, the mean squared error goes down a second time) has put the traditional view on model selection upside down. Essentially, for adequate neighbourhood selection in models with a large number of parameters, the volume of the model space needs to be included in the penalty. Most neighbourhood selection methods (e.g., Lasso, AIC, BIC) lead to spurious edges (high false positive rate), but we prove that in the high-dimensional setting, minimum description length leads to correct neighbourhood selection or smaller (low false positive rates) in both cases when either the model is correctly or incorrectly assumed linear

</details>


### [14] [Sinkhorn Linearization and the Spectral Proxy: Unifying the Statistical and Algorithmic Theory of Feature-Parameterized Inverse Optimal Transport via a Single Spectral Sandwich](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13201)
*Han Dong,Jiaming Li,Yongqiang Gong,Ruixi Li,Yin Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了特征参数化代价函数C_θ(i,j)=-θ^T φ(i,j)下逆最优传输(IOT)的统计与算法理论框架。核心贡献是Sinkhorn线性化（熵OT计划对代价的隐函数敏感性）及其谱代理公式，并在受限Hessian满足谱三明治界的基础上，推导出四个定理（可辨识性、稀疏一致性、适定性、收敛性）和一个观察（误设定分析），构成了IOT的完整理论体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优传输问题旨在从观测到的传输计划中恢复未知的代价函数参数θ。然而该问题的统计可辨识性、估计器的一致性和计算收敛性等核心理论问题尚缺乏系统性的刻画。本文旨在通过Sinkhorn线性化这一关键技术，为特征参数化IOT提供统一的、谱意义下精确且几何透明的理论框架，填补该领域的理论空白。

Method: 方法核心包括：(1) Sinkhorn线性化——利用隐函数定理推导熵OT计划对代价参数的敏感性，并给出谱精确的代理公式；(2) 在切空间上分析受限Hessian，证明其满足谱三明治界 (π_min/ε)I ≤ H_T^{-1} ≤ (π_max/ε)I，由此导出核心界 σ_min ≥ (π_min/(a_max ε))√λ_min(Σ)；(3) 基于该核心界分别推导可辨识性（商空间上的全局单射）、稀疏一致性（l1惩罚估计的支撑集恢复）、适定性（特征矩映射的强单调性与Lipschitz逆）及梯度下降收敛性（局部强凸性）四大定理，并通过数值实验刻画误设定下的Hölder连续性经验指数。

Result: 建立了四个理论结果：T1证明了θ在规范核的商空间上全局单射，给出维度界F≤(K-1)^2；T2证明了l1惩罚估计器在不可表示性和得分集中条件下以指数小概率失败恢复真实支撑集；T3证明了特征矩映射M(θ)的强单调性且逆为Lipschitz连续；T4证明了在μ≥π_min²λ_min(Σ)/ε²条件下梯度下降单调收敛。此外观察到误设定情形下估计器收敛到OT模型的真值投影，经验Hölder指数α_eff∈(0,1)呈设定依赖性。

Conclusion: 本文通过Sinkhorn线性化及其谱代理，为特征参数化逆最优传输建立了统摄可辨识性、统计推断与优化收敛的完备理论。谱三明治界作为统一工具，将熵正则化参数ε、传输计划上下界与特征协方差的最小特征值联系起来，形成了贯穿全部结果的单一核心界。这为IOT的实际应用提供了坚实的理论基础，并为后续研究（如更一般代价结构、高维问题等）开辟了方向。

Abstract: We develop the statistical and algorithmic theory of inverse optimal transport (IOT) under the feature-parameterized cost C_theta(i,j) = -theta^T phi(i,j). The core technical contribution is the Sinkhorn linearization -- the implicit-function sensitivity of the entropic OT plan to the cost -- together with its spectral proxy, a formula that is spectrally exact yet geometrically transparent.
  The restricted Hessian on the tangent space satisfies the spectral sandwich (pi_min/epsilon) I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon) I, yielding the single core bound sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma)) that drives the entire theory. On this core we establish four theorems and one observation.
  T1 (identifiability): theta is globally injective on the quotient of the gauge kernel, with dimension bound F <= (K-1)^2. T2 (sparsistency): the l1-penalized estimator recovers the true support under irrepresentability and score concentration, with exponential failure probability. T3 (well-posedness): the feature-moment map M(theta) = Phi^T x_theta is strongly monotone, and the inverse is Lipschitz with constant L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma)). T4 (convergence): local strong convexity with mu >= pi_min^2 lambda_min(Sigma) / epsilon^2 guarantees monotone gradient descent convergence. O5 (misspecification): the estimator converges to the OT-model projection of the truth; the Holder continuity of the projection map is assessed numerically, yielding setting-dependent empirical exponents alpha_eff in (0,1).

</details>


### [15] [Wasserstein Filtering: A Sample Selection Method for Robust Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13418)
*Yikai Xu,Zhao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein Filtering (WF)，一种基于样本选择的框架，通过保留与污染经验分布Wasserstein距离最大的子集来剔除几何上显著的离群点，从而恢复底层的干净数据分布。文章提供了三种可计算算法（SinkMarg、SinkWF、SlicedWF），引入了新的FELP污染模型，并证明了WF估计量在协方差有界的分布族上达到极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实数据集中，样本可能被污染，核心目标是恢复潜在的干净分布。需要一种框架能有效识别并丢弃可疑样本，同时保证估计的理论性质。

Method: 提出Wasserstein Filtering框架：选择一个子集，使其经验分布与完全污染的经验分布之间的Wasserstein距离最大化，从而隔离并移除几何上具有影响力的异常样本。为实现可计算性，提出三种算法：边缘筛选方案SinkMarg（基于熵正则最优传输）、联合优化算法SinkWF（熵最优传输）和SlicedWF（切片Wasserstein近似）。同时引入FELP（Far Exclusion and Local Projection）污染模型刻画由良好分离的离群点和局部不可区分扰动组成的污染。

Result: 理论上，在FELP污染模型下，证明了WF估计量在协方差有界分布族上达到极小极大最优性。实验上，在合成数据集、基准异常检测套件以及基于扩散模型的鲁棒生成学习上验证了WF的高度实用性，在异常检测性能和重污染下的生成建模下游任务中均有显著提升。

Conclusion: WF是一种实用、模型无关的预处理工具，能够有效完成离群点检测并为重污染场景下的生成建模提供实质性收益。

Abstract: Given a dataset where a portion of the samples are contaminated, our goal is to recover the underlying clean population distribution. To this end, we propose Wasserstein Filtering (WF), a novel sample selection framework that discards a fraction of suspicious samples and estimates the target distribution using the empirical measure of the remaining data. The core insight is to select a subset of samples whose empirical distribution maximizes its Wasserstein distance to the fully contaminated empirical distribution, thereby preferentially isolating and removing geometrically influential outliers. To render this optimization computationally tractable, we introduce three algorithms: a marginal screening scheme, SinkMarg, and two joint optimization algorithms, SinkWF and SlicedWF, leveraging entropic optimal transport and sliced Wasserstein approximations, respectively. On the theoretical front, we introduce the Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP) contamination model, which characterizes corruptions consisting of well-separated outliers and locally indistinguishable perturbations. Under this model, we prove that the WF estimator achieves minimax optimality over distribution families with bounded covariance. Extensive numerical experiments on synthetic datasets, benchmark anomaly detection suites, and robust generative learning with diffusion models demonstrate that WF serves as a highly practical, model-agnostic preprocessing tool. It delivers competitive outlier detection performance and provides substantial downstream benefits for generative modeling under heavy contamination.

</details>

Conclusion: 对抗鲁棒学习可以通过简单的Bagging+RERM组合实现线性样本复杂度，这揭示了随机化和聚合策略在鲁棒学习中的强大作用。同时下界结果表明，依赖RERM oracle的学习器在查询次数上存在固有限制，对偶VC维是刻画这一复杂度的关键量。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of learning predictors robust to adversarial examples at test-time. We prove that VC classes are adversarially robustly learnable with sample complexity linear in the VC dimension $d$, providing an exponential improvement over the previous upper bound of Montasser, Hanneke, and Srebro (2019). Remarkably, this result is achieved with a simple improper algorithm that combines the classic heuristic bagging (bootstrap aggregation) of Breiman (1996) with robust empirical risk minimization (RERM). Our algorithm computes RERMs on $O(d^\star)$ independent bootstrap samples and outputs their majority vote, where $d^\star$ denotes the dual VC dimension. We complement this result with a lower bound showing that this is unavoidable: in general, any learner in this oracle model requires $Ω(d^\star)$ calls to an RERM oracle, even when given arbitrarily many training examples.

Abstract: It is well-established that single-axis Transverse Ray Transform tomography data from high-energy X-ray diffraction is insufficient for general reconstruction of three-dimensional elastic strain. Lionheart, An explicit reconstruction algorithm for the transverse ray transform of a second rank tensor field from three axis data, Inverse Problems 32 (11) (2016) 115009], we formulate this constrained single-axis tomography problem in the Fourier domain and show that the resulting system is invertible everywhere outside a finite collection of measure zero characteristic planes. Abstract: We generalize the classical vector-valued tangential and normal trace theory on Lipschitz domains to the setting of non-Lipschitz $H^1$-extension domains, which includes domains with fractal boundaries such as the Koch snowflake. We define and study these operators based on the surjectivity of the trace operator for elements of $H^1(Ω)$ and the Hilbert structure of the associated trace space. The normal trace operator is defined on $\Hdiv$ in $\mathbb{R}^n$. A generalized Stokes formula allows us to introduce the tangential trace operator on $\Hcurl$ and $\Hc^1(Ω)$ in two and three dimensions. Following the approach of Buffa, Costabel and Sheen (2002) for Lipschitz domains, we define two abstract tangential boundary spaces as images of these trace operators, establish their Hilbert structure, and construct an abstract rotation operator linking them, in place of the geometric rotation based on the normal vector. Abstract: In this paper, we study the initial value problem of the sixth-order Boussinesq equation with quadratic and cubic nonlinearities in arbitrary spatial dimensions. Abstract: We audit the exact-arithmetic certificate in arXiv:2310.19781v2, its 2025 \emph{Communications in Mathematical Physics} version, and their shared MATLAB archive. CMP corrects none of the audited items; we therefore treat both versions together and use the openly accessible arXiv source for exact provenance. We found 11 proof-affecting defects (alongside typographical slips) and give reproducible exact counterexamples to several implemented bounds and reconstruction steps. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first independent, version-pinned, source-level audit of a published computer-assisted proof (CAP) in analysis to identify multiple proof-affecting defects in its released computational certificate. The findings do not refute the intended theorem or its analytic reduction, but they show that the published certificate does not prove the claimed conclusion. Abstract: We perform a linearized stability analysis of the Vlasov--Fokker--Planck equation obtained as the mean-field description of a stochastic spontaneous aggregation model with memory. When all relaxation rates are equal, we show that every Fourier mode that is unstable in the memoryless model possesses a unique critical relaxation parameter: sufficiently long memory stabilizes the mode, whereas it remains unstable for short memory. Thus, memory cannot create an overall instability of an otherwise stable homogeneous state, although it may change the set of the unstable modes. We present a numerical example for the normalized top-hat kernel, demonstrating that increasing the effective memory length successively stabilizes the Fourier modes, with higher frequencies being stabilized before lower ones. This is consistent with the coarsening effect observed in recent particle simulations. Finally, under suitable nondegeneracy and regularity assumptions, we use the Crandall--Rabinowitz theorem to obtain branches of spatially inhomogeneous stationary solutions bifurcating from the homogeneous state.

Method: 采用正则化技巧结合两点极大值原理证明第一正特征函数的对数凹性。通过构造坍缩的平滑凸域来识别p=2处的sharp转变现象。对于p≥2，建立退化加权Poincaré不等式，由此推导L^p-Poincaré不等式的定量稳定性估计及基本间隙的维度无关界。利用变分方法和紧性论证证明直径归一化间隙极小子的存在性与退化性。对于N=1情形，直接推导sharp不等式并确定等号成立条件。

We also prove existence of diameter-normalized gap minimizers for \(p>2\) and show that they degenerate as \(p\downarrow2\). Finally, for $N=1,$ we prove the sharp inequality \[ λ_{2,p}(I_D,V)-λ_{1,p}(I_D,V) \geq (p-1)(2^p-1)\left(\frac{π_p}{D}\right)^p \] for every \(p>1\) and every convex potential, with equality precisely for constant potentials.

Abstract: We solve the Fokker-Planck equation for a Brownian particle in a tilted cosine potential and derive the intermediate scattering function (ISF), which captures the full spatio-temporal dynamics of the system. The model consists of a single overdamped Brownian particle in one dimension. We derive a generalized ISF comprising two wave vectors to describe correlations in the periodic potential. Exploiting the periodicity via Bloch's theorem, we formulate the problem within a spectral-theoretical framework and numerically compute the corresponding eigenfunctions and eigenvalues, from which we obtain the ISF and the probability density. Using time-dependent perturbation theory, we expand the ISF and derive low-order moments, including the mean-square displacement, time-dependent diffusivity, skewness, and the non-Gaussian parameter. Our analytical results are validated by Brownian-dynamics simulations and analyzed focussing on different regimes of the tilting force. Abstract: Using patch-matrix-product methods, we study two reversible three-state interaction-round-a-face cellular automata introduced by Klobas and Prosen [J. Phys. A 55, 094003 (2022)] - the species-preserving and species-flipping rules - and a coherent quantum deformation interpolating between them. Within an explicit translationally invariant ansatz, the two classical rules share a one-parameter family of quasi-local conservation laws with an auxiliary-dimension-three realization. Every regular member is also the first centered logarithmic derivative of an analytic auxiliary-dimension-two family passing through the identity observable. On the closed span of this family and the three elementary local charges, the only nonzero Euler velocities are $\pm\sqrt{3/23}$. Consequently, $\sqrt{3/23}$ is a rigorous lower bound on the maximal Euler speed in any larger conserved sector. For the species-flipping rule, this value agrees with the reported extrapolated value $0.361$ of Klobas and Prosen, strongly supporting completeness of the known sound-active charges. The species-preserving rule admits, in addition, a staggered generating family whose logarithmic derivatives form an infinite tower of strictly local charges. In the quantum deformation, the same algebraic structures yield exact low-bond-dimension invariant states (scar candidates), exponentially long-lived quasi-local quasimodes, and diagonal quasi-local charges that produce a nonzero Mazur bound after projection away from the three elementary local charges.

Method: 作者为有限状态学习设备构建了类型化核算体系，定义了四个核心组件：训练侧拟合泛函Φ_fit、记录相关性存量J_D=I(M;D)、更新侧搜索账本σ_M、以及运营资本价值V(M;T,b)。通过删除记忆读取端口并从零重新优化的方式定义盲类对照，计算知情协议类与盲类之间的"工作差距"。在flat*区域、F5'稳定条件下的M局部更新以及无丢弃记录相关性条件(f)下推导精确恒等式与不等式界。

Abstract: What a finite learning device has recorded and what will hold value for it on future tasks are not the same quantity. We develop a typed accounting for finite-state learning devices that separates four components: a training-side fit functional $Φ_{\mathrm{fit}}$, the record-correlation stock $J_{D}=I(M;D)$, an update-side search ledger $σ_{M}$, and an operational capital value $V(M;T,b)$. This value is the work gap between an informed protocol class and a blind class obtained by deleting the memory-read port and re-optimizing from scratch. (I) Separation: for every $n$, there is a device family on which record correlation and world correlation grow by $n\ln 2$ while the capital gain is exactly zero. In the $\mathrm{flat}^{*}$ regime, data-free updates never increase $V$. (II) Capitalization ledger: an exact $\mathrm{flat}^{*}$ extraction identity and a universal ledger identity give, for (F5$'$)-stable $M$-local updates under a no-discarded-record-correlation condition (f), the bound $η_{\mathrm{cap}}\le 1$ for the capitalization efficiency $η_{\mathrm{cap}}=ΔV/(k T\,σ_{M})$, together with necessary and sufficient conditions for equality. (III) Value retention: for the retention gap $L_{\mathrm{gen}}$ and retention ratio $ρ_{\mathrm{gen}}$ (the former carries no sign constraint; the latter is defined for positive training-side value and is not confined to $[0,1]$) we give a two-layer alignment domain: an exact exchange rate between value and the side-information-adjusted record fit $I(M';D\mid Y)$ without any record-side-information independence assumption, and a raw record-stock exchange rate under a joint side-information neutrality condition $(M,D)\perp Y$, whose boundary is marked by an explicit one-time-pad witness. These are statements about finite-device value retention under task-distribution shift, not a theory of statistical generalization.

</details>

Method: 作者直接从全三维平衡密度场学习超额自由能泛函，在满足空间对称性和变分一致性的约束下进行训练，且不需要自由能或化学势作为监督标签。训练后的单一泛函可在不同温度、系统尺寸和统计系综间迁移使用。

Abstract: Liquids exhibit collective behavior that depends sensitively on thermodynamic conditions, interfaces and confinement, yet predicting each new state commonly requires a separate atomistic simulation. Classical density functional theory offers a reusable variational description, but its central excess free-energy functional is generally unknown, and learned approximations have largely remained restricted to planar or lower-dimensional settings. Here we show that this functional can be learned directly from fully three-dimensional equilibrium density fields while preserving spatial symmetry and variational consistency, without free-energy or chemical-potential labels. A single learned functional transfers across temperatures, system sizes and statistical ensembles, and recovers structure factors, the equation of state, liquid--vapor coexistence and interfacial broadening, none of which are used as training targets. Applied to complex three-dimensional geometries, it predicts the non-monotonic force associated with formation and rupture of a solvent-depleted bridge between colloids and adsorption in an interconnected gyroid pore. These results demonstrate that equilibrium density data can be converted into a transferable thermodynamic generator connecting microscopic liquid structure to response, phase behavior and collective phenomena.

### [53] [Monodromy of plane curve singularities and quiver mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12484)
*Roger Casals*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了可展实 Morse 化的箭图突变类唯一决定平面曲线奇异性的整幺模单演模，在可展不可约情形下建立了 Fomin-Pylyavskyy-Shustin-Thurston 猜想中的代数到拓扑蕴含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在建立代数结构与拓扑结构之间的深层对应关系，验证 S. Fomin、P. Pylyavskyy、E. Shustin 和 D. Thurston 提出的猜想：箭图突变类应能唯一决定平面曲线奇异性的拓扑类型。此前该猜想的蕴含方向尚未在一般情形下被证明。

Method: 作者发展了基于表示论的技术：利用微分双分次 Ginzburg 代数的导出范畴中连续有限维 dg 模上的等变 Euler 配对作为核心工具。关键步骤是通过这些技术证明 plabic fence 的箭图突变类能唯一恢复相关光滑链环的 Alexander 模的挠部分。

Result: 证明了可展实 Morse 化的箭图突变类唯一决定平面曲线奇异性的积分幺模单演模；特别地，可展 divide 的箭图突变类唯一决定不可约平面曲线奇异性的复拓扑类型，从而建立了该猜想在可展不可约情形下的代数到拓扑蕴含。

Conclusion: 本文在可展不可约情形下解决了 Fomin-Pylyavskyy-Shustin-Thurston 猜想中从代数到拓扑方向的蕴含问题，表明箭图突变类作为代数不变量能够完整恢复平面曲线奇异性的拓扑信息。

Abstract: The main result of this article shows that the quiver mutation class of a malleable real Morsification uniquely determines the integral monodromy module of a plane curve singularity. In particular, we show that the quiver mutation class of a malleable divide determines the complex topological type of an irreducible plane curve singularity. This establishes the algebraic-to-topological implication of a conjecture of S.~Fomin, P.~Pylyavskyy, E.~Shustin and D.~Thurston in the malleable irreducible case. The result is proven by developing representation-theoretic techniques based on an equivariant Euler pairing in the derived category of continuous finite-dimensional dg modules over a differential bigraded Ginzburg algebra. A key step uses these techniques to show that the quiver mutation class of a plabic fence uniquely recovers the torsion part of the Alexander module of the associated smooth link.

</details>

*You-Cheng Chou,Hsian-Hua Tseng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单纯稳定曲线模栈上的后代积分，利用约化态射的初等墙穿越分解，得到了用普通Witten-Kontsevich关联子表示的重构公式，并由此推导出修正的Virasoro递推关系。所有有限支撑理论被封装到完备的不相交支撑Fock空间中，其中扩展势能是Witten-Kontsevich势能的累积量平移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在深化对单纯稳定曲线模空间上后代积分（descendant integrals）的理解，特别是希望通过初等墙穿越（wall crossing）分解来建立与经典Witten-Kontsevich理论之间的联系，从而获得可计算的重构公式和递推关系。

Method: （1）对每个有限碰撞复形，将约化态射分解为初等墙穿越步骤；（2）基于该分解推导出后代积分关于普通Witten-Kontsevich关联子的重构公式；（3）利用重构公式导出修正的Virasoro递推递归；（4）在完备的不相交支撑Fock空间中整合所有有限支撑理论，并将扩展势能表述为Witten-Kontsevich势能的累积量平移形式。

Result: 获得了一个将单纯稳定曲线后代积分还原为普通Witten-Kontsevich关联子的重构公式，并据此得到了修正的Virasoro递归关系；同时在Fock空间框架下建立了扩展势能与Witten-Kontsevich势能之间的累积量平移关系。

Conclusion: 该工作将单纯稳定曲线的后代理论系统性地归结为经典的Witten-Kontsevich理论，揭示了二者之间的结构关系，并提供了统一的代数和递归框架。

Abstract: We study descendant integrals on moduli stacks of simplicially stable curves. For each finite collision complex, we factor the reduction morphism into elementary wall crossings and obtain a reconstruction formula in terms of ordinary Witten-Kontsevich correlators. We use the formula to derive corrected Virasoro recursions. We then package all finite-support theories in a completed disjoint-support Fock space, where the extended potential is a cumulant translate of the Witten-Kontsevich potential.

</details>


### [55] [Collision-Hull Compression for Homogeneous Keller Maps and a Forty-Variable Counterexample to Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12543)
*Thomas Prellberg*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文针对均匀Keller映射提出了一种基于碰撞的初等压缩原理。利用极化代数的语言，构造由碰撞生成的子代数，该子代数携带非单射的Keller限制并控制标准对称提升的维数。对Thompson的24变量三次均匀映射，精确极化给出增长序列2,4,11,20,20，所得子代数恰为MacFarlane的20维不变子空间，从而从碰撞本身规范地恢复了已知的24到20维压缩。文章还给出了一个350单项式的四次齐次映射，使Zhao消失猜想失效，并给出Q(i)上的精确梯度碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在建立一种由碰撞(collision)本身出发、通过极化代数的代数框架统一解释并推广Keller映射压缩现象的一般性原则，而非依赖偶然或特殊构造。同时希望检验赵消失猜想(Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture)的适用范围。

Method: 方法核心是极化代数语言下的碰撞生成子代数构造：(1) 对均匀Keller映射定义碰撞生成的子代数；(2) 证明该子代数具有非单射的Keller限制，并由此控制标准对称提升的维数；(3) 对Thompson的24变量三次均匀映射进行精确极化计算，复现增长序列2,4,11,20,20；(4) 构造350单项式的四次齐次映射及其精确梯度碰撞，并在Q(i)上做精确计算验证。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对Thompson的24变量映射，碰撞生成子代数恰好是MacFarlane的20维不变子空间，规范恢复了24到20的压缩；(2) 给出了350单项式四次齐次映射，其中Zhao消失猜想不成立；(3) 在Q(i)上给出了精确的梯度碰撞；(4) 在Q、Q(i)、C上，在对称提升保持不变之前不存在包含该碰撞的真不变线性子空间。

Conclusion: 碰撞本身即可规范地产生Keller映射的压缩子代数，这为理解Keller映射的维数缩减提供了统一的代数机制。同时Zhao消失猜想存在反例，说明其适用范围需要进一步修正。作者明确声明不声称全局极小性(minimality)，所有主要显式计算均有伴随的精确计算校验。

Abstract: We formulate an elementary collision-generated compression principle for homogeneous Keller maps. In the language of polarization algebras, the construction takes the subalgebra generated by a collision; the additional point is that this subalgebra carries a noninjective Keller restriction and controls the dimension of the standard symmetric lift. For Thompson's 24-variable cubic-homogeneous map, exact polarization gives growth \(2,4,11,20,20\), and the resulting subalgebra is exactly MacFarlane's 20-dimensional invariant subspace. Thus the known \(24\)-to-\(20\) compression is recovered canonically from the collision itself. Although the resulting 40-variable lift had already been noted, we give an explicit 350-monomial homogeneous quartic for which Zhao's Vanishing Conjecture fails, together with an exact gradient collision over \(\Q(i)\). Over \(\Q\), \(\Q(i)\), and \(\C\), no proper invariant linear subspace containing this collision is available before the unchanged symmetric lift. No global minimality is claimed. The principal explicit calculations are checked by an accompanying exact calculation.

</details>


### [56] [Rigidity for Lie algebras of locally finite derivations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12567)
*Mohamed Ali Belabbas*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：特征零域上有限生成交换代数的任意有限生成Lie子代数，若其元素在代数上都是局部有限的，则该Lie代数是有限维的。作为推论，有限生成的局部有限导子Lie子代数的有限维性、对代数的局部有限作用、元素局部有限性三者等价。关键工具是第二定理：元素均为局部幂零的导子Lie子代数是可解的，且约化情形下导出长度以dim A为上界并可达到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究源于仿射簇自同构群及其局部有限/局部幂零导子的结构问题，特别是对Popov在2005年提出的关于两个幺幂单参数子群生成的子群代数性问题提供回答。此前局部有限导子的Lie代数结构缺乏系统刻画，需要新的代数工具。

Method: 核心创新在于引入一个余有限理想，使其覆盖Spec A所有相伴点的闭包；在沿相应有限子簇方向二阶消失的导子子代数上，局部有限性被迫退化为局部幂零性。该子代数与L的交由第二定理可解，且在L中具有有限余维；结合伴随作用的局部有限性，推出L的有限维性。第二定理本身通过Lie根理论证明所有元素局部幂零的导子Lie代数是可解的，并给出约化情形的精确导出长度界。

Result: 1) 有限生成且元素局部有限的Lie导子子代数是有限维的；2) 元素局部幂零的Lie导子子代数可解，约化情形的导出长度至多为dim A且该界是紧的；3) 对仿射簇X，有限个连通代数子群生成的Aut(X)子群为代数子群当且仅当其切代数生成的Lie代数的每个元素局部有限；4) 由此回答了Popov 2005年的问题；5) 给出了多项式控制系统存在以状态坐标为观测的精确有限维双线性实现的刻画。

Conclusion: 文章建立了局部有限导子Lie代数有限维性的完整判据，揭示了局部有限与局部幂零在二阶消失子代数上的本质联系，并通过Lie理论给出了可解性的统一证明。该结果统一并推广了自同构群、代数群作用及多项式系统理论中的多个经典命题。

Abstract: Let $A$ be a finitely generated commutative algebra over a field $\mathbb K$ of characteristic zero. We prove that every finitely generated Lie subalgebra $L\subseteq\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally finite on $A$ is finite-dimensional. Consequently, for a Lie subalgebra generated by finitely many locally finite derivations, the following are equivalent: it is finite-dimensional, it acts locally finitely on $A$, and all its elements are locally finite. A key ingredient is a second theorem of independent interest: every Lie subalgebra of $\operatorname{Der}_{\mathbb K}(A)$ whose elements are locally nilpotent is solvable; when $A$ is reduced, its derived length is at most $\dim A$, and this bound is sharp. For an affine variety $X$ over an algebraically closed field, we deduce that a subgroup of $\operatorname{Aut}(X)$ generated by finitely many connected algebraic subgroups is algebraic if and only if every element of the Lie algebra generated by their tangent algebras is locally finite. For two unipotent one-parameter subgroups, this provides an answer to a problem posed by Popov in 2005. Our results also characterize polynomial control systems admitting an exact finite-dimensional bilinear realization by polynomial observables containing the state coordinates. The proofs rest on the introduction of a cofinite ideal meeting the closure of every associated point of $\operatorname{Spec} A$: on the subalgebra of derivations vanishing to second order along the corresponding finite subscheme, local finiteness forces local nilpotence. The intersection of that subalgebra with $L$ is therefore solvable by the second theorem, and has finite codimension in $L$; together with local finiteness of the adjoint action, this yields finite-dimensionality.

</details>


### [57] [Tame fundamental groups of rigid spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12601)
*Piotr Achinger,Katharina Hübner,Marcin Lara,Jakob Stix*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文引入非阿基米德域K上刚性空间X的tame étale基本群π₁^t(X/K)，证明了在X为准紧准分离（qcqs）且K具有拓扑有限生成tame Galois群（如代数闭域或局部域）的条件下，该基本群拓扑有限生成；若X还是严格半稳定形式概形的刚性一般纤维且其特殊纤维的光滑 locus 允许射影 snc 紧化，则该基本群拓扑有限展示。证明依赖对数几何技术（扩展到有限生成幺半群通常范围之外）、tame log étale 基本群类似的有限性结论，以及 adic 空间映射的"垂直紧化"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非阿基米德几何中，刚性空间的基本群是理解其拓扑和算术结构的重要不变量。作者希望建立刚性空间上tame étale基本群的有限性理论（拓扑有限生成和有限展示），从而为后续研究提供基础工具。这一目标需要对经典对数几何进行推广，因为标准的对数几何框架限于有限生成的幺半群，不足以覆盖一般刚性空间的情形。

Method: 论文采用以下技术途径：(1) 将对数几何方法推广到非有限生成幺半群的情形，从而定义并研究tame log étale基本群；(2) 对于tame log étale基本群证明类似的有限性定理；(3) 利用adic空间的"垂直紧化"（vertical compactification）技术，将刚性空间X与适当的模型建立联系，使得光滑 locus 的射影 snc 紧化条件得以利用，从而推导出基本群的有限展示性质。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 若X是qcqs刚性空间且K的tame Galois群拓扑有限生成（如K为代数闭域或局部域），则π₁^t(X/K)拓扑有限生成；(2) 若X进一步是严格半稳定形式概形的刚性一般纤维，且其特殊纤维的光滑 locus 有射影 snc 紧化，则π₁^t(X/K)拓扑有限展示。

Conclusion: 论文确立了刚性空间上tame étale基本群的有限性定理，并通过对数几何的推广为处理一般刚性空间提供了新方法。这些结果将tame基本群的有限展示性与其模型几何（特殊纤维）的紧化性质联系起来，为深入研究非阿基米德刚性空间的算术基本群结构奠定了基础。

Abstract: We introduce the tame étale fundamental group $π_1^t(X/K)$ of a rigid space X over a non-archimedean field K. We show that if X is qcqs and K has topologically finitely generated tame Galois group (e.g. algebraically closed or a local field), then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely generated. If X is moreover the rigid generic fibre of a strictly semistable formal scheme such that the smooth locus of its special fibre admits a projective snc compactification, then $π_1^t(X/K)$ is topologically finitely presented. The proofs rely on techniques of logarithmic geometry (extended beyond its usual scope of finitely generated monoids), in particular on an analogous finiteness statement for the tame log étale fundamental group, and on the 'vertical compactification' of a map of adic spaces.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文针对最大非奇异实射影簇的Hilbert平方，建立了Smith-Thom亏缺的下界，恢复了已知的曲面情形，并在所有维数至少为2的情况下提供了新的Smith-Thom极大性障碍。作为应用，证明了维数至少为2的实阿贝尔簇的Hilbert平方不是Smith-Thom极大的，且最大实阿贝尔簇的Hilbert平方的亏缺随维数指数增长，还获得了某些笛卡尔积的Hilbert平方的非极大性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smith-Thom极大性是实代数几何中度量实簇拓扑复杂度的重要概念。作者希望将已知的曲面情况推广到更高维，建立更一般的亏缺下界，从而为实射影簇的Hilbert平方提供新的Smith-Thom极大性障碍。

Method: 作者通过建立Smith-Thom亏缺的下界来分析Hilbert平方的拓扑结构。具体方法涉及对最大非奇异实射影簇的Hilbert平方进行亏缺估计，并将该估计应用于实阿贝尔簇和笛卡尔积等具体情形。

Result: 建立了Hilbert平方Smith-Thom亏缺的下界，恢复了已有的二维（曲面）情形；证明了维数至少2的实阿贝尔簇的Hilbert平方非Smith-Thom极大；最大实阿贝尔簇的Hilbert平方亏缺随维数指数增长；对某些笛卡尔积的Hilbert平方也获得了非极大性结果。

Conclusion: 本文的结果在各个维数（≥2）下提供了新的Smith-Thom极大性障碍，说明Hilbert平方在较高维数下普遍不满足Smith-Thom极大性，尤其对实阿贝尔簇和笛卡尔积而言。

Abstract: We establish lower bounds for the Smith--Thom deficiency of the Hilbert square of a maximal nonsingular real projective variety. These bounds recover the known surface case and provide new obstructions to Smith-Thom maximality in every dimension at least two. As applications, we prove that the Hilbert square of any real abelian variety of dimension at least two is not Smith-Thom maximal. We further show that the deficiencies of the Hilbert squares of maximal real abelian varieties grow exponentially with the dimension. We also obtain nonmaximality results for the Hilbert squares of certain Cartesian products.

</details>


### [59] [Topological obstructions to geometric positivity and negativity on Calabi-Yau manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12705)
*Ping Li*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Calabi-Yau流形的基础拓扑是否能够支持自然的几何正性或负性结构。在偶数复维数n≥4（当n≥6时假设b₂=1）下，作者加强了Oguiso-Peternell定理，证明了Calabi-Yau流形不同胚于弱Fano n维流形；同样的障碍适用于具有拟正全纯截面曲率的Kähler流形。变换群类比排除了具有孤立不动点的Hamiltonian圆作用的辛流形。在负性方面，证明了Calabi-Yau流形的基本群不与Kähler双曲流形的基本群同构。这些结果共同展现了将Calabi-Yau流形与受几何正性/负性支配的若干基本类分离的拓扑刚性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Calabi-Yau流形在拓扑层面是否可能承载自然的几何正性或负性结构，这是代数几何与微分几何中的一个基本问题。通过建立拓扑障碍，作者希望将Calabi-Yau流形与具有正性或负性曲率条件的流形类区分开来，深化对Calabi-Yau流形拓扑刚性的理解。

Method: 作者通过加强Oguiso-Peternell定理来证明Calabi-Yau流形不同胚于弱Fano流形；利用全纯截面曲率拟正的条件构造相同的拓扑障碍；通过变换群类比将结果推广到具有孤立不动点的Hamiltonian圆作用的辛流形；在负性方向，通过基本群的不可能性证明Calabi-Yau流形的基本群与Kähler双曲流形的基本群不同构。

Result: 在偶数复维数n≥4且b₂=1（n≥6时）的假设下，证明了Calabi-Yau流形不同胚于弱Fano n维流形；排除了具有拟正全纯截面曲率的Kähler流形；排除了具有孤立不动点的Hamiltonian圆作用辛流形；并证明Calabi-Yau基本群不与Kähler双曲流形基本群同构。

Conclusion: Calabi-Yau流形在拓扑上展现出统一的刚性，使其与由几何正性或负性所支配的多个基本流形类（弱Fano、拟正全纯截面曲率的Kähler流形、带Hamiltonian圆作用的辛流形、Kähler双曲流形）分离，揭示了正性/负性几何结构与Calabi-Yau拓扑之间的深刻对立。

Abstract: We study whether the topology underlying a Calabi-Yau manifold can support natural geometric positivity or negativity structures. In even complex dimension $n \geq 4$ (assuming $b_2=1$ when $n \geq 6$), we strengthen a theorem of Oguiso-Peternell by proving that a Calabi-Yau manifold is not homeomorphic to a weak Fano $n$-fold. The same obstruction applies to Kähler manifolds with quasi-positive holomorphic sectional curvature. A transformation-group analogue excludes, in particular, symplectic manifolds admitting Hamiltonian circle actions with isolated fixed points. On the negative side, we show that the fundamental group of a Calabi-Yau manifold is not isomorphic to that of a Kähler hyperbolic manifold. Taken together, these results exhibit a common topological rigidity separating Calabi-Yau manifolds from several fundamental classes governed by geometric positivity or negativity.

</details>

*Mikhail Kapranov*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文用超对称（supersymmetry）重新诠释了M. Sato于1972年提出的、用于证明Thetanullwerte模性（modularity）的无限阶微分算子（DOI）方法。作者发现，任何奇超对称生成元的"朴素指数"天然构成DOI，并以超群OSp(1|2n)及其metaplectic覆盖作用于Siegel平面的超加厚为框架。n=2时对应三维N=1超共形群和自由无质量标量超多重态；n>2时得到拉格朗日Grassmannian上广义共形结构的超扩展。此外，奇超对称生成元在"on-shell"（方程解空间）满足偶型Heisenberg对易关系，这些运动方程升级为与osp(1|2n)超Weil表示的BGG型消解相对应的微分算子复形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sato提出的DOI方法虽然已被Kashiwara、Kawai、Takei和Yoshida等人发展，但其内在结构和来源尚不十分清晰。作者希望通过超对称视角为DOI提供一个"自然来源"——即奇超对称生成元的指数形式，从而在数学上更深刻地理解这一方法，并揭示其与超共形几何、超表示论的联系。

Method: 作者建立Sato DOI方法、超对称与超群表示论之间的联系：将奇超对称生成元的朴素指数识别为DOI；引入超群OSp(1|2n)（及其metaplectic覆盖）作用于Siegel平面的自然超加厚；对n=2与n>2分别分析其超共形/广义共形结构；研究on-shell奇生成元的Heisenberg型代数关系，并将运动方程构建为对应osp(1|2n)超Weil表示的BGG型消解的微分算子复形。

Result: 给出了Sato DOI方法的超对称解释，明确了Riemann theta函数（亏格n）的情形由OSp(1|2n)超群控制；n=2时对应三维N=1超共形代数和自由无质量标量超多重态（合并Laplace与Dirac方程）；n>2时得到Hermitian对称空间上广义共形结构的超扩展。发现on-shell奇生成元满足偶型Heisenberg对易关系，运动方程对应于超Weil表示的BGG型消解复形。

Conclusion: 该工作为Sato的DOI方法提供了自然的超对称来源，建立了超对称物理（超共形几何、超多重态）与数论/模形式（theta函数模性）之间的桥梁，并揭示了osp(1|2n)超Weil表示的BGG型消解这一深层结构。这为模性证明与超表示论之间提供了新的统一视角。

Abstract: In 1972, M. Sato proposed an approach to proving modularity of forms like Thetanullwerte by characterizing them via certain differential operators of infinite order (DOI) in the modular variable(s) alone. A DOI is an infinite series in derivatives decreasing so fast that it acts on holomorphic functions by a sheaf morphism. This approach was developed by several authors including Kashiwara, Kawai, Takei and Yoshida.
  We give an interpretation of this approach using supersymmetry which provides a natural source of DOIs: the naive exponential of any odd supersymmetry generator is a DOI. The case of the Riemann theta function of genus n is governed by the supergroup OSp(1|2n) (and its metaplectic cover) acting on a natural super-thickening of the Siegel plane. For n=2 this is the 3-dimensional N=1 superconformal group and the structure at hand is precisely the free massless scalar supermultiplet (combining the Laplace and Dirac equations). For n>2 we get a super-extension of the generalized conformal structure existing on the Lagrangian Grassmannian as on any Hermitian symmetric space.
  An additional interesting feature here is that the odd supersymmetry generators acting ``on-shell'' (i.e., in the space of solutions of the equations of motion) satisfy even-style Heisenberg commutation relations. These equations of motion upgrade to a complex of differential operators corresponding to a natural BGG-type resolution of the super-Weil representation of osp(1|2n).

</details>

Abstract: Let $Y$ be a very general prime Fano threefold of genus 7. We exhibit an explicit 2-cycle on $Y\times Y$ that is Abel-Jacobi trivial but non-torsion in the Chow group $A^4(Y\times Y)$. As a consequence, $Y$ does not admit a multiplicative Chow-Künneth decomposition, in the sense of Shen-Vial. <details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规则书按词典序对规则违规排序，模型预测控制通过L+1个顺序程序或加权和（用分离启发式w1>>w2>>...>>wL调参）来强制执行该排序。作者质疑这种广泛使用的分离启发式方法是否正确，因为它回答了一个错误的问题。

Method: 从理论上将加权和精确复现词典序最优解的充要条件形式化为：增强单位性能系数后的权重须支持成就映射在词典序点处的上像，可行权重构成单位性能切片的外向法向锥。针对铰链惩罚将该切片刻画为缩放KKT系统的投影多面体，用线性规划求带认证余量的内点权重；针对平方铰链惩罚证明当极限乘子非零时无有限权重精确，违约沿局部极小分支以O(1/w)衰减。在nuPlan闭环实验中用25条规则书进行校准与验证，并分析证书在采样时刻上的点态性。

Result: 在十一个可校准场景类中，九个类别的校准权重具有近似相等的层级分量；与匹配的启发式权重相比，在闭环nuPlan实验中合法层级事件精度约翻倍。但证书是点态的：单个权重无法在完整回合的所有采样时刻有效，中位寿命仅为一个采样间隔（原生速率下后续零个时刻），持续性取决于活动集稳定性。合规模式监视器仅能检测到一部分测量到的失效。

Abstract: Automated-driving rulebooks rank rule violations lexicographically, and model predictive control enforces that ranking either exactly, through $L{+}1$ sequential programs per tick, or approximately, through a weighted sum tuned by the separation heuristic $w_1\gg w_2\gg\cdots\gg w_L$. We show the heuristic answers the wrong question. For a convex priority-ordered program, a weighted sum reproduces the lexicographic optimum precisely when its weight, augmented by a unit performance coefficient, supports the upper image of the achievement map at the lexicographic point; the admissible weights form the unit-performance slice of an outward normal cone. Under hinge penalties this slice is a polyhedron obtained by projecting a scaled-KKT system, and a linear program returns an interior weight with a certified margin; under squared-hinge penalties no finite weight is exact whenever the limiting multiplier is nonzero, with violation along the local minimizer branch decaying as $O(1/w)$. Calibrated on held-out logs, the resulting weights have near-equal tier components in nine of eleven calibration-eligible scenario classes and roughly double legal-tier event precision against a matched heuristic weight in closed-loop nuPlan experiments on a 25-rule rulebook. The certificate is, however, pointwise: no single weight is valid across the sampled ticks of an episode, the median lifetime is one sampling interval (zero subsequent ticks at the native rate), and persistence tracks active-set stability. These findings motivate monitored weighted solves with selective cascade fallback, although the compliance-pattern monitor detects only a subset of measured lapses.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然储热在DHN中的益处已得到公认，但由于DHN在热源、生产与消费模式、网络热损失及燃料成本等方面的多样性，确定最优储热容量仍然充满挑战。现有方法缺乏可扩展性，且此前的物理建模储热定容研究规模较小。

Method: 提出一种基于数学优化的可扩展自动化方法：采用非线性、基于物理的建模方法，同时建模DHN、热生产者与储热器，以最小化总经济成本为目标进行储热容量优化，明确考虑随时间变化的热生产成本、热需求与热损失。

Result: 在比利时一个含30个用户的第三代CHP-DHN上验证（超越此前物理建模定容研究的规模）。最优储热集成使20年总成本从441万欧元降至368万欧元，节省73万欧元（16.5%），其中热生产成本降低107万欧元（通过在低电价时段转移热提取实现），储热投资为32.3万欧元。与常用的简化储热定容方法对比，简化方法无法识别最优储热容量。

Conclusion: 所提出的整体性、基于物理的优化方法在确保设计可行性、准确成本评估及最优储热容量确定方面优于常用的简化方法，为不同DHN的储热定容提供了可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Integrating heat storages in district heating networks (DHNs) supports managing dynamic characteristics such as heat-demand variations and changing energy prices, and the intermittency of renewable sources. A common application are DHNs with combined heat and power (CHP) units, where a storage allows shifting heat extraction to periods with favorable electricity prices. Although the benefits of heat storage in DHNs are well established, determining the optimal storage size remains challenging due to the diversity of DHNs in terms of heat sources, production and consumption patterns, network heat losses, and fuel costs. This paper presents a scalable, automated methodology for optimizing short-term heat storage in DHNs using mathematical optimization. The nonlinear, physics-based approach models the DHN, heat producers, and storages simultaneously to minimize total economic cost through optimal storage sizing, explicitly considering time-varying heat-production costs, heat demands, and heat losses. The methodology is demonstrated on a 3rd-generation CHP-DHN in Belgium with 30 consumers, exceeding the scale of previous physics-based storage-sizing studies. For this system, optimal storage integration reduces the 20-year cost by 730 k EUR (16.5%), from 4.41 M EUR to 3.68 M EUR. The reduction results from a 1.07 M EUR decrease in heat-production cost achieved by shifting heat extraction to periods of low electricity prices, while the storage investment amounts to 323 k EUR. A comparison to a commonly used simplified storage sizing method, which fails to identify the optimal storage size, demonstrates the advantage of the proposed holistic, physics-based optimization approach in ensuring feasible designs, accurate cost assessments, and optimal storage sizing.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 梯度-特征步算法不仅是一种高效的迭代方法，还能通过渐进采样策略在大规模样本平均定义的优化问题中实现更优的样本复杂度，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: For solving nonconvex equality-constrained optimization problems, a recent Gradient-Eigenstep Algorithm by Goyens et al.~is an iteration-efficient approach, based on minimizing Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian function, for finding an approximate second-order stationary point from an arbitrary starting point. In this paper, the analysis of this algorithm is extended, offering a two-fold contribution. First, it is shown that a local-linear rate of convergence can be obtained by this method if it is initiated sufficiently close to a strong second-order stationary point and employs a sufficiently small step-size parameter and sufficiently large penalty parameter. In this case, the algorithm reduces to a gradient descent algorithm applied to minimize Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian. Second, as a particularly useful application of the first result, it is shown that the Gradient-Eigenstep algorithm can be used as an iteration-efficient subproblem solver in the context of a progressive sampling strategy for solving equality-constrained optimization problems when the objective and constraint functions are defined by large sample averages, ultimately offering an algorithm with an improved worst-case sample complexity when compared to an approach that solves a full-sample problem directly.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 利用Low Carbon London项目5,566户家庭半年小时级数据（1.678亿条验证读数），基于准实验设计估计对照组下的价格响应，通过bootstrap构建需求情景，使用HiGHS求解随机混合整数规划，实现分段保护的随机电价设计；并在73个保留测试日上评估削峰、收入变动与账单影响，同时对比分段保护与代表性家庭（尾部感知）变体。

Result: 在全部73个测试日上，分段保护随机电价的平均削峰为2.29%（95% CI [2.11,2.48]），收入变动-2.26%，最差分段账单上涨2.48%。移除分段上限后削峰仅升至2.37%但最差分段账单上涨升至6.81%。家庭95分位账单上涨为8.61%（CVaR95为14.76%），表明分段平均上限不约束家庭尾部。代表性家庭变体将p95降至7.05%，削峰仅从2.29%微调至2.28%。

Abstract: Day-ahead residential tariffs must be posted before demand is observed. Aggressive high prices can cut peaks in simulation, but they can also raise customer bills. This paper measures how much peak reduction is lost when a tariff design problem is required to keep revenue near a flat-tariff baseline and to limit bill increases. Using half-hourly data from 5,566 Low Carbon London households (167.8 million validated readings), we estimate quasi-experimental price response against the standard-tariff comparison group, form bootstrap demand scenarios, and solve stochastic mixed-integer programs with HiGHS. On all 73 eligible held-out test days, a segment-protected stochastic tariff reduces simulated peak demand by 2.29% on average (95% day-bootstrap CI [2.11, 2.48]), with revenue change -2.26% and mean worst-segment bill increase 2.48%. Removing the segment bill cap raises peak reduction only to 2.37%, while the worst segment bill increase rises to 6.81%: in this sample, substantial average protection costs little peak-shaving performance. The same schedules leave a household 95th-percentile bill increase of 8.61% (CVaR95 14.76%), so segment-average caps do not bound household tails. We report the full price-of-protection frontier under consistent household simulation and compare segment protection with a representative-household (tail-aware) variant: the latter cuts household p95 from 8.61% to 7.05% while changing mean peak reduction only from 2.29% to 2.28%. All optimized outcomes are model-based counterfactuals under an opt-in trial; wholesale costs are unavailable, so we do not optimize profit.

Result: 通过数值算例验证了所提出优化算法的有效性，证明了该方法能够在无需数值积分的情况下生成权重矩阵并实现终端状态最小化。

Conclusion: 该优化算法有效克服了传统方法中数值积分的高计算成本，提供了一种系统化生成权重矩阵的途径，预期在科学和工程的控制理论应用中具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: The necessary conditions for formulating optimal feedback control algorithms have been known for many years. Free parameters exist in the performance index in the form of state and control penalty and terminal state penalty matrices for tuning the performance of the optimally controlled system. The selection process is typically experimental and iterative. To generate the weight matrices for optimal feedback control algorithms, an optimization process is proposed. Typically, the optimization process for the weight matrices requires several numerical integration processes that are computationally expensive; this work overcomes the classical high computational cost by exploiting closed-form solutions for the time-varying Riccati matrix, state trajectories, state transition matrix, and the optimal performance index. Closed-form algebraic equations are used to generate all partial derivative calculations, and no numerical integration is required. The closed-form partial derivatives are used to generate analytic gradients for the optimization steps. The optimization strategy seeks to minimize the terminal state values for the feedback control problem. A numerical example is presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed optimization algorithm. The resulting computational procedures are expected to be broadly useful for control theory applications in science and engineering.

</details>

*Yicong Jiang,Feihu Huang*

Motivation: 多目标双层优化在AI领域（如自动化学习、多任务元学习）有广泛应用，但现有方法依赖于下层问题的（强）凸性假设。实际中的多目标双层学习问题通常是非凸的，下层问题尤其如此。现有方法的理论基础与实际应用场景之间存在明显鸿沟，亟需针对非凸下层问题的多目标双层优化算法。

Method: 1) 利用Moreau包络将多目标双层优化问题转化为带包络约束的多目标单层优化问题；2) 引入光滑加权Tchebycheff标量化技术，使算法在多变体设定下保持单循环且无需计算Hessian矩阵，具有计算优势；3) 进一步提出基于动量的MB-MOMEHA变体以处理随机多目标双层学习问题。

Result: 在确定性和随机性两种设定下均证明了算法的收敛性。在少样本元学习（few-shot meta-learning）和神经架构搜索（neural architecture search）实验中，所提方法在Pareto前沿方面优于现有方法，验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MOMEHA及其随机变体MB-MOMEHA填补了非凸下层多目标双层优化研究的空白，通过Moreau包络与光滑Tchebycheff标化的结合，在保证理论收敛性的同时保持了计算效率，并在实际任务中展示了优越的性能。

Abstract: Multi-objective bilevel optimization has wide applications in the AI area such as automated learning and multi-task meta-learning. Although recently some works have been begun to study the multi-objective bilevel optimization, the proposed methods rely on the (strongly) convex lower level problems. In fact, these multi-objective bilevel learning problems are generally nonconvex, and particularly their lower level problems are nonconvex. To fill this gap, we propose a class of Multi-Objective Moreau Envelope based Hessian-free Algorithms (MOMEHA) to solve the multi-objective bilevel learning problems with nonconvex lower level. Specifically, our method uses the Moreau envelope to convert the original problem into a multi-objective single-level optimization with an envelope constraint. In particular, our method retains computational advantages of being single-loop and Hessian-free in the multi-objective setting by incorporating a smooth weighted Tchebycheff scalarization. Furthermore, we propose a momentum-based variant of MOMEHA (i.e., MB-MOMEHA) method to solve the stochastic multi-objective bilevel learning problems. In theory, we provide the convergence properties of our algorithms under both deterministic and stochastic setting. Some experiments on few-shot meta-learning and neural architecture search demonstrate that our methods outperform the existing approaches in Pareto front, validating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>

Conclusion: 本文通过形状设计逼近方法克服了退化性带来的困难，将控制区域触及退化集的情形纳入可处理的框架，扩展了退化双曲方程可控性理论的应用范围。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the observability and controllability of a class of degenerate hyperbolic equations with a control region intersecting the degenerate set. Unlike the existing results that mainly deal with control regions separated from the degeneracy, we consider the case where the control region reaches the degenerate part. To handle the difficulty caused by the degeneracy, we introduce a shape-design-based approximation method based on shape design by approximating the degenerate equation with a family of uniformly hyperbolic equations. The proof relies on the spectral approximation of the associated operators, precise estimates for weak solutions, and the multiplier method. We first establish observability inequalities for the approximating equations with constants independent of the approximation parameter. Then, by passing to the degenerate limit, we obtain the observability inequality for the original degenerate equation.

</details>

Method: 在给定图拉普拉斯的设定下，提出DCR图学习框架：1) 通过正则化MLE近似拉普拉斯伪逆的谱作用；2) 通过双重表示将对LR-NNLS重写，将伪逆学习与实例推理解耦；3) 采用可微的对偶引导学习方案实现高效原始解重构；4) 建立算法的稳定性及唯一不动点理论保证。

Result: 数值实验表明，DCR方法在性能上优于凸求解器和图滤波基线，并在多种图拓扑下展现出稳健的性能表现。

Conclusion: DCR框架有效解决了拉普拉斯伪逆稠密且病态带来的计算瓶颈，在不直接求逆的情况下实现伪逆谱作用的高效逼近与学习，兼具理论保证和实际性能优势。

Abstract: Laplacian-regularized minimization is fundamental in signal processing and machine learning, but is limited by the dense and ill-conditioned nature of the graph Laplacian pseudoinverse. While the Laplacian itself is sparse, its pseudoinverse is dense and often ill-conditioned, rendering direct computation impractical at scale. Moreover, pseudoinverse learning is more challenging than Laplacian learning. To address this challenge, this paper considers the setting where the graph Laplacian is given and proposes a Difference-of-Convex Regularizer (DCR) graph learning framework that approximates the spectral action of the Laplacian pseudoinverse without direct inversion via regularized Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE). By reformulating Laplacian-Regularized Nonnegative Least Squares (LR-NNLS) through a dual representation, DCR decouples pseudoinverse learning from instance-specific inference and enables efficient primal solution reconstruction via a differentiable dual-guided learning scheme. We establish theoretical guarantees on stability and the existence of a unique fixed point for DCR algorithm. Numerical experiments demonstrate improved performance over convex solvers and graph filtering baselines and robust performance across diverse graph topologies.

</details>


### [73] [Adaptive Schauder Stochastic Mirror Descent in Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13121)
*Jinhui Bai,Shuai Lu,Lei Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在无限维Banach空间上扩展随机镜像下降（SMD）的方法，用于解决一类仅通过抽样获得随机梯度信息的风险泛函最小化问题，实现了高效的正则化策略和最优收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将随机镜像下降方法扩展到无限维Banach空间以解决风险泛函最小化问题，此前在无限维空间中该方法的理论和算法实现面临挑战，需要根据空间结构设计合适的Bregman距离和有限维子空间近似方案。

Method: 1) 根据Banach空间的均匀凸性选择合适的Bregman距离，对非均匀凸空间L^1_{ρ_X}(Ω)构造基于熵函数的Bregman距离；2) 基于Schauder基引入适应样本量n的有限维子空间族；3) 每步迭代将子问题限制在对应有限维子空间，并将随机梯度投影到对偶空间，形成新的正则化策略；4) 算法获得O(n^{1+θ})时间和O(n^θ)内存复杂度。

Result: 算法达到O(n^{-1/p_1})的收敛率（对数因子以内），其中p_1≥2由底层空间的凸性决定；在次正则（misspecified）设置下仍能收敛到最小值；算法扩展到逆问题并通过数值实验验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 通过在无限维Banach空间中引入基于Schauder基的自适应有限维子空间正则化策略，成功实现了SMD在无限维风险函数最小化中的高效求解和理论保证，并展示了在逆问题中的扩展应用。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend stochastic mirror descent (SMD) to infinite-dimensional Banach spaces for solving a class of risk functional minimization problems, where stochastic gradient information is only available through sampling. We first choose the Bregman distance according to the uniform convexity properties of the Banach space. For the non-uniformly convex space $\mathcal{L}_{ρ_X}^1(Ω)$, we instead construct a Bregman distance induced by the entropy function. Based on a Schauder basis of the Banach space, we introduce a family of finite-dimensional subspaces that adapt to the sample size $n$. At each SMD iteration, we restrict the subproblem to the corresponding finite-dimensional subspace and project the stochastic gradient onto the associated finite-dimensional dual space, thereby introducing a new regularization strategy. This regularization strategy allows us to explicitly solve the SMD subproblem efficiently and to achieve a bias--variance trade-off. The algorithm requires $\mathcal{O}(n^{1+θ})$ time and $\mathcal{O}(n^θ)$ memory, where $θ>0$ can be chosen arbitrarily small when the minimizer has sufficient regularity. By developing a new analytical framework, we prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-1/p_1}\right)$ up to logarithmic factors, where $p_1\geq2$ is determined by the convexity properties of the underlying space. In the misspecified setting where the minimizer satisfies only weaker regularity conditions, we show that the proposed algorithm still converges to the minimum. We further extend the algorithm to inverse problems and validate its effectiveness in numerical experiments.

</details>

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the introduction and analysis
  of a penalty-type method for the numerical treatment of
  a class of bilevel optimization problems arising from inverse optimal control.
  The algorithm is designed to compute stationary points of the associated relaxed value function reformulation.
  This is achieved by determining a sequence of stationary points associated with a sequence of surrogate problems
  where the relaxed value function constraint is penalized,
  where the updates of upper- and lower-level decision variables are decoupled,
  and where the penalty parameter is enlarged only in those iterations
  which do not come along with a sufficient improvement of some feasibility measure.
  The resulting method does not comprise any linesearch, the lower-level problem has to be evaluated just once per iteration,
  and the penalty parameter does not need to be driven to infinity.
  Nevertheless, subsequential convergence results are obtained under reasonable assumptions.
  Numerical experiments, where the relaxation parameter is also driven to zero, visualize effectiveness of the approach.

</details>

We refer to this collection of systems as a discrete linear ensemble system. The ensemble system is feedback stabilizable if there exists a common feedback control input that asymptotically stabilizes every system simultaneously. Unlike finite-dimensional linear systems, an infinite-dimensional linear system is not guaranteed to be stable if its poles lie in the open left half-plane. Stability is guaranteed, however, if (i) its poles are contained in the closed left half-plane and (ii) its infinitesimal generator is diagonalizable. In this paper, we provide necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a static, linear feedback control law that ensures the closed-loop ensemble system satisfies (i) and (ii). Abstract: In this paper, we prove the local null controllability of the Boussinesq system in dimensions two and three with a reduced number of localized controls. More precisely, the controls act on the temperature equation and on only $N-2$ components of the velocity equation. Our proof is based on a spectral approach in the spirit of the Lebeau--Robbiano method.

Their natural spectral decompositions are different, and there is no common spectral localization naturally adapted to the coupled system. Therefore, the usual componentwise spectral argument cannot be applied directly. We show that the cascade structure of the linearized system makes it possible to overcome this difficulty. The main ingredient is a mixed observability estimate in which only the Stokes component is spectrally localized, while the heat component is treated without any frequency restriction. Combining this estimate with the dissipation of the high Stokes frequencies allows us to carry out a Lebeau--Robbiano iteration for the linearized system. The resulting linear controllability estimate is transferred to the nonlinear Boussinesq system through a time-iteration argument. As an important consequence, the controls have a small-time cost bounded by $C\exp(C/T)$, which recovers the expected parabolic order while preserving the reduced number of controls.

</details>

Abstract: Distribution steering seeks feedback laws that drive the state law of a dynamical system between prescribed initial and terminal distributions. Optimal transport provides a natural geometric approach, but its implementation generally requires a transport map or coupling in the full state space. Sliced optimal transport avoids this full-dimensional construction through one-dimensional projections. Yet, the resulting projected maps specify only directional displacements and do not by themselves prescribe a realizable feedback law. Averaging over projection directions gives a deterministic sliced feedback. For the single-integrator dynamics, the averaged feedback makes the sliced Wasserstein distance to the target non-increasing. For Gaussian endpoint laws, it is affine, preserves Gaussianity, and steers the mean and covariance to their prescribed terminal values. We further identify a law-dependent gain that yields linear decay of the sliced Wasserstein distance together with an explicit characterization of the control energy. We also prove that the randomized controller converges to the averaged sliced flow as the sampling period vanishes. Finally, we extend the construction to linear dynamical systems. Reachability-normalized coordinates allow instantaneous realization of the sliced velocity for uniformly fully actuated systems, while local controllability Gramians provide exact finite-step realization for general controllable systems.
