Abstract: Scheduling follow-up Computed Tomography (CT) examinations requires balancing two competing objectives: assigning patients as close as possible to their recommended examination dates while ensuring an equitable distribution of radiologists' workload. Existing approaches optimize scanner utilization or patient waiting times, overlooking reporting activities and the need to balance fairness across multiple stakeholders. This paper proposes a predictive-prescriptive framework for fairness-aware follow-up CT scheduling. Patient-specific Machine Learning (ML) models are first developed to predict both examination and reporting durations. These predictions are then embedded into a multi-objective Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model that simultaneously minimizes deviations from patients' preferred examination dates and balances radiologists' reporting workloads through a lexicographic min-max fairness criterion. We derive a dominance reduction property within an $ε$-constraint framework that substantially reduces the number of optimization problems required to generate the Pareto frontier. Computational experiments based on data from a real-world emergency radiology department show that allowing patients a scheduling flexibility of only one to two days is sufficient to substantially improve workload equity among radiologists while preserving timely access to follow-up examinations. The proposed dominance reduction strategy eliminates most $ε$-constraint evaluations without affecting the Pareto frontier. Finally, evaluating predictive models through downstream optimization regret demonstrates that XGBoost provides the most effective support for scheduling decisions, outperforming models that achieve lower prediction errors according to conventional predictive metrics.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黎曼SQP方法仅在约束规范成立时保证全局收敛性，但对于退化问题（即约束规范可能失效的经典RNLP问题），其收敛行为无法保证。需要扩展稳定化SQP框架以处理退化问题。

Method: 将欧几里得空间中的稳定化SQP框架推广到黎曼流形上，用于处理带等式和不等式约束的黎曼非线性规划问题（RNLP）。在不假设任何约束规范的前提下，分析生成序列的收敛性质。

Result: 证明了生成的序列存在一个聚点，该聚点要么是KKT点，要么是AKKT点，要么是相关可行性问题的驻点。数值实验确认了该方法对退化问题的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的稳定化SQP方法能够在不依赖约束规范的情况下保证退化黎曼约束优化问题的收敛性，填补了现有黎曼SQP方法在处理退化问题上的空白。

Abstract: We propose a stabilized sequential quadratic programming (SQP) method for degenerate constrained optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds. The problem considered in this study is a Riemannian nonlinear programming problem (RNLP) with equality and inequality constraints, where classical constraint qualifications may fail. While existing Riemannian SQP methods guarantee global convergence only under constraint qualifications, their convergence behavior is not ensured for degenerate problems. To address this limitation, we extend the stabilized SQP framework from Euclidean spaces to Riemannian manifolds. Without assuming any constraint qualification, we prove that the generated sequence has an accumulation point that is a Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) point, an approximate KKT (AKKT) point, or a stationary point of an associated feasibility problem. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to confirm the effectiveness of the proposed method for degenerate problems.

</details>


### [83] [Distributed and Dynamic Hub Network Operation Planning in a Hyperconnected Less-Than-Truckload Operating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13419)
*Tiankuo Zhang,Jihye Jung,Paria Nourmohammadi,Benoit Montreuil,Alan Erera,Sahrish Jaleel Shaikh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对零担运输（LTL）行业中平均成本偏高、网络碎片化的问题，基于物理互联网（PI）原则提出了一套分布式多枢纽运营规划协议，支持多个区域枢纽网络之间的协同合作，通过智能规划枢纽内与枢纽间的货流分配和拖车调度，提升物流效率与可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零担运输行业具有比整车运输更强的整合潜力，但由于承运商规模小、网络碎片化，运营效率低且平均成本高。现有研究缺乏对多枢纽网络间协同运营的系统性支持，需要建立分布式、动态的物流枢纽网络系统以改善这一现状。

Method: 基于物理互联网（PI）的模块化集装箱和开放资源共享原则，设计了一套多枢纽运营规划协议。该协议由分布式枢纽运营商分别用于：（1）规划枢纽内货物向目的地特定拖车的分配；（2）规划枢纽间拖车的发运调度。通过网络间精心设计的互联机制，使整体枢纽网络系统实现协同效应并满足运输需求。通过模拟实验在运营商多枢纽网络中多个场景下评估协议有效性。

Result: 通过基于多个场景的仿真实验，验证了所提出协议在多枢纽运营环境中的有效性，表明该协议能够帮助聚合式枢纽网络系统实现合作结果并满足货运请求，提高了PI网络的实用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究提升了基于物理互联网的枢纽网络系统的实用性与鲁棒性，支持枢纽网络之间更大程度的合作，有助于构建更高效、更可持续的物流系统。

Abstract: The less-than-truckload (LTL) industry plays a vital role in enhancing the efficiency and sustainability of logistics systems, as LTL shipments offer greater consolidation opportunities than full-truckload shipments. Despite of this flexibility, the average cost of LTL shipments remains considerably higher due to less efficient operations and highly fragmented networks of small and medium-sized carriers. Building on our ongoing effort to develop a distributed and dynamic logistics hub network system grounded in the Physical Internet (PI) principles of modular containers and open resource sharing, this study focuses specifically on inter-hub and in-hub operations, with cooperation among multiple regional hub networks. Therefore, a shipment may traverse multiple cooperating hub networks. With respect to each hub network each shipment enters, it is defined by its expected arrival time at the entry hub and its latest arrival time at the exit hub. Based on the defined shipment information, we design a set of multi-hub operation planning protocols for distributed hub operators. In their operating networks, operators use our smartly designed protocol separately to plan in-hub shipments' assignments to destination-specific trailers and inter-hub trailers' dispatch schedules. With carefully designed interconnections between hub networks, the aggregated hub network system is well-positioned to achieve cooperative outcomes and fulfill shipment requests. We evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed protocol through a simulation-based experiment under multiple scenarios in an operator's multi-hub network. Overall, this research improves the practicality and robustness of PI-based networks and supports greater cooperation among hub networks toward more efficient and sustainable logistics systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [84] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LoKiFormer，一种新型LLM架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决自注意力缺乏局部归纳偏置和MoE耦合知识存储与计算路径的问题，预训练收敛速度比基线快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练中存在两大效率瓶颈：(i) 标准自注意力缺乏对序列内部局部信息的显式归纳偏置，导致冗余建模；(ii) 混合专家模型（MoE）将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。

Method: 在标准解码器上增加两个专用模块：1) 局部融合注意力（LFA），将卷积融合引入注意力机制，显式捕获局部模式，使注意力作用于更具表征力的输入；2) 知识记忆模块（KMM），引入参数化的键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址的槽位中，实现存储与计算的解耦，支持直接知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明LoKiFormer在预训练阶段的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，验证了该架构在信息融合效率与效果上的优越性。

Conclusion: LoKiFormer通过显式解耦局部信息建模（LFA）与全局知识存储/检索（KMM），有效解决了传统LLM架构在预训练中的两大瓶颈，实现了更高效的信息集成，性能优于现有LLM架构。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [85] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量与卫星气象数据），针对尼泊尔喜马拉雅地区的冰湖溃决洪水、降雨型滑坡和冰川池塘小洪水三类灾害，构建了预测模型。结果显示，先兆天气对触发时机的预测（ROC 0.73-0.83）优于地形对易发性的排序（0.54-0.76），深度学习方法未能显著超越梯度提升基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期可行性研究表明，卫星雷达干涉测量可识别冰碛坝的变形迹象（指示哪个湖在失稳），卫星气象数据可标记湖泊受压的时期（指示何时存在风险），但缺少一个真正可用的预测模型。本文旨在填补这一空白，利用免费数据构建并评估预测模型，回答"哪个场地易受影响"与"何时会触发"两个问题。

Method: 使用三个数据源：589条HMAGLOFDB冰湖溃决记录、数千条编目滑坡、同类但未失稳的场地作为对照。每个灾害对应两个独立问题（易感性和触发时机），不做混合建模。所有模型均在空间交叉验证下进行，通过保留整张地图瓦片防止模型因识别训练过的邻域而虚高。比较天气时序特征（触发时机）与地形特征（易感性）的性能，并使用五种深度学习模型与梯度提升基线对比。

Result: 先兆天气对触发时机预测：大冰湖溃决ROC 0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82。地形对易感性排序：天真评分接近0.9，但匹配可比邻域后诚实值为0.76（大洪水）、0.71（滑坡）、0.54（小洪水，等于随机）。尼泊尔单一区域的大洪水信号达0.89。五种深度学习方法未能决定性胜过梯度提升基线，仅在滑坡上略高但不足以确认。冰湖灾害上基线完胜，可由三规则决策树（崎岖度+季风降雨）复现。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据在触发时机预测上表现可靠，但地形易感性排序的作用有限，尤其是小洪水场景几乎无预测力。深度学习未带来明显增益，简单基线模型更具实用性。最终给出尼泊尔排名监测清单作为优先级辅助工具（非预测），并指出免费数据的能力边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [86] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MARCH是一种网络架构，通过周期性缓存循环状态检查点作为"状态锚点"，并利用内容条件化的锚点键实现注意力式聚合，从而在保持计算效率的同时将状态空间模型扩展到超出固定维度的记忆容量，在常识推理、LongBench和上下文检索任务上优于多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在长上下文检索中表现优异得益于token级记忆，但训练时O(n²)复杂度和推理时线性增长的KV缓存代价高昂；循环替代方案虽然推理高效，但固定大小的状态压缩导致早期信息被覆盖，在召回密集型任务上表现不佳。需要一种既能保持循环模型高效解码、又能克服固定状态容量限制的架构。

Method: MARCH周期性缓存累积的循环状态检查点作为状态锚点（state anchors），并为每个锚点配备紧凑的、内容条件化的锚点键（anchor key）。在每个token处，MARCH生成一个锚点查询（anchor query）来注意所有因果可用的状态锚点，输出通过注意力式聚合计算所有历史锚点对当前状态的贡献。记忆库可随上下文长度增长，在历史分辨率与记忆成本之间提供可控权衡。

Result: 经过标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench基准和上下文检索任务上持续优于多种线性注意力变体，证明了内容路由的状态缓存能显著增强循环模型的长距离记忆能力，同时保留其固有的计算路径。

Conclusion: 内容路由的状态缓存机制是增强循环模型长距离记忆的有效方法，通过将固定大小的状态空间扩展为可增长的内存银行，MARCH在保持循环模型高效解码优势的同时弥合了与Transformer在召回密集型任务上的性能差距。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [87] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VGG-MADiffRL（值梯度引导的多智能体扩散强化学习算法）与MDCA（基于扩散的分层控制架构），用于多AUV自组网下的机动目标协同跟踪，在受限声学通信、动态拓扑和海洋扰动条件下，通过值梯度引导扩散策略的逆去噪生成过程以及孪生价值网络联合优化，实现更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体强化学习方法在高维联合状态-动作建模和噪声敏感的策略生成方面存在缺陷，导致训练不稳定和跟踪性能退化。在多AUV自组网协同跟踪场景下，受限声学通信、动态拓扑和不确定海洋扰动的复杂约束进一步加剧了这些问题，亟需一种更稳健的分布式协调方法。

Method: 1) 将多AUV自组网协同跟踪建模为MDP，显式建模声纳探测机制和洋流扰动；2) 提出MDCA三层闭环控制架构（全局智能控制层、局部在线训练层、物理动作执行层），实现任务分配、局部决策与执行反馈的协同优化；3) 提出VGG-MADiffRL：基于扩散策略，在逆去噪过程中引入值梯度引导动作生成朝向更高期望回报，并采用孪生价值网络联合优化与软目标更新来缓解过估计和训练振荡。

Result: 实验结果表明，VGG-MADiffRL在协同跟踪场景中持续实现更快的收敛速度、更高的跟踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其有效性和在动态水下环境中的实际工程价值。

Conclusion: VGG-MADiffRL与MDCA架构为多AUV自组网协同跟踪提供了稳定且高效的方法，能够有效应对水下声学通信受限、动态拓扑和海洋扰动带来的挑战，具有实际工程应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [88] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将生成式建模表述为一个路径积分，其中基于流、扩散、变分和对抗的模型均统一为同一主作用量的不同评估原则。利用Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de-Dominicis（MSRJD）形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，从而引入图论微扰论。该展开可在无随机采样成本的情况下给出确定性采样器的一圈修正，在可解与非线性漂移上可将53%的树级误差降至1.6%。不完美的学习分数作为插入项产生响应加权的分数匹配目标，而对称等变漂移设计成为具有EFT幂次计数的算子展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建立一个统一的理论框架，将流模型、扩散模型、变分自编码器和生成对抗网络等看似不同的生成式建模方法纳入同一数学形式体系中，并借助场论工具（路径积分与微扰论）来理解、改进和扩展这些方法，特别是提升确定性采样器的精度。

Method: 将生成式建模表述为路径积分形式，通过MSRJD形式化方法区分自由与相互作用的概率流，并对作用量进行图论微扰展开。该框架支持一圈修正、不完美分数作为插入项的处理，以及对称等变漂移的算子化设计。

Result: 在可解和非线性漂移的实验中，一圈修正将确定性采样器的树级误差从53%降低到1.6%，且不增加随机采样的计算成本；同时导出了响应加权的分数匹配目标，并建立对称等变漂移设计的EFT幂次计数体系。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个统一的路径积分视角来涵盖多种生成式建模范式，通过微扰论显著提升了确定性采样精度，并为分数匹配目标设计与对称性引入提供了系统的理论基础，预示着生成模型理论化的新方向。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [89] [Structure-preserving uncertainty quantification for GENERIC dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12624)
*Zequn He,Celia Reina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出结构保持认知神经网络（S-PENNs），一种用于具有硬架构约束的科学机器学习模型的不确定性量化通用框架。通过在预训练模型的约束组件上附加轻量级epinets，确保每个采样实现都保持物理可行性，并结合分裂共形预测提供有限样本边际覆盖保证。在GENERIC动力学上的三个数值实验中，S-PENNs在保持热力学一致性的同时，比深度集成降低了1-3个数量级的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构保持机器学习将物理结构直接嵌入模型架构，但此类硬约束模型的不确定性量化仍然十分有限。标准UQ方法可能违反编码的可允许性条件（如热力学定律）、需要修改架构或带来巨大的计算开销，这限制了在科学计算中对物理约束模型进行可靠的不确定性估计。

Method: S-PENNs框架的核心思想是在预训练模型的约束组件上附加轻量级epinets（认知网络），通过神经网络集成方式估计认知不确定性，同时确保模型输出的每个采样实现都满足物理约束。该方法将S-PENNs应用于GENERIC动力学，生成满足热力学第一、第二定律的随机轨迹，并与分裂共形预测结合作为事后校准方法，产生具有有限样本边际覆盖保证的预测区间。

Result: 在三个数值实验（谐波振荡器耦合热浴、理想化学马达的ODE模型，以及一维粘塑性模型的PDE模型）中，S-PENNs在所有示例上均产生热力学一致（满足第一、第二定律）的随机实现和良好校准的预测区间，同时相比深度集成方法将计算成本降低了约1至3个数量级。

Conclusion: S-PENNs为硬约束科学机器学习模型提供了一种高效、通用且物理一致的不确定性量化框架。虽然本文聚焦于GENERIC动力学，但该方法可广泛扩展到计算力学中具有硬或软约束的科学机器学习模型。

Abstract: Structure-preserving machine learning embeds physical structure directly into model architectures, yet uncertainty quantification (UQ) for such hard-constrained models remains limited because standard UQ methods may violate the encoded admissibility conditions, require architectural modifications, or impose substantial computational costs. In this work, we propose Structure-Preserving Epistemic Neural Networks (S-PENNs), a general framework for UQ in scientific machine learning models with hard architectural constraints, and instantiate it for GENERIC (General Equation for Non-Equilibrium Reversible-Irreversible Coupling) dynamics. S-PENNs preserve the structural constraints of a pretrained model by attaching lightweight epinets to its constrained components, ensuring that every sampled realization remains physically admissible by construction. When applied to GENERIC dynamics, such a proposed framework yields thermodynamically consistent rollouts that preserve the first and second laws. Furthermore, we combine S-PENNs with split conformal prediction as a post-hoc calibration method to produce prediction intervals with finite-sample marginal coverage guarantees. We validate S-PENNs on three numerical examples: a harmonic oscillator coupled to a heat bath and an idealized chemical motor, both governed by ODEs, and a one-dimensional viscoplastic model governed by PDEs. Across all three examples, S-PENNs produce thermodynamically consistent stochastic realizations and well-calibrated prediction intervals while reducing the computational cost by about one to three orders of magnitude compared to deep ensembles. Although the present study focuses on GENERIC dynamics, S-PENNs can be extended more broadly to scientific machine learning models in computational mechanics with either hard or soft constraints.

</details>


### [90] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了X-AddGraph，一个针对动态图边级异常检测模型AddGraph的纯事后（post-hoc）可解释性框架。它基于双时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个与AddGraph架构模块对应的组件：基于梯度的空间相关性归因、直接读取推理时已计算的上下文注意力权重（零额外成本的短期时间归因）、以及通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚（长期时间归因）。由于检测器保持冻结，检测性能完全不变（Delta AUC = 0），同时为原本完全不可解释的AddGraph提供了完整的解释能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图上的深度学习异常检测器虽然准确率高，但完全不透明：当一条边被标记为异常时，分析者只能得到一个分数而没有任何解释说明。在协作式、受监管的信息系统中，这种不透明性是不可接受的，因为自动化决策必须可审计且可信。AddGraph作为动态图边级异常检测的基础性GCN+GRU框架，迄今从未配备任何形式的可解释性机制，这正是本文要解决的关键空白。

Method: 提出X-AddGraph，采用双时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个组件：(1) 基于梯度的相关性归因，作用于当前邻接结构（空间维度）；(2) 直接读取推理时已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时间维度，零额外成本）；(3) 通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚（长期时间维度）。整个框架是严格事后（post-hoc）的，检测器保持冻结状态，因此不改变模型本身。

Result: 在UCI Message基准上，训练的AddGraph基线平均每快照AUC为0.8705，超过原作者发表的结果；X-AddGraph精确复现每个分数（Delta AUC = 0，经验验证到小数点后十位）。在四类边群体（高置信真阳性、低置信真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）上评估，长期归因能够识别出比随机选择携带显著更多反事实信号的历史快照（0.127 vs 0.074），这是空间盲解释器所无法提供的能力。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph成功为AddGraph这一基础性动态图异常检测框架提供了首套可解释性机制，在不损失任何检测性能的前提下生成了空间和时间两个维度的归因解释。长期归因对历史快照的反事实信号识别能力具有独特价值，且代码已开源以支持可复现性。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [91] [Neural Quadratic Forms: A Unified Minimal Model for Sudden Learning and Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13335)
*Liu Ziyin,Yizhou Xu,Tomaso Poggio,Isaac Chuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，即使网络在光滑代价函数上由梯度下降训练，学习仍可能呈现阶梯式（平台期+突降），而训练损失则遵循光滑幂律。作者证明对称性（单元重标号不变性）强制确定了相关集体变量：在一系列架构中，近零权重附近的扩张统一为二次形式 Tr[WW^T A(x)]，所有架构细节浓缩进单一“结构矩阵”A(x)。训练动力学在序参量 M=WW^T 上闭合，在数据矩阵共享特征基时可约化为 Lotka–Volterra 方程，模式逐个开启；初值越小，各模式开启时间间隔越大，平台期是光滑流的奇异极限，而众多模式未分辨时则合并成训练时间上的幂律。理论在多种训练方法和架构上得到数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解为什么不同微观结构的神经网络在训练中都会出现类似的阶段性学习行为（平台期与突降），以及训练损失呈现光滑幂律两种看似矛盾的现象。作者试图寻找一个统一的、由对称性决定的普适解释框架。

Method: 首先利用网络层关于可互换单元重标号的不变性（对称性），结合光滑性和单元梯度在原点处消失的条件，推导出近零权重附近展开的普适二次形式 Tr[WW^T A(x)]，将所有架构差异归结为单一的结构矩阵 A(x)。随后证明训练动力学在序参量 M=WW^T 上闭合，并在数据矩阵共享特征基时约化为 Lotka–Volterra 方程，从而得到模式逐个开启的动力学。理论还给出了奇异极限下平台期出现以及未分辨模式合并为幂律的预测。

Result: 作者证明感知机、注意力层、专家混合和卷积等架构在该框架下只是结构矩阵 A(x) 不同的同一个模型。训练动力学由 Lotka–Volterra 方程描述，模式随训练逐个开启；初始权重越小，开启时间间隔越大，平台期成为光滑流的奇异极限。当大量模式未分辨时，事件合并为训练时间上的幂律，且幂指数可由理论预测。数值实验在多种训练方法和架构上验证了这些结论。

Conclusion: 对称性决定了神经网络训练中少数量相关集体变量，从而统一解释了阶梯式学习与光滑幂律两种看似矛盾的行为。这一理论将不同架构统一为一个模型，并提供了对训练动力学（模式切换、平台期、幂律指数）的可预测框架。

Abstract: Neural networks trained by gradient descent on a smooth cost function can nevertheless learn in steps: the cost holds on long plateaus and then drops abruptly. Meanwhile, training losses instead follow smooth power laws. Variants of both behaviors occur in architectures with very different microscopic structures, which is the signature of a few relevant collective variables. We show that a symmetry fixes what those variables are: a network layer is a sum over interchangeable units, so relabeling the units leaves it unchanged; given smoothness and the condition that a unit's gradient vanish at the origin, symmetry then enforces a universal leading form for the expansion about the near-zero weights present at the start of training, the quadratic $\Tr[WW^{\top}A(x)]$, in which every architectural detail is confined to a single ``structure matrix" $A(x)$ that we compute for each architecture. Perceptrons, attention layers, mixtures of experts, and convolutions become one model at different $A$. Its training dynamics then close on the ``order parameter" $M=WW^{\top}$ and, whenever the data matrices share an eigenbasis, reduce to a Lotka--Volterra equation whose modes switch on one after another. The smaller the initial weights, the further apart the switch-on times, and the plateaus appear as a singular limit of a smooth flow; when many modes are unresolved the same events merge into a power law in training time whose exponent the theory predicts. We confirm both numerically across training methods and architectures.

</details>


### [92] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的睡眠分期金标准构建方法，利用多标注者数据的集体行为生成更可靠的分期参考标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动睡眠分期研究以单一参考分期图为基准评估模型，但已知不同评分者之间存在标注变异性（inter-scorer variability），单一参考可能不够可靠。

Method: 利用DOD-H和DOD-O公开数据集，提取EEG (C3-M2)和chin EMG各30个特征共60维。提出学习型睡眠分期图（LBH），通过机器学习模型构建每个评分者的混淆矩阵，经列归一化后估计真实睡眠分期条件下的各评分者标记概率，再跨评分者聚合概率生成最终分期标签。使用随机森林、支持向量机和多层感知机在EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下评估。

Result: LBH在多类分类器配置下均优于数据集分期图（DH）和最佳评分者分期图（BSH）。最佳结果来自随机森林结合EEG+EMG：DOD-H上准确率86.07%、精确率85.46%、F1 85.29%；DOD-O上准确率86.04%、精确率85.21%、F1 84.70%。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模能够在不丢弃单个专家信息的情况下改进参考分期图的构建，为睡眠分期评估提供更可靠的基准。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [93] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现训练后的Transformer模型会发展出"特权基"——预测方向（模型当前预测token的unembedding方向）作为一个内容定义的特权锚点。以该锚点为参照，残差流中的变异在几何和行为上均按与预测的接近度分层。这一分层在18个模型中普遍成立（涵盖7B-120B、稠密/MoE、基座/指令微调）。预测近端区域高度结构化并聚类相关提示，而远端补集平坦且对提示组反判别。预测方向几乎正交于主方差轴，因此基于方差的分析只能部分恢复此组织。因果干扰实验表明预测接口是狭窄但功能决定性的切片，补集虽每方向弱对齐但因果和时序上承重，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。这些结果确立了预测方向作为不同于先前描述的坐标轴的特权锚点，并为高维计算与线性读出共存提供了几何解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer模型内部表示的关键问题是：模型发展出的特权基（privileged basis）选择的是什么方向？之前的方差分析无法完全捕获残差流中的组织结构，作者希望找到一种更根本的几何锚点来揭示残差流的分层结构，并解释高维计算如何与线性读出（linear readout）共存。

Method: 作者以预测方向（即当前预测token的unembedding方向）作为锚点，度量残差流中每个方向与该锚点的接近度，从而对残差流进行几何分层。他们进一步通过因果干预实验（干扰距预测方向不同距离的方差方向）来验证各层在行为和时序上的功能差异。实验覆盖18个模型（稠密和MoE、7B-120B、基座和指令微调），并使用提示多样性作为变量来测试方差分析的恢复程度。

Result: 三种主要发现：（1）预测方向在所有测试模型中都作为内容定义的特权锚点，残差流按与预测的接近度形成几何梯度；（2）预测近端区域高度结构化并聚类相关提示，补集较平坦且反判别提示组；预测接口狭窄但功能决定性——破坏接近预测的方差方向导致立即分歧和任务框转换，而破坏次近层仅延迟分歧；（3）补集虽每方向弱读出对齐，但在因果和时序上是承重的，且行为由方向而非幅度驱动。预测方向与主方差轴近乎正交，导致基于方差的分析随提示异质性增大而损失更多信息。

Conclusion: 预测方向构成了一个区别于先前描述坐标轴的真正特权锚点。该锚点揭示了残差流中"狭窄但决定性"的预测接口与"广阔但承重"的远端补集之间的精细分工，为Transformer中高维计算与线性读出共存的机制提供了几何层面的一致性解释。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [94] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习的客观分类方法，通过对大量阵风引起的压力载荷测量数据进行机器学习表征和摘要化处理，选择最小的高显著性样本作为“范例”，从而建立基于相似性的客观分类准则。在3480组跨越六种飞行姿态的飞翼模型随机阵风压力载荷数据上，识别出九种跨姿态重复出现的基本响应类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，阵风引起的载荷分类依赖于基于粗糙参数（如飞行姿态）的标签，这种方式不够客观且难以解释复杂的多飞行条件下阵风载荷的多样性。作者希望找到一种客观的分类准则，既能够组织多种飞行条件下阵风载荷的复杂性，又能保持与传统粗参数标签相当的的可解释性。

Method: 该方法包括两个步骤：(1) 通过机器学习表征（machine-learned representation）对大量实验观测数据进行编码；(2) 应用摘要化程序（summarization procedure）选择最小子集的极高显著性范例（exemplars）。这些范例为所有观测提供基于相似性的客观分类准则，便于专家检查并可作为进一步精细化实验的对象。

Result: 在包含3480组随机阵风压力载荷测量数据（覆盖六种飞行姿态的飞翼模型）的数据库上，该方法识别出九种跨多个姿态重复出现的基本响应类型。对每种响应类型的瞬态响应分析能够为底层流体力学提供物理直觉。

Conclusion: 通过机器学习表征与范例摘要化相结合的方法，可以建立既有客观性又保留可解释性的阵风载荷分类准则。识别出的九种基本响应类型可在不同飞行姿态间通用，为理解阵风-结构相互作用的流体力学机制提供了新的视角和工具。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [95] [When Can You Trust Offline Evaluation of Equal-Cost Top-k Allocation? A Controlled, Reproducible Benchmark and Practitioner's Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12489)
*Binshuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对预算约束下组织从日志数据中评估确定性top-k目标分配策略的价值问题，系统对比了六种离线策略评估（OPE）估计器在五个数据集和两组已知效应扫描上的表现，并用非仿真配对参考验证了其机制。研究发现三个关键问题：弱重叠主要由日志记录器与目标行动的对齐程度而非记录的锐度决定；优化器诅咒无法仅通过交叉拟合结果扰动项来消除，需要诚实的策略级划分；倾向性估计误差是最大的性能退化来源，其对IPS的损害超过其他所有压力测试。研究还发现权重下限（0.02）使两个混合估计器退化为各自的未调优基础版本，最终只有四种实际不同的估计器，所有精确值表面均为合成或半合成数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在预算约束下需要决定对谁进行干预，且希望在部署目标分配规则前就知道该规则能带来多少收益。离线策略评估（OPE）可以从历史日志数据中实现这一预测，但可部署的规则是确定性的top-k策略——它消除了对行动的所有平均化处理，导致弱重叠问题直接冲击估计结果。现有的OPE方法在无重叠/弱重叠场景下的表现缺乏系统性基准评估。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含六种估计器、五个数据集、两组已知效应扫描的全面基准测试框架，并设计了一个非仿真的配对参考来验证各种机制。实验系统性地操控三个因素：日志记录器与目标的行动对齐程度（通过使用目标自身评分构建的记录器锐化，或给记录器注入行动级别的分歧）、交叉拟合策略（仅交叉拟合结果扰动项vs诚实的策略级划分）、以及倾向性估计方式（样本内vs折外估计，倾向性下限设为0.02）。

Result: （1）弱重叠由记录器与目标行动的对齐度决定，而非仅由记录锐度决定：用目标自身评分锐化记录器几乎不改变重叠，而行动级分歧则会使其崩溃；有效样本量（ESS）能在不同日志环境间排序风险，但在单一日志内排序候选估计器的能力较弱，且其截断点不具有迁移性。（2）交叉拟合结果扰动项不能解决优化器诅咒：在评估所用数据上拟合规则时，仅交叉拟合扰动项不仅保留重用偏差还使其更糟；诚实的策略级划分通过改变估计目标（评估学习过程的价值而非全样本策略的偏差校正）来解决该问题。（3）倾向性估计误差是最大的性能退化来源：折外估计对IPS的损害超过其他所有压力测试，但对双重稳健估计几乎无影响，甚至可能反转重叠诊断本身。

Conclusion: 论文得出三个核心结论：弱重叠的真正驱动因素是记录器与目标的行动对齐程度而非记录锐度；优化器诅咒需要策略级的诚实划分来改变估计目标，而非仅通过交叉拟合扰动项来去偏；倾向性估计误差是OPE管道中最关键的退化因素。同时，倾向性下限设为0.02使得所有失败都发生在有界权重下，且该下限使两个调优后的混合估计器退化为未调优的基础版本，最终只有四种实际不同的估计器。所有精确值表面均为合成或半合成数据，基准已公开（仅公开数据）。

Abstract: Organizations decide whom to treat under a budget and want to know what a targeting rule would have earned before deploying it. Off-policy evaluation promises this from logged data, but the deployable rule is a deterministic top-k policy: it removes all averaging over actions, so weak overlap hits the estimate directly. We benchmark six estimators across five datasets and two known-effect sweeps, and validate the mechanisms against a non-simulated paired reference. First, weak overlap is governed by logger-target action alignment, not by logging sharpness alone: what governs support is the logger's probability of the target's actions. Sharpening a logger built from the target's own score barely moves overlap over the tested range; action-level disagreement collapses it. Effective sample size ranks this risk across logging environments, but is weak at ranking candidates within the single log a practitioner holds, and its cut point does not transfer. Second, the optimizer's curse is not fixed by cross-fitting the outcome nuisance. When the rule is fit on the data used to evaluate it, cross-fitting the nuisance alone leaves the reuse bias in place and makes it worse. Honest policy-level splitting avoids the reuse by targeting the learning procedure's value -- a change of estimand, not a de-biasing of the full-sample policy. Third, propensity-estimation error is the largest degradation we measure: an out-of-fold estimate hurts IPS more than any other stress we apply, leaves doubly-robust estimation almost unchanged, and can invert the overlap diagnostic itself. Logging is synthesized and propensities floored at 0.02, so every failure occurs with bounded weights; the floor also reduces the two tuned hybrids to their untuned parents, leaving four practically distinct estimators, and all exact-value surfaces are synthetic or semi-synthetic. We release the benchmark; public data only.

</details>


### [96] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文以心脏骤停后神经预后预测为案例，指出当治疗决策使临床相关结局对部分患者永久不可观测时，传统的预测模型评估指标会掩盖重要失效模式。作者提出将患者分为"确定结局"和"不确定结局"两类进行显式拆分评估的框架，并提出一个能在两类标签间权衡的简单预测模型。实验表明，尽管模型在确定案例上的AUROC相近，但在Brier分数和不确定案例的概率估计上差异显著，揭示了两者之间存在标准评估无法体现的显式权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床预测模型的开发假设所有患者的结局都是干净可观测的，但现实中治疗决策会使临床相关结局对部分患者永久不可观测。作者以心脏骤停后神经预后预测为案例，指出标准评估指标在这些"不确定案例"上会掩盖模型的关键失效模式，而这些恰恰是最需要预后辅助支持的人群。

Method: 作者提出一个将评估显式拆分为"确定案例"（结局可观测）和"不确定案例"（由独立临床专家提供反事实结局推测）两部分的评估框架。在此基础上，提出一个简单预测模型，使用确定和不确定案例的标签进行训练，并允许在这两类目标标签之间进行权衡。实验中使用该神经模型与一系列表格基线模型进行对比。

Result: 在2,497名患者（含1,429名结局不确定的患者）的队列中，具有相似确定案例AUROC的模型在确定案例Brier分数和不确定案例概率估计上存在显著差异。提高不确定案例标签的校准（alignment）通常以牺牲确定案例的准确率为代价，凸显出标准评估所掩盖的显式权衡。

Conclusion: 当治疗决策决定临床有意义结局是否可观测时，传统评估指标会遗漏对最需要预后支持的患者的重大失效模式。因此，预测模型的评估和开发必须显式考虑这类结构性的数据缺失问题。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models are often developed as if the outcome of interest were cleanly observed for every patient. This assumption fails when treatment decisions make the clinically relevant outcome permanently unobservable. As a case study of this problem, we consider post-cardiac-arrest neurological prognostication using a cohort of 2,497 patients, including 1,429 patients whose outcomes were rendered indeterminate by treatment decisions. These patients with indeterminate outcomes were reviewed by independent clinical experts, who provided their guesses of counterfactual outcomes about what would have happened to the patients. We refer to these patients as uncertain cases. We also have patients for whom we observe their clinically relevant outcomes; we refer to these patients as certain cases. We propose a framework for evaluating prediction models that explicitly splits the evaluation between certain and uncertain cases. Here, we cannot easily evaluate both types of cases in a uniform manner as the available target labels differ. We then propose a simple prediction model that uses target labels from both certain and uncertain cases in a manner that allows us to trade off between them. Across the proposed neural model and a collection of tabular baselines, models with similar certain-case AUROC can nevertheless differ substantially in both certain-case Brier score and their probability estimates for uncertain cases. Improving alignment with target labels of uncertain cases for our proposed model generally comes at the cost of worse accuracy on certain cases, highlighting an explicit tradeoff that standard evaluation conceals. These results show that when treatment decisions determine whether clinically meaningful outcomes remain observable, conventional evaluation metrics can miss important failure modes in the very patients for whom prognostic support matters most.

</details>


### [97] [Interpretable Causal Discovery via Causal-Effect Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12640)
*Cixuan Zhang,Guy Van den Broeck,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种条件因果发现方法，将因果发现与稀有事件估计技术相结合，在贝叶斯框架下对满足特定因果事件约束（如因果效应大小）的因果图和参数的后验分布进行推断，通过粒子驱动的逐步逼近策略有效应对后验质量极小区域的采样挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现的目标不仅是预测因果边，还要能够解释和解释观察到的或假设的现象（如特别大的因果效应）。当事件（如因果效应约束）在后验中占据极小质量时，现有的贝叶斯因果发现方法难以有效采样，需要新的方法来解决这一计算挑战。

Method: 将条件因果发现形式化为贝叶斯推断问题，目标是在事件约束（如因果效应约束）条件下估计因果图和参数的后验分布。采用稀有事件估计技术（rare-event estimation techniques），在联合图-参数空间中通过粒子群逐步驱向约束区域，同时保持样本近似条件后验分布。

Result: 在合成图上进行了小规模和大规模的验证实验，确认了方法的准确性。并在Sachs蛋白数据集上的案例研究中展示了该方法如何通过提供通路级摘要来辅助科学探索。

Conclusion: 该方法有效地解决了在事件约束下进行贝叶斯因果发现的计算难题，尤其适用于后验质量极小的情形，并能为科学研究提供通路层面的可解释性摘要，弥合了从数据中预测因果关系到解释因果现象之间的差距。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover the underlying causal relationships given data generated from a system. The goal, however, is not merely to predict causal edges given data, but also to be able to interpret and explain either observed or hypothesized phenomena, such as a particularly large causal effect. We consider this task of conditional causal discovery and cast it as a Bayesian inference problem, in which we target the posterior over causal graphs and parameters conditional on an event such as a causal-effect constraint. Unfortunately, this poses a computational challenge: existing approaches to Bayesian causal discovery struggle when the event has small posterior mass. To address this, we adapt rare-event estimation techniques to perform inference the joint graph-parameter space. Our method gradually drives a particle population toward the constrained region while maintaining samples that approximate the conditional posterior. Empirical evaluation on synthetic graphs validates the accuracy of our approach at small and large scales, and we show in a case study on the Sachs protein dataset how our method can be used to aid scientific exploration by providing pathway-level summaries.

</details>


### [98] [Training Under Challenge: Executable Certificates and Challenge-Closed Optimality for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12655)
*Farhang Yeganegi,Arian Eamaz,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Training Under Challenge"（挑战式训练）框架，通过预声明的、架构合法的程序构造等价类中的完整替代方案并重新评估同一目标函数，任何更低值的候选者都是可复现的"见证"，用于下界估计检查点的经验全局最优性差距。作者定义了资源索引的挑战幂模量，证明无覆盖情况下一阶ReLU训练器可收敛到非全局点，并在ResNet-18蒸馏问题和量化去噪研究上验证了该框架的诊断、修复和重新认证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦的训练曲线无法区分神经网络是否达到全局最优、陷入局部陷阱、受表示能力限制或与训练器不匹配。传统方法无法提供可验证的最优性保证，因此需要一种可执行的证书框架来系统性地评估检查点的全局最优性差距。

Method: 提出Training Under Challenge框架：1) 预声明且架构合法的程序在认证类别中构造完整的替代方案并重新评估同一目标；2) 较低值的候选者作为可复现见证，下界估计检查点的经验全局最优性差距；3) 定义资源索引的挑战幂模量（challenge-power modulus）刻画与通过兼容的最大差距；4) 对平方损失采用块下降算子使覆盖可检查，得到一致和实现的残差界；5) 证明无覆盖时的逆向边界（converse frontier）。

Result: 在信道门控ResNet-18蒸馏问题中，8个内部挑战覆盖全部240个审计输出方向，实现残差界在真实差距的1.74-3.02倍范围内。成对预测证书能区分解码器利用不足与表示不足。量化去噪研究展示了诊断、修复和当前状态重新认证的能力。

Conclusion: 通过有限测试套件只能获得相对于套件的结论，全局差距结论需要单独论证的覆盖机制。该框架为神经网络训练过程提供了可执行、可复现的最优性证书，为实践中判断训练是否真正"完成"提供了新工具。

Abstract: A flat training curve does not reveal whether a neural network has reached a global optimum, is locally trapped, is representation-limited, or is mismatched to its trainer. We introduce Training Under Challenge, an executable-certificate framework in which predeclared, architecture-valid procedures construct complete alternatives in the same certified class and reevaluate the same objective. Any lower-valued candidate is a replayable witness that lower-bounds the checkpoint's empirical global-optimality gap. Passing a finite suite is only suite-relative; global-gap conclusions require a separately justified coverage mechanism. We define a resource-indexed challenge-power modulus that characterizes the largest gap compatible with passage. For squared loss, current block-decrease operators make coverage checkable and yield uniform and realized-residual bounds. We prove the converse frontier: without coverage, a first-order ReLU trainer can reach infinitely many exact conditional head optima while converging to a non-global point. On a channel-gated ResNet-18 distillation problem with known optimum, eight internal challenges cover all 240 audited output directions, and realized-residual bounds lie within factors of 1.74--3.02 of the true gap. Paired predictive certificates separate decoder under-use from representation insufficiency, while quantized-denoising studies demonstrate diagnosis, repair, and current-state recertification.

</details>


### [99] [Exploring Oversmoothing with Householder Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12514)
*Bhaskar Karol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HouseGNN（Householder图神经网络），通过使用Householder反射器和GroupSort激活函数构建逐层正交的图神经网络层，从理论上解决深度GNN的过平滑问题，证明每层保持节点欧氏范数不变，并允许节点间距离产生变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图神经网络（GNN）存在过平滑（oversmoothing）问题：随着网络深度增加，重复应用归一化图传播算子会导致节点表示逐渐坍缩到低信息子空间，损失区分度。标准的GCN直接将聚合的邻域消息更新到隐藏表示上，加剧了这一退化过程。

Method: HouseGNN的核心创新在于不直接使用聚合的邻域消息更新隐藏状态，而是仅将聚合消息用于估计反射方向；节点嵌入通过Householder反射器更新，再经过GroupSort激活函数，形成逐片正交（piecewise orthogonal）层。每个节点、每个深度都保持欧氏范数不变。

Result: 论文证明了三个核心性质：(i) 每一内部层保持节点级欧氏范数；(ii) Householder反射器对消息具有尺度和符号不变性；(iii) 节点间的成对距离可通过节点级正交算子的不匹配而发生改变，即不同节点可保持范数但方向不同，从而避免表示坍缩。

Conclusion: HouseGNN通过正交变换约束从理论上保证深度网络中层间表示不会因过平滑而坍缩，同时保留节点间的可区分性，为构建深度GNN提供了一种理论可靠的设计范式。

Abstract: Deep graph neural networks(GNNs) suffer from oversmoothing- a progressive collapse of node representation towards a low information subspace as network depth increases because the normalized graph propagation operator is repeatedly applied directly to the hidden representations. In this work we study Householder Graph Neural Network (HouseGNN). Rather than updating the hidden state like standard GCN, HouseGNN uses the aggregated neighbourhood message solely to estimate a reflection direction; the node embedding is then updated by a Householder reflector followed by GroupSort, yielding a piecewise orthogonal layer that preserves Euclidean norm at every node and at every depth. We prove three core properties: (i) every internal layer preserves the node-wise Euclidean norm; (ii) the Householder reflector is scale scale and sign-invariant in the message; and (iii) pairwise distance between nodes can change through mismatch between node-wise orthogonal operators.

</details>


### [100] [GENADA: efficient generative time series adversarial attack framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12535)
*Michael Baronov,Denis Vorobev,Margarita Rusanova,Petr Sokerin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GENADA，一种基于生成模型的对抗攻击方法，用于时间序列预测任务。通过训练生成模型在单次前向传播中直接产生欺骗性扰动，替代传统的迭代梯度攻击方法，在保持攻击效果的同时大幅降低推理时的计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于梯度的对抗攻击（如迭代一阶方法）需要对受害模型进行多次反向传播以计算输入梯度，计算成本高昂。作者希望设计一种更高效的攻击方法，能够在单次前向传播中生成对抗扰动，减少推理阶段的时间开销。

Method: GENADA通过训练一个生成模型来学习产生对抗性扰动，该模型经过训练后可在单次前向传播中直接输出欺骗性扰动。方法包含两种变体：单步生成攻击（single-step）和迭代生成攻击方案（iterative）。训练过程针对时间序列数据在低维受控环境下进行。

Result: 在时间序列领域的多个神经模型和数据集上进行验证，GENADA在攻击质量上与强基线方法相当，同时在推理阶段生成扰动所需的时间更少，体现了计算效率优势。

Conclusion: GENADA证明了生成式对抗攻击方法在时间序列任务上的可行性，能够在保证攻击效果的前提下显著降低扰动生成的计算成本，为高效对抗攻击提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Deep learning models are widely used for time series analysis in domains such as healthcare, finance, energy systems, and environmental monitoring. However, these models remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where small input perturbations cause severe degradation in predictive performance. Commonly used gradient-based attacks, iterative first-order methods, are computationally burdensome, as they repeatedly backpropagate through the victim model to compute input gradients during a number of iterative refinement steps. We propose a GENerative ADversarial Attack (GENADA) that learns a generative model to produce deceptive perturbations directly in a single forward pass and a procedure to train it. Variants include single-step and iterative generative attack schemes. The validation considers attacks on several neural models and datasets in the time-series domain, a controlled, low-dimensional setting. Empirically, GENADA achieves comparable attack quality to strong baselines while requiring less time to generate perturbations during inference.

</details>


### [101] [Scaling Automatic Research Agents via World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12564)
*Xiyuan Yang,Sheikh Sarwar,Jingru Cheng,Zhan Shi,Duanshun Li,Huiyuan Chen,Haiyang Zhang,Chenlei Guo,Jingrui He,Zhenyu Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出World Model RL（WMRL），用世界模型替代真实环境执行来解决AutoResearch智能体强化学习训练中的瓶颈，通过在线去偏和逆方差去噪两种缓解手段应对世界模型的不完美性，在各类任务上实现3-4倍加速并超越标准RL基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AutoResearch智能体的轨迹由两个部分组成：智能体的生成和环境执行。生成通过批处理共享计算资源，而每次执行独占沙箱和真实机器时间。随着轨迹增长，环境执行成为训练成本的主导瓶颈。需要解决这种生成与执行之间的根本性扩展张力。

Method: 提出WMRL框架：1）用世界模型替代真实环境执行，消除环境执行瓶颈；2）针对世界模型的不完美性（奖励被偏差和噪声污染），引入Online Debiasing（在线去偏）和Inverse-Variance Denoising（逆方差去噪）两种缓解机制，分别抵消偏差和抑制噪声。

Result: 理论上证明WMRL的两种缓解机制都严格改善收敛保证；实证上WMRL在多种任务和不同智能体规模下实现3-4倍训练加速，同时超越标准RL基线性能；后训练的4B和9B智能体在held-out基准上超越48B和120B的更大开源权重智能体；WMRL还成功迁移到embodied VLA策略的后训练，展示了方法的通用性。

Conclusion: WMRL通过世界模型替换环境执行有效解决了AutoResearch场景下强化学习训练的环境瓶颈问题，兼顾了训练效率与性能，且具有跨领域的通用性。

Abstract: Automating empirical research is a long-standing direction of AI. Recent automatic research (AutoResearch) agents bring this goal within reach, as modern LLMs show the capability to independently implement solutions and learn from the execution outcomes. Behind these gains, post-training (especially RL) plays a central role. In this paper, we identify a fundamental tension when scaling RL for these agents: the two components of every AutoResearch trajectory (agent generation and environment execution) scale in very different manners, since all generation shares compute through batching, while each execution occupies its exclusive sandbox and real machine time. As a result, the environment execution dominates the training cost and becomes the bottleneck as trajectories grow. To resolve this tension, we propose World Model RL (WMRL), which replaces environment execution with a world model to remove this bottleneck. Additionally, the world model can be imperfect, as its rewards are corrupted by bias and noise. Therefore, we further equip WMRL with two mitigations, Online Debiasing and Inverse-Variance Denoising, which offset the bias and suppress the noise respectively. Theoretically, we prove that both mitigations of WMRL strictly improve the convergence guarantee. Empirically, WMRL accelerates training by 3-4x on various tasks at different agent scales, while exceeding the performance of standard RL baselines. Moreover, our post-trained 4B and 9B agents outperform much larger open-weight agents of 48B and 120B on held-out benchmarks. Beyond AutoResearch, WMRL also transfers to post-training embodied VLA policies, which demonstrates the generalizability of our method.

</details>


### [102] [CardioState-JEPA: Delay-Aware Cross-Modal Learning of a Shared Cardiac Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12944)
*Hamza Shafiq,Hung Manh Pham,Bin Zhu,Pan Zhou,Jun Hu,Aaqib Saeed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CardioState-JEPA是一个基于生理感知联合嵌入预测架构的心脏基础模型，统一学习ECG、PPG和PCG三种心脏信号的共享表征，通过掩码潜态预测和延迟对齐器处理多模态信号，在25个下游任务上显著超越现有自监督基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ECG、PPG和PCG从不同角度反映同一心脏周期，但现有心脏基础模型仅针对单一传感模态训练，未能利用跨传感器的共享生理信息，限制了对心脏整体状态的理解。

Method: 采用生理感知的联合嵌入预测架构(JEPA)，将异构波形映射到统一token空间，用单一共享Transformer编码器处理；预训练目标为预测掩码潜态心脏状态而非传感器特定波形外观；引入学习式延迟对齐器处理电-机械-血流事件间的时间偏移；先利用丰富单模态数据学习模态内结构，再通过配对数据在潜态心脏时间中对齐模态。

Result: 作为冻结编码器在25个下游任务上评估，PPG分类平均提升8.2 AUROC，PCG杂音检测提升18.8 AUROC，ECG分类提升15.5 AUROC；在多个ECG基准上与使用特权临床文本或有监督标签训练的心脏模型持平或超越。

Conclusion: 异构心脏信号可以相互监督一个统一的心脏生理基础模型，跨模态共享表征比单模态训练更具优势，为心脏多模态基础模型提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Electrocardiography (ECG), photoplethysmography (PPG), and phonocardiography (PCG) provide complementary views of the same cardiac cycle, yet existing cardiac foundation models are trained for a single sensing modality, leaving the shared physiology across sensors unexploited. We introduce CardioState-JEPA, a cardiac foundation model to learn a single shared representation jointly across ECG, PPG, and PCG, built on a physiology-aware joint-embedding predictive architecture. The model maps heterogeneous waveforms into a common token space, processes them with a single shared Transformer encoder, and learns by predicting masked latent cardiac states, placing the pretraining target on shared physiology rather than sensor-specific waveform appearance. To handle the temporal offsets between electrical, mechanical, and hemodynamic events, cross-modal prediction uses a learned delay aligner that matches signals at the corresponding cardiac time. Because synchronized multi-sensor recordings are scarce, CardioState-JEPA first learns within-modality structure from abundant unimodal data and then uses paired data to align modalities in latent cardiac time. Evaluated as a frozen encoder across 25 downstream tasks spanning ECG, PPG, and PCG, our encoder improves average PPG classification by 8.2 AUROC points, PCG murmur detection by 18.8 AUROC points, and ECG classification by 15.5 AUROC points over the best self-supervised signal baseline and matches or exceeds cardiac models trained with privileged clinical text or supervised labels on several ECG benchmarks. These results establish that heterogeneous cardiac signals can mutually supervise a single foundation model of cardiac physiology.

</details>


### [103] [Prof-K: Probabilistic One-Pass Filtering for Efficient Top-k Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12573)
*Tadeusz Dziarmaga,Witold Sikora,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Mazur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prof-K，一种快速、可扩展且分布无关的top-k选择算法。它通过单遍过滤流程（随机采样估计自适应阈值、流式缓冲输入、在缓冲区上执行精确top-k）实现概率正确性保证，正确率至少为1-ε。相比PyTorch topk和RadiK实现获得1.5-10倍加速，且保证独立于输入分布，适用于对抗性输入场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据规模增长，现有top-k方法效率低下：精确方法计算和内存开销高，近似方法依赖脆弱的启发式规则，在对抗性或重尾输入下性能退化。需要一种兼具速度、可扩展性且不受输入分布影响的稳健top-k算法。

Method: Prof-K采用单遍过滤流程：（1）从N个输入元素中随机抽取小样本估计自适应阈值；（2）将全部N个元素流式遍历一遍，仅将高于阈值的元素写入紧凑缓冲区；（3）在缓冲区上运行精确top-k算法恢复真实的top-k元素。算法推导了正确性和缓冲区大小的高概率保证，以及近似最优的样本大小（作为N和k的函数）以最小化开销。

Result: 实验表明，Prof-K在高度优化的PyTorch topk和RadiK实现基础上实现1.5x-10x加速，在大型、小到中等k值的场景中增益最大。此外，可放宽recall目标（如恢复95%的真实top-k值）来获取原理化的精度-速度权衡，并在训练BatchTopK稀疏自编码器（SAEs）中展示了对训练成本的显著降低。

Conclusion: Prof-K提供了分布无关的top-k选择方法，其概率正确性保证不受输入分布影响，在对抗性环境下依然稳健。通过精确的数据结构设计和理论保证，它在速度和准确性之间提供了可调节的权衡，并在实际机器学习工作负载（如稀疏自编码器训练）中展现出显著加速效果。

Abstract: Top-k selection is a fundamental computational primitive with applications spanning databases, information retrieval, signal processing, and modern machine learning workloads, including sparse activations and attention pruning. As data sizes grow, existing approaches become inefficient: exact methods incur high memory and compute overhead, while approximate methods often rely on brittle heuristics that degrade under adversarial or heavy-tailed inputs. In this paper, we introduce Prof-K, a fast, scalable, and distribution-agnostic top-k algorithm with probabilistic correctness guarantees. Prof-K performs a single-pass filtering procedure: a small random sample estimates an adaptive threshold, the N input elements are streamed once into a compact buffer, and an exact top-k routine on this buffer recovers the true top-k elements with probability at least 1 - $ε$, where $ε$ > 0 is user specified. We derive high-probability guarantees for correctness and buffer size, together with an approximately optimal sample size that minimizes overhead as a function of N and k. Empirically, Prof-K achieves 1.5x-10x speedups over the highly optimized PyTorch topk and recent RadiK implementations, with the largest gains in the large-scale, small-to-moderate-k regime where prior methods struggle most. Unlike previous approaches, these guarantees hold independently of the input distribution, ensuring robustness to adversarial settings. By relaxing the recall target (e.g., recovering 95% of the true top-k values), Prof-K additionally provides a principled accuracy-speed trade-off. We further demonstrate its impact on training BatchTopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where top-k selection constitutes a significant portion of the training cost.

</details>


### [104] [The data geometry of masking diffusion: Certified-optimal schedules via unmasking growth complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13520)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究离散采样的掩码扩散（masking diffusion），引入了一种新的数据几何路径度量——去掩码增长复杂度（UGC），其在局部增量上直接控制KL离散化误差，从而统一分析了Bernoulli子集和固定基数去掩码方案。基于此结构可设计优化调度，并利用沿耦合揭示轨迹的KL增量估计UGC增量，构建达到预设KL误差且迭代复杂度为常数因子的认证最优采样器。在细划分极限下，UGC密度的平方根积分平方确定最优欧拉离散化误差的首阶项，示例显示可相对粗调度实现√d量级的维度相关改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型近年来在生成任务中表现出色，但其离散采样的离散化误差缺乏统一的理论框架。此前分析依赖于特定调度和数据假设，难以刻画数据几何对采样效率的影响。本文旨在建立一种统一的、由数据几何信息驱动的度量方式，以理解并优化掩码扩散中的调度选择与计算分配问题。

Method: 作者定义了一种称为去掩码增长复杂度（UGC）的路径度量。利用其局部增量直接关联KL离散化误差，在log-揭示-比值坐标系下推导出优化的单块与多块调度。通过沿耦合揭示轨迹估计KL增量来估计UGC增量，从而构建在预设KL误差下高概率达标且迭代复杂度为常数因子的认证最优采样器。在细划分极限下分析UGC密度的平方根积分，确定最优欧拉离散化误差的首阶渐近行为。

Result: 建立了统一的KL离散化误差分析框架，证明了UGC路径坍缩后的聚合质量与经典多元依赖度量及先前离散扩散复杂度度量相关联。在细划分极限下获得了最优欧拉离散化误差的精确首阶表达式，并展示了通过常数个自适应放置的块即可实现̃Ω(√d)的维度相关改进，体现了显著超越粗调度的优势。

Conclusion: UGC提供了一种由数据几何驱动的、可估计的度量框架，统一了多样化掩码扩散方案的分析，并能指导自适应调度的设计，在不增加块复杂度的情况下获得实质性的维度增益。该框架兼具理论精确性与实践可估计性，为离散扩散采样器的性能保障提供了新的基础。

Abstract: We study masking diffusion for discrete sampling and introduce a path-resolved measure of data geometry called the \emph{unmasking growth complexity} ({\textsf{UGC}\xspace}). Its local increments directly control Kullback--Leibler (KL) discretization error, yielding a unified analysis of Bernoulli-subset and fixed-cardinality unmasking schemes. In log-reveal-odds coordinates, this structure yields optimized single-block and multi-block schedules, and quantifies the gains from adapting computational effort to data geometry. Crucially, we show how {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} increments can be estimated from samples via KL increments along coupled reveal trajectories. This leads to \emph{certified-optimal} samplers that achieve a prescribed KL error with high probability and iteration complexity within a constant factor of the corresponding oracle procedure. Collapsing the \ugc path yields the aggregate {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} mass, which connects to classical multivariate dependence measures and complexity measures from previous analyses of discrete diffusion. In the fine-partition limit, the squared integral of the square-root {\textsf{UGC}\xspace} density determines the sharp leading-order optimal Euler discretization error. Examples exhibit substantial dimension-dependent gains over coarse schedules, including $\widetildeΩ(\sqrt{d})$ improvements achievable with a constant number of adaptively placed blocks.

</details>


### [105] [Represent, Then Generate: Multimodal-Conditioned Time-Series Generation under Irregular Missingness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12592)
*Haochen Zhang,Jiaheng Guo,Yu-Chao Huang,Nicholas Knoz,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCoGen是一个两阶段条件生成框架，将多模态条件表示与目标信号生成解耦：第一阶段用掩码自编码器将每个时变条件模态蒸馏为紧凑且对缺失鲁棒的token序列，第二阶段用流匹配生成器融合这些token与静态条件合成目标信号。在三个生理基准（AI-READI连续血糖监测、MIMIC-III/IV动脉血压生成）上，ReCoGen在所有16个（数据集、任务、指标）组合中均优于六个代表性条件生成器，其中13个设置的效用达到或超过真实信号的效用水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床监测中许多最有信息量的生理信号（如连续血糖、动脉血压）具有侵入性、昂贵或对特定患者不可用。虽然条件生成可以通过共同记录的信号合成缺失信号，但现有生成器仅基于单一条件模态构建，无法处理实践中常见的异构、不规则缺失的时变信号与静态协变量混合场景。

Method: ReCoGen采用两阶段框架：阶段一，为每个模态训练独立的掩码自编码器（masked autoencoder），将时变条件蒸馏为紧凑且容忍缺失的token序列；阶段二，训练一个流匹配（flow-matching）生成器，通过可学习的跨注意力机制融合冻结的各模态编码器输出，并采用双通道（token+AdaLN）方式融合静态条件，最终合成目标信号。

Result: 在包含AI-READI连续血糖监测和MIMIC-III/IV动脉血压生成的三个生理基准上，ReCoGen在所有16个（数据集、任务、指标）设置中取得最佳下游效用，超越六个代表性条件生成器；其中13个设置达到或超过真实信号上测得的效用水平。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明增益来自条件路径：冻结每模态编码器上的可学习跨注意力机制，以及静态条件的双token+AdaLN路线。ReCoGen证明了将常规采集信号转化为侵入性或不可用信号的近似替代品的可行性，是迈向侵入性更小、成本更低的连续临床监测的一步。

Abstract: Continuous physiological time series underpin modern clinical monitoring, yet many of the most informative signals are invasive, expensive, or simply unavailable for a given patient. Conditional generation offers a remedy: an absent signal can be synthesized from co-recorded signals and routine clinical variables. Existing generators, however, are built around a single conditioning modality and degrade when forced to handle the heterogeneous, irregularly missing mix of time-variant signals and static covariates seen in practice. We propose ReCoGen (Represent Conditions, then Generate), a two-stage framework that decouples multimodal condition representation from target generation. Stage I trains one masked autoencoder per modality, distilling each time-variant condition into a compact and missingness-tolerant token sequence. Stage II trains a flow-matching generator that fuses these tokens with static conditions to synthesize the target signal. Across three physiological benchmarks, including continuous glucose monitoring on AI-READI and arterial blood pressure generation on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV, ReCoGen attains the best downstream utility on all sixteen (dataset, task, metric) settings, surpassing six representative conditional generators; on thirteen of them its utility also reaches or exceeds the utility measured on the real signal, a reference we read as an approximate anchor rather than a ceiling. Ablations trace the gains to the conditioning path: learnable cross-attention over the frozen per-modality encoders, and a dual token-plus-AdaLN route for the static conditions. ReCoGen thus turns routinely collected signals into informative surrogates for invasive or unavailable ones, a step toward less invasive, lower-cost continuous clinical monitoring.

</details>


### [106] [Exponential Convex Calibration Dimension for the Multi-Label Jaccard Measure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13549)
*Mingyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多标签分类中逐实例Jaccard得分（IoU）的校准理论。证明了Jaccard得分、移位损失和普通损失矩阵的非奇异性，给出了Calibration维度（CCdim）的上下界（2^{s-1}到2^s-1），表明精确校准需要指数多个预测坐标。同时提供了两个多项式维度的近似保证：F_1到Jaccard的迁移给出asymptotic regret ≤ 3-2√2，MinHash平方损失代理给出任意固定additive regret容差的O((s+log(1/ρ))/α²)维度构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jaccard得分（IoU）是多标签分类和二元分割中的标准指标，但其逐实例版本（per-instance）的校准理论尚不清晰。关键问题是：是否存在多项式维度的代理损失能够精确校准Jaccard得分？以及如果可以接受近似校准，需要多少预测坐标？本文旨在严格刻画Jaccard校准的维度复杂度上下界，并给出实用的近似代理构造。

Method: (1) 利用有限MinHash Gram表示与布尔Möbius反演证明Jaccard、shifted-loss和ordinary loss矩阵的非奇异性及损失列的仿射维度为2^s-1。(2) 下界证明使用因子加权分布（factorial weighted distribution）支持2^{s-1}+1个结果和Bayes最优报告，推导CCdim≥2^{s-1}；上界由矩阵非奇异性得CCdim≤2^s-1。(3) 近似部分：将现有(s²+1)维F_1代理转化为Jaccard代理，实现asymptotic regret ≤ 3-2√2；构造MinHash平方损失代理，分别给出直接构造（O((s²+s log(1/ρ))/α²)维）和带符号变体（O((s+log(1/ρ))/α²)维）的维度保证。

Result: (1) 证明了Jaccard、shifted-loss和ordinary loss矩阵非奇异，损失列仿射维度为2^s-1。(2) 精确校准的CCdim满足2^{s-1} ≤ CCdim ≤ 2^s-1，因此任何精确校准的凸代理至少需要指数多个预测坐标。(3) F_1到Jaccard的迁移给出asymptotic Jaccard regret ≤ 3-2√2 ≈ 0.1716。(4) MinHash平方损失代理可实现任意固定的additive regret容差α，带符号变体维度为O((s+log(1/ρ))/α²)。

Conclusion: 精确校准Jaccard得分本质上是困难的——需要指数维度的预测坐标（CCdim下界为2^{s-1}），这源于Jaccard损失矩阵的几何结构。然而，若仅要求固定的additive regret容差α>0，则存在多项式维度的代理构造（带符号MinHash变体仅需O(s/α²)维）。这刻画了Jaccard校准中"精确vs近似"的维度本质分野：零遗憾需要指数维度，任何固定容差则只需多项式维度。

Abstract: The per-instance Jaccard score, or intersection over union (IoU), is standard in multi-label classification and binary segmentation. With $s$ labels, its loss matrix has $2^s$ outcomes and reports. Under the convention $\mathrm{Jac}(\varnothing,\varnothing)=1$, we prove that the Jaccard score, shifted-loss, and ordinary loss matrices are nonsingular and that the loss columns have affine dimension $2^s-1$. The proof combines a finite MinHash Gram representation with Boolean Möbius inversion. For exact calibration, we prove $2^{s-1} \leq \mathrm{CCdim}(L^{\mathrm{Jac}}) \leq 2^s-1$. The lower bound uses a factorially weighted distribution with $2^{s-1}+1$ supported outcomes and Bayes-optimal reports. Consequently, every exactly calibrated convex surrogate requires exponentially many prediction coordinates. We also give two polynomial-dimensional approximation guarantees with explicit regret transfers. A new $F_1$-to-Jaccard transfer turns an existing $(s^2+1)$-dimensional $F_1$ surrogate into a polynomial-time rule with asymptotic Jaccard regret at most $3-2\sqrt{2}$. For any $α>0$ and $0<ρ<1$, a MinHash square-loss surrogate attains Jaccard-regret floor $α$ uniformly over arbitrary conditional label distributions. With probability at least $1-ρ$, the direct construction has dimension $O((s^2+s\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$, while a signed variant has dimension $O((s+\log(1/ρ))/α^2)$. Thus zero-regret calibration requires exponential dimension, whereas every fixed additive regret tolerance admits polynomial prediction dimension.

</details>


### [107] [Predicting When Random Low-Dimensional Reparameterizations Train Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12597)
*Andrew Cheng,Ali Eslamian,Jie Cheng,Mehdi Zargham,Qiang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究通过随机低维重参数化训练神经网络的可行性，提出了一个方向分辨的二次主公式来预测随机切片残差，并基于此设计了RaMaN框架（使用结构化Hadamard或种子再生高斯映射），避免了稠密随机映射的O(dP)存储开销，将优化器状态内存从O(P)降至O(d)。实验表明该预测器能紧密跟踪过渡位置，且在方向相关的场景中优于方向不可知的近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机低维重参数化框架下，一个核心实践问题是：潜在搜索空间需要多大维度才能达到低损失区域？已有的可达性过渡（accessibility transition）理论需要扩展为更实用的、能预测随机切片残差的定量工具，以指导实际网络训练中的维度选择。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1) 将已知的可达性过渡以等价锥形式表达，以极锥的统计维度为中心；(2) 提出方向分辨的二次主公式，同时考虑曲率谱和参考到解位移轮廓，预测随机切片残差；(3) 推导自洽的各向同性方向预测器，并在保守的仅半径特化下恢复先前的Gaussian宽度二次上界；(4) 基于该分析提出RaMaN框架，使用结构化Hadamard或种子再生高斯映射实例化预测的潜维度，避免O(dP)存储，将优化器状态从O(P)降至O(d)；(5) 开发无矩阵曲率近似和免扫描维度选择方法。

Result: 在受控二次和神经曲率实验中，方向分辨预测器能紧密跟踪测量的过渡位置，且在位移方向重要时优于方向不可知的近似。端到端实验在图像和语言模型上展示了尖锐且依赖协议的训练过渡。

Conclusion: 方向分辨的二次主公式为随机低维重参数化提供了精确的定量预测工具；RaMaN框架在保持预测能力的同时大幅降低了存储和计算开销，使得该方法可扩展到实际的大规模神经网络训练场景。

Abstract: Neural networks can often be trained or fine-tuned through random low-dimensional reparameterization, where a small latent vector is mapped into a full parameter update by a frozen random map. This raises a practical question: how large must the latent search space be to reach a low-loss region? We first express the known accessibility transition in an equivalent conic form, centered for compact convex targets at the statistical dimension of the polar cone. Our main theoretical contribution is an orientation-resolved quadratic master formula that predicts the random-slice residual from both the curvature spectrum and the reference-to-solution displacement profile. It yields a self-consistent isotropic-orientation predictor and, in a conservative radius-only specialization, recovers the earlier Gaussian-width quadratic bound. Building on this analysis, we introduce Random Mapping Networks (RaMaN), which instantiate the predicted latent dimension using structured Hadamard or seed-regenerated Gaussian maps. These constructions avoid the O(dP) storage of dense random maps and reduce optimizer-state memory from O(P) to O(d). We also develop matrix-free curvature approximations and sweep-free dimension selection. Across controlled quadratic and neural-curvature experiments, the orientation-resolved predictor closely tracks measured transition locations and outperforms orientation-agnostic approximations when displacement direction matters. End-to-end experiments further show sharp, protocol-dependent training transitions across image and language models.

</details>


### [108] [Defensive Boosting for Online Probabilistic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13554)
*Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"防御性提升器"(Defensive Booster)，一种用于自适应对手选择的二元结果在线概率预测算法。它同时实现两种不可比较的保证：与在线梯度提升相当的Brier分数竞争性，以及与在线分类提升相当的弱学习条件下的分类误差保证，且仅需一个弱学习器即可实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线提升技术分别提供两种不可同时获得的保证：在线梯度提升在Brier分数上与H的跨度诱导的最佳预测器竞争，但当跨度不含准确预测器时无所作为；在线弱到强提升在弱学习条件下将分类误差降至零，但条件不满足时无保证。作者希望用一种算法同时获得这两种保证。

Method: 作者操作化了提升的"对偶观点"：当算法随机分类误差持续偏高时，其错误权重形成一种平滑重加权，使得每个弱假设的边缘都很低，从而提供事后硬核证书证明弱学习条件失败。算法仅访问一个弱类别学习器，而非维持大规模弱学习器集成。此外还开发了强自适应变体，能在每个时间区间上同时满足两种保证。

Result: Defensive Booster在每条自适应序列上以与在线梯度提升相同的速率实现Brier分数竞争性；同时，当实际转录满足平滑弱学习条件时，其Brier分数和随机分类误差与在线分类提升具有相同的速率保证。实验表明其在合成和真实数据流上表现优异，有时大幅超越所有先前的基线，且运行时间快数个数量级。

Conclusion: Defensive Booster通过防御性预测框架统一了在线提升的两种不可比较的保证，其核心洞察是将提升的对偶视图操作化：持续的随机分类错误本身即构成弱学习条件失败的硬核证书。该方法在性能与计算效率上均优于先前方法。

Abstract: We study online probabilistic forecasting of binary outcomes chosen by an adaptive adversary. Given an online learning algorithm for a weak hypothesis class $H$, we would like to efficiently obtain two incomparable guarantees that existing online boosting techniques provide separately. Online gradient boosting competes in Brier score with the best predictor induced by the span of $H$ on every sequence, but promises nothing when the span does not contain an accurate predictor. Online weak-to-strong boosting drives classification error to zero under a weak-learning condition, but promises little when that condition fails.
  We give a simple defensive forecasting algorithm, the Defensive Booster, that obtains both guarantees. On every adaptive sequence, its Brier score is competitive with the best prediction induced by the span of $H$ at the same rate as online gradient boosting; simultaneously, whenever the realized transcript satisfies the smooth weak-learning condition, its Brier score and randomized classification error satisfy the same rate guarantee as online classification boosting. This is achieved by operationalizing the "dual view" of boosting: When the algorithm's randomized classification error is persistently high, its mistake weights form a smooth reweighting on which every weak hypothesis has low edge, yielding an ex-post hard-core certificate that the weak-learning condition fails. We also develop a strongly adaptive variant, which satisfies both guarantees on every time interval. The Defensive Booster is very efficient: it accesses just one weak-class learner, whereas the prior online boosting methods we compare against maintain large weak-learner ensembles. Experiments on synthetic and real data streams demonstrate its strong predictive performance (sometimes substantially improving over all prior baselines) coupled with orders-of-magnitude faster runtime.

</details>


### [109] [The Boolean Power of ReLU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12617)
*Pablo Barceló,Floris Geerts,Matthias Lanzinger,Klara Pakhomenko,Jan Van den Bussche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在有限简单无向图（带单个布尔节点特征）上，任何由最终常数激活函数集合Σ（任意实数系数）定义的Σ-MPLang可表达的布尔查询，都是ReLU-MPLang可表达布尔查询的严格子类。这解决了近期提出的开放问题：ReLU-MPLang在布尔查询上是否比trReLU-MPLang更强大。由此推出ReLU-GNN在布尔查询上严格强于{TrReLU,id}-GNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决一个近期提出的开放问题，即ReLU-MPLang与trReLU-MPLang在布尔查询表达能力上是否存在差异，从而厘清不同激活函数对GNN布尔查询表达能力的影响。

Method: 通过构造性证明，在带单个布尔节点特征的有限简单无向图上，证明任意最终常数激活函数集合Σ对应的Σ-MPLang布尔查询严格包含于ReLU-MPLang的布尔查询类中，建立两个语言类之间的严格包含关系。

Result: 证明了Σ-MPLang的布尔查询类是ReLU-MPLang布尔查询类的严格子类，从而回答了该开放问题，并推出ReLU-GNN严格强于{TrReLU,id}-GNN在布尔查询方面的表达能力。

Conclusion: ReLU激活函数在GNN布尔查询表达能力上具有优越性，ReLU-MPLang（及其对应GNN）在表达能力上严格强于使用最终常数激活函数的变体。

Abstract: We prove that, on finite simple undirected graphs equipped with a single Boolean node feature, the Boolean queries expressible in $Σ$-MPLang, for any collection $Σ$ of eventually constant activation functions and with arbitrary real coefficients, form a strict subclass of the Boolean queries expressible in ReLU-MPLang. We thereby settle a recently posed open problem: whether ReLU-MPLang is more powerful than trReLU-MPLang when it comes to Boolean queries. In particular, this implies that ReLU-GNNs are strictly more expressive than {TrReLU,id}-GNNs with respect to Boolean queries on Boolean-featured graphs.

</details>


### [110] [CAKE: Compiler-Agent Co-Design for Frontier Kernel Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12629)
*Zihao Ye,Yingyi Huang,Hongyi Jin,Bohan Hou,Junru Shao,Zhongming Yu,Jinqi Chen,Meghan Cowan,Shiyi Cao,Shanli Xing,Hanfeng Chen,Vinod Grover,Tianqi Chen,Luis Ceze*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAKE是一个编译器-代理协同设计框架，通过硬件显式的类型化调度表示(CAKE IR)让GPU内核生成代理直接操控warp角色、内存移动、同步和流水线，并通过自演化的编译器基础设施（失败转为验证规则、IR原语、模型校准和优化策略）实现专家级内核性能。在Flash-KMeans基准上超越FlashML baseline 1.144x，在Kimi Delta Attention上实现2.05x加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内核代理和GPU编程语言各自独立发展，导致专家级内核难以复现。代理通常将编译器视为固定黑盒，只能接收错误信息、正确性结果和计时数据；而现有DSL要么隐藏关键调度决策，要么通过难以理解的布局抽象暴露它们。这种割裂阻碍了AI代理自动生成高性能可移植的GPU内核。

Method: 提出CAKE（Compiler-Agent Kernel Evolution）协同设计方法：1) 设计CAKE IR——一种类型化、硬件显式（hardware-explicit）的调度表示，暴露warp角色、内存移动、同步和流水线等底层细节；2) 提供验证、成本建模和局部化诊断支持；3) 构建自演进（self-evolving）的编译基础设施——失败的案例自动转化为验证器规则、IR原语、模型校准和可复用优化策略；4) 分离单形状（single-shape）内核演化与库泛化和调度分发。

Result: 在B200上、实现细节隐藏的Flash-KMeans干净起步（clean start）评测中，80M-token预算下最优CAKE IR候选达到tuned FlashML baseline的1.144x，而直接使用CUDA/PTX仅达0.928x。代理生成的Kimi Delta Attention相对官方FlashKDA实现2.05x几何平均加速并通过端到端服务验证。基于调度器（dispatcher）的KNN和KMeans在400多种shape上提升1.42x到2.12x。已有四个内核变更作为上游PR提交。

Conclusion: CAKE证明了编译器与代理的深度协同设计（而非将编译器视为黑盒）是释放代理生成高性能GPU内核潜力的关键路径。硬件显式的IR加上自演化的工具链能显著超越传统直接编程方式，覆盖从Ampere到Blackwell的NVIDIA GPU架构，并支持从单形状优化到库级泛化与分发的完整流程。

Abstract: GPU kernel agents and GPU programming languages have advanced separately, leaving expert kernels difficult to reproduce. Agents usually treat the compiler as a fixed black box and receive only errors, correctness outcomes, and timing, while existing DSLs either hide critical scheduling decisions or expose them through difficult layout abstractions. We present CAKE, a compiler-agent co-design in which agents author CAKE IR, a typed, hardware-explicit schedule representation. CAKE exposes warp roles, memory movement, synchronization, and pipelines while supporting verification, cost modeling, and localized diagnostics. The harness itself evolves: recurring failures become verifier rules, IR primitives, model calibrations, and reusable optimization tactics. In matched implementation-hidden Flash-KMeans clean starts on B200, the best CAKE IR candidate at an 80-million-token budget runs at 1.144x the tuned FlashML baseline, compared with 0.928x for direct CUDA/PTX. Beyond this benchmark, agent-generated Kimi Delta Attention achieves a 2.05x geometric-mean speedup over official FlashKDA and passes end-to-end serving validation. Dispatcher-backed KNN and KMeans improve performance by 1.42x to 2.12x across more than 400 shapes, and four kernel changes are available as upstream PRs. CAKE targets NVIDIA GPUs from Ampere through Blackwell and separates single-shape evolution from library generalization and dispatch.

</details>


### [111] [Federated Compositional Muon Optimizer for Matrix-Wise Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12710)
*Wang Yan,Feihu Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCoMuon优化器及其方差缩减变体FedCoMuon-VR，用于解决分布式矩阵逐元素组合优化问题。后者在非i.i.d.非凸设置下达到O(ε⁻³)的更低样本复杂度，并在鲁棒联邦学习和任务分布式风险敏感元学习实验中取得最优精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Muon优化器及其变体虽在矩阵逐元素模型中表现出色，但难以直接适用于层次化结构问题，尤其是分布式矩阵逐元素组合优化场景，亟需针对性的优化方法填补这一空白。

Method: FedCoMuon基于组合梯度跟踪（compositional gradient tracking）和正交化动量（orthogonalized momentum）构建；在此基础上引入基于动量的方差缩减技术，提出FedCoMuon-VR变体，以降低样本复杂度并提升收敛效率。

Result: 在非i.i.d.和非凸设置下，FedCoMuon-VR获得O(ε⁻³)的样本复杂度，优于现有FedMuon算法。在鲁棒联邦学习和任务分布式风险敏感元学习实验中，所提方法在多个设置下达到最佳报告精度。

Conclusion: FedCoMuon及FedCoMuon-VR有效解决分布式矩阵逐元素组合优化问题，在理论上保证更优收敛性，在实验中展现出与现有组合基线相当甚至更优的性能。

Abstract: Muon, a more recently developed optimizer, is useful for matrix-wise models in AI areas. Although many works have studied Muon and its variants, these methods are still not particularly well-suited for hierarchical structured problems. To fill this gap, we propose an effective federated compositional Muon (FedCoMuon) optimizer to solve distributed matrix-wise compositional optimization problems. Specifically, our FedCoMuon optimizer builds on compositional gradient tracking and orthogonalized momentum. Moreover, we propose a variance reduced variant of FedCoMuon (FedCoMuon-VR) based on a momentum-based variance reduced technique. In theory, we analyze the convergence properties of our algorithms under the non-i.i.d. and non-convex settings. In particular, we prove that our FedCoMuon-VR obtains a lower sample complexity of $O(ε^{-3})$ for finding an $ε$-stationary solution than the existing FedMuon algorithms. Extensive numerical experiments on robust federated learning and task-distributed risk-sensitive meta learning show that our proposed methods are competitive with existing compositional baselines and achieve the best reported accuracy in several settings.

</details>


### [112] [Sampling Luck Masquerades as Allocation Gain: Auditing Test-Time Budget Allocation for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13087)
*Jinhyung Bae*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文审视了神经组合优化（NCO）求解器中非均匀预算分配的价值。研究发现：在分布内工作负载上，基于同一样本计算和评估的oracle分配声称有2.2-2.6%的收益，但这些"幻影增益"在样本外测量时与零无显著差异；而在分布偏移（混合均匀与聚类实例）下，由样本外统计量引导的分配能带来11.5%-12.0%的真实增益（AM和SymNCO），而POMO作为负对照显示-0.3%。论文提供了校正程序、报告检查清单，并发布了全部数据、代码和预注册记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NCO求解器默认对每个实例分配相同的采样预算（best-of-k），但非均匀分配是否能在固定总预算下带来收益从未被严格测量。更重要的是，先前研究中常见的"样本内评估"程序可能存在显著偏差，需要对此审计。

Method: 作者对三个预训练求解器（POMO、AM、SymNCO）在uniform TSP-100及其他工作负载上进行实验。首先进行样本内oracle分配评估与样本外评估的对比，校准偏差；随后在分布偏移场景下进行预注册的确认性实验，使用样本外统计量引导分配，并设置冻结分布标签基线和负控制（POMO），同时探索性测试了计入20样本探针成本的策略。

Result: 样本内oracle评估在三个求解器上报告2.2-2.6%的增益（置信区间排除零），但样本外测量显示增益与零不可区分（0.457%, 0.015%, -0.512%）。在分布偏移下，预注册实验发现AM增益11.5%（95% CI [7.4, 19.7]）、SymNCO增益12.0%，超过冻结标签基线4.2个百分点；POMO负对照为-0.3%。计入20样本探针成本后，AM和SymNCO仍分别保留3.4%和4.6%的增益。

Conclusion: 非均匀预算分配在分布内工作负载上没有可检测的收益，论文报告了样本内评估造成的幻影增益偏差（该偏差不随样本量或实例数增加而缩小）；但在分布偏移下，基于样本外统计量的分配策略能带来显著且可复现的真实增益。作者给出了校正程序和报告检查清单，并公开了全部数据、代码和预注册记录。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) solvers report the best of many sampled solutions per instance, and the sample count is, by convention, identical for every instance. Whether a non-uniform allocation of a fixed total budget would buy anything has not been measured. We measure it, and we audit the measurement itself.
  First, on in-distribution workloads the allocation headroom is not detectable. Across three pretrained solvers (POMO, AM, SymNCO) on uniform TSP-100, an oracle allocation computed and evaluated on the same stored samples reports a 2.2-2.6% gain with intervals excluding zero; measured out of sample the same gain is indistinguishable from zero (0.457, 0.015, -0.512 percent). Following the customary in-sample procedure, all three solvers would have supported a published 2%-level gain that does not exist. We calibrate this bias against an instance-wise null in which the true gain is zero by construction; over the ranges we test it does not shrink with more samples or more instances.
  Second, the same correction that removes the phantom gains preserves a real one.
