Under distribution shift (a workload mixing uniform and clustered instances), a pre-registered confirmatory experiment finds that allocation guided by held-out sample statistics improves best-of-k by 11.5% (AM, primary endpoint; 95% CI [7.4, 19.7]) and 12.0% (SymNCO, replication) at equal evaluation budget, with the signal-acquisition cost not charged; a pre-registered negative control (POMO, an order of magnitude more robust to shift) shows -0.3% [-0.7, 0.24]. The gain exceeds a frozen distribution-label baseline by 4.2 points [1.9, 7.7]. An exploratory policy charging a 20-sample probe against the same budget retains 3.4% (AM) and 4.6% (SymNCO).
  We give a correction procedure and a reporting checklist, and release all data, code, and the pre-registration record.

</details>


### [113] [Demand Transfer Estimation at Scale via Restricted Logit Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12680)
*Lakshya Garg,Deep Narayan Mishra,Swapnil Yadav,Haoan Wang,Sujal Alugubelli,Karthik Kumaran,Anupriya Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在大规模商品集合（超过100万件商品）上高效计算需求转移（Demand Transfer, DT）系数的方法，用于品类级的需求预测。该方法结合了独立需求预测的效率和基于商品间替代关系的调整，避免了传统方法需要为每个可能的商品组合单独预测需求的低效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于客户选择模型的品类优化方法需要为每个可能的商品组合单独进行需求预测，在商品数量庞大、类别众多的情况下效率极低。因此需要一种既能独立高效预测需求，又能考虑商品间替代关系的替代方案。

Method: 引入需求转移（DT）系数的概念，该系数表示当目标商品（顾客进店意图购买的商品）从货架移除时，其需求被重定向到其他商品的百分比。作者提出了一种能够在拥有100万以上商品的大规模商品集合上计算这些DT系数的算法。

Result: 在真实数据以及多个地点的历史交易数据上的实验表明，当替代行为的某些合理假设成立时，所提出的方法能够准确估计潜在的DT系数，从而改善需求预测的准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法在不牺牲预测精度的前提下，显著提升了大规模商品集合上需求预测的计算效率，为大规模品类优化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Item demand forecasting is an integral component of store assortment optimization. Existing literature focuses on learning a suitable customer choice model and using this model to determine the value of an objective function (i.e. expected demand) with respect to an assortment proposal. However, for large item universe with many categories, this approach can prove inefficient, needing a separate demand forecast for every possible item assortment. An alternate approach exists whereby we combine the efficiency of forecasting item demand independently, while at the same time applying adjustments to the independent forecasts that account for the relations between item demand and the availability of other similar items on the shelf.
  Central to this approach is the estimation of Demand Transfer (DT) coefficients. These DT coefficients represent the percent of a particular target item's (item that the customer walked in the store to buy) demand that is redirected to each other item in the universe should the target item be removed from the shelf. We introduce an approach that allows us to compute these DT coefficients on large item universes (assortments having 1 million+ items). Experiments on data as well as historical transaction data for multiple locations within categories demonstrate that when certain reasonable assumptions about substitution behavior are satisfied, our procedure is able to accurately estimate underlying DT coefficients and lead to improvements in demand forecasting.

</details>


### [114] [Finding the Needle in a Haystack: Test-Time Analog Circuit Representation Adaptation for Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12687)
*Fin Amin,Sounak Dutta,Paul D. Franzon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTARO是一个在线深度核贝叶斯优化框架，通过在搜索过程中利用已有的FoM标签持续适配电路表示（包括非线性特征变换和高斯过程代理），使搜索空间与优化目标对齐，相比固定表示BO和DKL显著降低regret AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表示基BO方法在编码器训练后将电路嵌入视为固定，导致表示学习与优化目标不匹配：嵌入学习旨在编码或重构电路结构，而非根据正在优化的FoM（figure of merit）进行组织，从而影响优化效率。

Method: TTARO从预训练的电路嵌入出发，在搜索过程中每次评估新候选后，联合学习一个非线性特征变换和高斯过程代理（利用已评估电路的FoM标签），并在选择下一个候选前更新表示和代理，实现表示与优化目标的持续对齐。

Result: 在40种编码器/核/采集函数设置下，TTARO相比固定嵌入BO平均降低regret AUC 15.2%，相比DKL降低20.7%，多数设置下超越现有最优方法，最大降幅达46.7%。

Conclusion: 通过持续将新观测的FoM标签纳入表示学习，TTARO使搜索空间随BO进程不断与优化目标对齐，验证了在线表示适配在模拟电路拓扑搜索中的有效性。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is a sample-efficient framework for analog circuit topology search, where evaluating each candidate topology can require costly simulation. However, representation-based BO methods typically treat circuit embeddings as fixed after encoder training. This creates a mismatch between representation learning and optimization: embeddings learned to encode or reconstruct circuit structure are not necessarily organized according to the figure of merit (FoM) being optimized. This paper introduces Test-Time Analog Representation Adaptation for Bayesian Optimization (TTARO), an online deep-kernel BO framework that adapts circuit representations throughout the search process. Starting from pretrained circuit embeddings, TTARO jointly learns a nonlinear feature transformation and a Gaussian-process surrogate using the FoM labels of the circuits evaluated so far. Following each new evaluation, TTARO updates the representation and surrogate before selecting the next candidate. We compare TTARO with conventional Gaussian Process-based BO over fixed embeddings and with Deep Kernel Learning (DKL), which learns the representation only from the initial evaluated designs and keeps it fixed throughout the remainder of the search. By continually incorporating newly observed FoM labels into representation learning, TTARO aligns the search space with the optimization objective as BO progresses. In our experiments, TTARO reduces regret AUC by 15.2% on average relative to BO and by 20.7% relative to DKL across 40 encoder/kernel/acquisition settings, outperforming prior art in most settings with reductions as large as 46.7%.

</details>


### [115] [The Impact of Temporal Context Length and Encoding Strategies on Self-Supervised ECG Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12695)
*Ahmed Sameh,Ramzi Al-Sharawi,Yogatheesan Varatharajah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在Icentia11k单导联心电数据集上系统研究了自监督心电模型的两个关键设计选择：输入时间跨度（16秒至10分钟）和前段表征方式（连续卷积patch嵌入 vs 离散向量量化token）。结果发现更长的时间上下文带来更强的迁移性能和检索准确率，且连续patch嵌入始终优于离散token，表明量化可能丢失临床相关的波形细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督心电模型通常只使用几秒的心电信号，且越来越多地采用离散化token序列进行训练，但这些选择是否牺牲了真实世界动态心电记录中节律推断和纵向一致性所需的信息尚不清楚。作者希望通过受控实验揭示这些设计选择对下游任务的影响。

Method: 在Icentia11k单导联数据集上进行受控对照研究，系统地改变两个因素：(i) 输入时间跨度（16秒、1分钟、5分钟、10分钟）；(ii) 前段表征方式（连续卷积patch嵌入 vs 固定向量量化token）。保持Transformer主干网络和训练协议不变，通过下游异常节律检测和患者级别的检索任务评估表征质量。

Result: 将时间上下文从16秒延长至更长时间可增强迁移性能和检索准确率，其中5分钟和10分钟模型表现最佳，表明其更好地捕获了缓慢变化的节律动力学和个体特异性结构。在所有评估的时间跨度下，连续patch嵌入均优于离散token，显示量化可能丢弃临床相关的波形信息。

Conclusion: 研究结果为心电基础模型的设计提供了重要指导：应强调扩展的时间上下文和连续的编码器，以更好地支持临床预测和基于相似性的检索应用。

Abstract: Self-supervised electrocardiogram (ECG) models are often trained on a few seconds of ECG signal and, increasingly, on discretized token sequences. It remains unclear whether these choices sacrifice information needed for rhythm inference and longitudinal consistency in real-world ambulatory recordings. We present a controlled study on the Icentia11k single-lead dataset that varies (i) the input horizon (16 seconds, 1 minute, 5 minutes, and 10 minutes) and (ii) the front-end representation (continuous convolutional patch embeddings vs. fixed vector-quantized tokens), while holding the Transformer backbone and training protocol constant. Representations are assessed by downstream abnormal rhythm detection and by patient-level retrieval that probes cross-session stability. Our results show that increasing temporal context beyond 16-second snapshots yields stronger transfer and higher retrieval accuracy, with the strongest performance achieved by the 5- and 10-minute models, indicating improved capture of slow-varying rhythm dynamics and individual-specific structure. Across all evaluated horizons, continuous patch embeddings outperform discretized tokens, suggesting that quantization can discard clinically relevant waveform detail. These findings motivate ECG foundation models that emphasize extended context and continuous encoders for clinical prediction and similarity-based applications. Our code and pretrained models are publicly available at https://github.com/muha-0/ecg-ssl-representation-learning.

</details>


### [116] [A Contract-Grade Verifier for LLM-Generated GPU Kernels, and a Native Blackwell Backward for the Gated-Linear-Recurrence Family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12700)
*Rishi Shah,Rishav Shrestha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出基于语言模型的GPU内核生成系统"高正确率"实则来自松散测试（固定形状、少量随机输入、阈值宽松），大量内核虽然通过测试但仍存在隐蔽错误。作者构建了一个包含十二项对抗性检查的"合同级"验证器（其中多项无容差），用其审计2,638个已被公开系统接受的机器生成内核，发现39.5%存在实质错误、62.1%至少有一项违规；该验证器拒绝了标准测试接受的1,487个内核（反向仅14个）。作者用四种独立方式验证了这一发现，并将验证器用于验证自己开发的Blackwell tcgen05 GDN反向训练内核，证明其正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前GPU内核生成系统报告的高正确率建立在极宽松的测试之上：仅用固定形状的少量随机输入、阈值容差来判断内核是否"正确"。这种测试无法捕捉NaN/Inf错误、运行时不确定性、形状变化导致的崩溃、以及浮点精度累积差异等隐蔽缺陷。作者希望构建一个严格、无容差依赖的正确性验证器，揭示真实情况并纠正领域内的错误认知。

Method: 作者设计了包含十二个对抗性门控（adversarial gates）的"合同级"验证器，每个门控对应正确内核必须满足的属性，多项为无容差（tolerance-free）检查，杜绝通过调整阈值掩盖失败。验证面向外部：审计2,638个已被公开系统自身harness接受的内核；验证面向内部：对自研的Blackwell tcgen05 GDN训练反向内核进行独立验证（对照双精度oracle）。并提供四种防御性验证手段：7/7阳性对照、阈值校准扫描、与参考基准自身正确性代码98.5%的一致性、以及分层人工审计。

Result: 审计发现39.5%的机器生成内核"超越任何容差论证"地存在错误，62.1%至少有一项违规。标准测试接受了1,487个被验证器拒绝的内核，而反向不一致仅14个（即标准测试几乎没有比验证器更严格的时候）。作者自研的Blackwell tcgen05原生训练反向内核通过了独立验证，包括此前只能使用fallback实现的反向状态阶段（reverse-state），并用该内核成功训练了五个GDN家族成员。

Conclusion: 内核生成领域报告的高正确率信号远弱于数字所暗示的。松散测试的"通过"并不等于"正确"。一套无容差的合同级验证器（tolerance-free contracts）就能缩小绝大部分认知差距。作者同时证明，即使在Blackwell tcgen05这样的前沿硬件上，也可以通过严格的验证流程产出真正正确的高性能训练内核。

Abstract: Systems that generate GPU kernels with language models report high correctness rates. Those rates come from a single loose test: run the kernel on a few random inputs at one fixed shape and accept it if the output is close to a reference. A kernel can pass that test and still be silently wrong. It can return an ordinary number where the true answer is a NaN or an infinity, differ from run to run, break when the shape changes, or accumulate in fp16 where the reference keeps an fp32 total. We build the instrument that checks correctness properly: a contract-grade verifier of twelve adversarial gates, each a property a correct kernel must satisfy, several of them tolerance-free, so no choice of threshold can explain a failure away. Aimed outward, the verifier audits 2,638 machine-generated kernels that a public system's own harness had already accepted as correct. It finds 39.5% broken beyond any tolerance argument and 62.1% carrying at least one violation. The field's standard test accepts 1,487 kernels the verifier rejects, against only 14 the other way. We defend the finding four independent ways: a 7/7 positive control, a threshold-calibration sweep, 98.5% agreement with the reference benchmark's own correctness code, and a stratified hand-audit. Aimed inward, the verifier judges a kernel of our own: the first native Blackwell tcgen05 training backward for the gated-linear-recurrence (GDN) family, including the reverse-state stage the field still runs on a fallback. We establish its correctness independently, against a double-precision oracle, and train five family members through it. The correctness signal behind reported progress in kernel generation is far weaker than the numbers suggest, and a set of tolerance-free contracts would close most of the gap.

</details>


### [117] [Perturbation-based Regional Interpretability through Subtraction Mapping (PRISM): naming-error dissociations in language models and post-stroke aphasia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12717)
*Xiang Guan,Roger D. Newman-Norlund,Yong Yang,Saeed Ahmadi,Regan Willis,Nadra Salman,Kalil Warren,Srihari Nelakuditi,Chris Rorden,Leonardo Bonilha,Julius Fridriksson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，将人类神经影像学中的减法分析（subtraction analysis）适配到大型语言模型的扰动变换器中，通过逐层扰动和成对误差分类相减，以"种子"作为被试进行组分析，实现在模型内部的空间解析式、可证伪的功能特化检验。研究者将同一逻辑平行应用于213名慢性卒中后失语症患者的脑损伤数据，在双侧都复现了稳健的语音偏好（phonemic-favoring）解离、深层聚类与前额-外侧裂皮层聚类，而语义偏好方向在两侧均呈一致符号但非显著的趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的机制可解释性缺乏空间解析、可证伪的工具来检验内部组件是否专门化于不同的认知操作。传统方法难以像神经科学验证大脑功能分离那样验证模型内部功能特化，因此需要将成熟的神经影像学分析范式迁移到变换器模型中。

Method: 基于Brain-LLM Unified Model（BLUM）的先前工作，开发PRISM框架：将费城命名测验（PNT）的七个临床类别进行成对误差类别相减，将每个扰动种子视为组分析中的一名被试，沿层轴使用无阈值聚类增强（TFCE）处理。对照侧采用213名慢性卒中后失语症患者的相关性差异病变-症状映射（correlation-difference lesion-symptom mapping）。两侧设计在被试维度（种子vs患者）、空间维度（层vs图谱分区皮层）和阈值化上匹配，但对比算子不同：LLM采用被试内误差比例差，皮层采用被试间相关差。

Result: 两个基质（LLM与皮层）均恢复出稳健的语音偏好解离、深层聚类（深层层聚类）和额叶-外侧裂皮层聚类，且两侧均能在保留的拆分数据上复现。语义偏好方向在两侧均表现为一致符号但非显著的统计趋势。验证性ROI级干预（PRISM第三阶段）留待后续工作。

Conclusion: PRISM为变换器语言模型的功能特化主张提供了一种可证伪、空间解析的检验工具，并通过平行的人类脑损伤分析验证了该框架的有效性。该结果表明模型与生物大脑在认知功能组织上存在可比较的解离模式，为跨物种类的机制可解释性铺平了道路。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability of large language models lacks spatially resolved, falsifiable tools for testing whether internal components are specialized for distinct cognitive operations. We adapt subtraction analysis, the standard framework of human neuroimaging, from biological brains to perturbed transformers, and apply the same logic to both substrates in parallel. Building on the Brain-LLM Unified Model (BLUM), which showed that layer-perturbed LLaVA-1.6-Vicuna-13B error profiles match the lesion patterns of aphasic patients, we develop PRISM (Perturbation-based Regional Interpretability through Subtraction Mapping). PRISM maps the seven clinical Philadelphia Naming Test categories, subtracts error classes pairwise, and treats each perturbation seed as a subject in a group analysis with threshold-free cluster enhancement along the layer axis. We run a structurally matched analysis on 213 chronic post-stroke aphasia patients using correlation-difference lesion-symptom mapping, and replicate both sides on held-out splits. The designs match in subject dimension (seeds, patients), spatial dimension (layers, atlas-parcellated cortex) and thresholding, but the contrast operator differs: a within-subject error-proportion difference for the LLM, a between-subject correlation difference for the cortex. Both substrates recover a robust phonemic-favoring dissociation, a deep layer cluster and a frontal-perisylvian cortical cluster, both replicating; the semantic-favoring direction is a consistently signed but non-significant trend on both. PRISM thus gives a falsifiable, spatially resolved test of functional-specialization claims in transformer language models. A confirmatory ROI-level intervention (PRISM Stage 3) licensing the strongest causal-mechanism claim is left to subsequent work.

</details>


### [118] [MAG: MAnifold Guided Semi-Supervised Multi-modal In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12724)
*Zirui Cheng,Xun Xu,Tiankai Chen,Fady Rezk,Bowen Zheng,Xiaodong Shi,Shijie Li,Kangkang Lu,Bharadwaj Veeravalli,Nancy F. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MAG框架，一种利用未标注多模态数据来改进少样本情境学习（ICL）演示选择的半监督方法。MAG将演示选择建模为多模态图上的半监督传播问题，采用两阶段策略：先通过相关性分数传播筛选高影响力的未标注样本进行伪标注，再利用多模态相关性选择最终演示。实验表明MAG在八个多模态基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的少样本情境学习（ICL）性能高度依赖于所选演示样本的质量和覆盖范围。虽然未标注的多模态数据丰富，但如何有效利用这些数据来改进ICL仍然是一个未解决的问题。

Method: MAG框架将演示选择建模为多模态图上的半监督传播问题，采用两阶段策略：(i) 通过相关性分数传播识别一组紧凑的高影响力未标注样本进行伪标注，降低MLLM推理成本；(ii) 利用多模态相关性选择最终演示。研究发现文本表示更适合相关性传播，而视觉和文本模态对于高质量的演示选择都至关重要。

Result: 在八个多模态基准上的实验表明，MAG在标签稀缺的场景下始终优于强基线，在有限的伪标注预算下取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: MAG证明了一种高效的半监督演示选择方法可以有效利用未标注多模态数据来改进MLLM的少样本ICL性能，其中文本表示用于传播、多模态特征用于最终选择是一种有效且经济的策略。

Abstract: Few-shot in-context learning (ICL) with multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) enables task adaptation without parameter updates, but its performance is highly sensitive to the quality and coverage of the selected demonstrations. While unlabeled multi-modal data is abundant, it remains elusive how to exploit them for ICL. We propose MAG (MAnifold-Guided semi-supervised in-context demonstra- tion selection), an efficient framework that leverages unlabeled data to improve multi-modal ICL. MAG formulates demonstration selection as a semi-supervised propagation problem on a multi-modal graph and adopts a two-stage strategy: (i) relevance score propagation identifies a compact set of high-impact unlabeled samples for pseudo-labeling, reducing MLLM inference cost; (ii) multi-modal relevance is used to select the final demonstrations. We show that textual represen- tations are more effective for relevance propagation, while both visual and textual modalities are crucial for high-quality demonstration selection. Experiments on eight multi-modal benchmarks demonstrate that MAG consistently outperforms strong baselines in label-scarce regimes, achieving significant gains with a limited pseudo-labeling budget.

</details>


### [119] [A Cloud-Edge System for Multimodal Clinical Screening in Resource-Constrained Rural Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12745)
*Hei Ting,Chan,Chenwei Wu,Xueshen Liu,Zesen Zhao,Boyuan Zheng,Luis Filipe Nakayama,Michael G. Morley,Liyue Shen,Jiasi Chen,Z. Morley Mao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种云-边缘协同架构,将轻量级边缘模型将原始多模态医疗数据转化为紧凑结构化输出,由云端LLM总结生成临床报告。在20个多模态临床案例、三种模拟网络条件下,该混合系统达到98-99%工具召回率、92-96%精确率,临床准确率与云端基线持平或更优,延迟不受带宽影响(25-35秒),token成本降低4-15倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI虽已展现专家级诊断能力,但在带宽稀缺、计算受限、需要多模态融合的农村资源受限环境中难以部署。现有方案难以在低资源条件下实现高效的多模态临床决策支持,需要架构层面的创新来解决这一矛盾。

Method: 采用云-边缘协同架构:(1)边缘端部署轻量级领域专用模型,将原始多模态医疗数据转换为紧凑的结构化输出;(2)云端大语言模型综合这些输出生成临床摘要;(3)基于LLM的编排器根据患者上下文动态选择诊断工具,实现全面模态覆盖同时避免处理无关输入。在20个涵盖心脏、产科、创伤和筛查场景的临床案例上,于500kbps-5Mbps三种模拟网络条件下评估。

Result: 混合系统实现98-99%的诊断工具召回率和92-96%的精确率;临床准确率与仅云端基线持平或更优;在带宽变化下保持延迟不变(25-35秒);token成本降低4-15倍;相比仅云端方案,在部署约束下改善了事实依据(事实基础)。

Conclusion: 架构设计在实现高效多模态集成方面起关键作用,云-边缘协同范式能在资源受限的农村环境中提供高准确率、低延迟、低成本的临床决策支持,同时比仅云端方案具有更好的事实依据能力。

Abstract: Medical AI has demonstrated specialist-level diagnostic accuracy, yet these capabilities remain largely inaccessible in resource-constrained rural settings where bandwidth is scarce, compute is limited, and clinical decision-making requires integrating heterogeneous modalities. We introduce a cloud--edge collaborative architecture that addresses these constraints: lightweight, domain-specific models on the edge transform raw medical data into compact structured outputs, while a cloud LLM synthesizes these outputs into clinical summaries. An LLM-based orchestrator dynamically selects diagnostic tools based on patient context, promoting comprehensive modality coverage without processing irrelevant inputs. We evaluate on 20 multimodal clinical cases spanning cardiac, obstetric, trauma, and screening scenarios under three simulated network profiles (500,kbps--5,Mbps). The hybrid system achieves 98--99% diagnostic tool recall with 92--96% precision, matches or exceeds cloud-only baselines on clinical accuracy, and maintains bandwidth-invariant latency (25--35,s) at 4--15x lower token cost. These results highlight the role of architectural design in enabling efficient multimodal integration and improving factual grounding compared to cloud-only approaches under deployment constraints.

</details>


### [120] [Decentralized Multi-Player Q-Learning in Episodic Markov Decision Processes with Information Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12753)
*Larissa Xu,King Bi,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种信息不对称下的事件性表格马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）去中心化多智能体强化学习问题。对于问题A（未观测动作+共享奖励）和B（可观测动作+独立奖励），提出mQ-learning和mQ-learning-intervals算法，实现Õ(√(H⁴ S A_joint T))的遗憾界；对于问题C（未观测动作+独立奖励），提出mEXC和mEXC-Bellman两阶段探索-利用算法，实现Õ(H(S A_joint)^{1/3} T^{2/3})的遗憾界。在集中式联合动作基准下，去中心化学习在信息不对称条件下匹配单智能体Q-learning的速率（至多对数因子差异）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 去中心化多智能体强化学习中，智能体无法通信但可预先约定协议，且面临不同类型的信息不对称（动作不可观测、奖励独立等）。需要在这些约束下设计算法，并评估其与集中式最优策略的差距。

Method: 针对问题A和B提出mQ-learning和mQ-learning-intervals，采用Q-learning框架在去中心化协议下估计联合动作价值；针对问题C提出mEXC和mEXC-Bellman，采用两阶段探索-利用（explore-then-commit）策略，先探索环境再基于估计提交策略。

Result: 问题A和B达到Õ(√(H⁴ S A_joint T))的遗憾界；问题C达到Õ(H(S A_joint)^{1/3} T^{2/3})的遗憾界。这些界与集中式单智能体Q-learning的速率匹配（至多对数因子），由于A_joint随M指数增长，结果在小M或小动作集场景下最有意义。

Conclusion: 去中心化多智能体强化学习在信息不对称条件下的遗憾界可与集中式基准相匹配，证明了即使智能体无法通信、存在信息不对称，通过预先协议设计仍能达到接近集中式学习的性能。但联合动作空间随智能体数量指数增长，限制了其在大规模智能体场景下的适用性。

Abstract: We study decentralized multi-player reinforcement learning in episodic tabular Markov decision processes (MDPs) under three forms of information asymmetry: (A) unobserved actions with common rewards, (B) observed actions with independent rewards, and (C) unobserved actions with independent rewards. Players cannot communicate during learning but may agree on a protocol a priori. For Problems A and B we propose \texttt{mQ-learning} and \texttt{mQ-learning-intervals}, achieving $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{H^4 S A_{\text{joint}}\, T})$ regret, where $H$ is the horizon, $S$ the state count, $T = KH$ the total steps, and $A_{\text{joint}} = \prod_{i=1}^M |\mathcal{A}_i|$ the joint action space across $M$ players. For Problem C we give \texttt{mEXC} and \texttt{mEXC-Bellman}, two-phase explore-then-commit algorithms with regret $\tilde{O}(H (S A_{\text{joint}})^{1/3} T^{2/3})$. Against the centralized joint-action benchmark, decentralized learning under information asymmetry matches the single-agent Q-learning rate of \cite{jin2018q} up to logarithmic factors. Because $A_{\text{joint}}$ grows exponentially in $M$, the bounds are most meaningful for small $M$ or small per-player action sets.

</details>


### [121] [Beyond Outcome Rewards: Step-Level Self-Distilled Policy Optimization for Deep Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12764)
*Haoze Wu,Chuqiao Kuang,Tianyi Zhuang,Xiaoguang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对深度搜索智能体轨迹长、奖励稀疏的问题，本文提出SSPO方法：通过从网络提取Evidence Anchors作为步骤级特权信息，并设计步骤级自蒸馏策略优化，将教师-学生分歧转化为GRPO中的步骤级优势权重，且仅作用于错误轨迹，从而在不增加过多计算开销的情况下显著提升搜索智能体的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索智能体的轨迹跨越数十步，但标准强化学习仅提供每轨迹单一的结果奖励，过于稀疏，难以有效进行信用分配。虽然OPSD（on-policy self-distillation）通过模型自身logits作为密集的token级教师信号来缓解此问题，但将其扩展到搜索智能体时存在根本矛盾：教师拥有正确答案等特权信息，其输出分布与学生的探索式推理系统性地不同，朴素蒸馏会让学生继承信息不对称而非学到更好的搜索策略。

Method: 本文提出两个核心贡献：1) Evidence Anchors—从网络提取的简洁步骤级证据片段，作为特权信息，捕捉关键推理步骤但不泄露完整答案路径；2) Step-Level Self-Distilled Policy Optimization (SSPO)—将教师-学生分歧转化为GRPO中的步骤级优势权重，仅应用于错误轨迹。该设计解耦了"更新什么"与"更新多少"：结果奖励决定策略变化方向，教师调制每步的变化幅度。正确轨迹不被修改，保留其多样性。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，SSPO在BrowseComp、GAIA和FRAMES三个基准上始终优于GRPO，达到或超过使用两倍梯度步数训练的GRPO的性能，而每步仅增加约5%的计算开销（来源于一次额外的正向传播）。

Conclusion: SSPO通过步骤级的证据锚点与自蒸馏优势权重，有效解决了搜索智能体中稀疏奖励和教师-学生信息不对称的问题，在不显著增加计算成本的前提下提升了搜索策略的学习效率与最终性能。

Abstract: Deep search agents operate over trajectories spanning dozens of steps, yet standard reinforcement learning provides only a single outcome reward per trajectory, which is far too sparse for effective credit assignment. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) addresses this by using the model's own logits as dense token-level teachers, but extending it to search agents introduces a fundamental tension: the teacher, having access to privileged information such as the correct answer, produces a distribution that differs systematically from the student's exploration-based reasoning, and naive distillation causes the student to inherit this information asymmetry rather than learn better search strategies. We resolve this tension through two contributions. First, we construct Evidence Anchors, which are concise, step-level evidence snippets extracted from the web, as privileged information that captures key reasoning steps without revealing the entire answer path. Second, we propose Step-Level Self-Distilled Policy Optimization (SSPO), which converts teacher-student disagreement into step-level advantage weights within GRPO, applied exclusively to incorrect trajectories. This design decouples what to update from how much to update: the outcome reward determines the direction of policy change, while the teacher modulates its magnitude at each step. Correct trajectories are left untouched, preserving their diversity. On Qwen3-8B, SSPO consistently outperforms GRPO across BrowseComp, GAIA, and FRAMES, surpassing or matching GRPO trained with twice as many gradient steps while adding only about 5 percent overhead per step from a single additional forward pass.

</details>


### [122] [CoMedBench: A Multi-Source Benchmark of Synthetic Medical Data Fidelity and Downstream Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12805)
*Akanta Das,Al Amin Farhad,Mrinmoy Sarkar Anto,David Rehkopf,Ayin Vala,Tanmoy Sarkar Pias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMedBench，一个可复现的基准测试框架，用于系统评估合成临床数据在不同生成器、数据集和下游任务上的效用。它在37个数据集-任务对上评估统计保真度与任务效用，发现表格型任务中合成数据保留大部分信号（参考生成器AUROC效用90.6%，最强生成器97.3%），而时序ICU任务更具挑战性且对生成器高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床数据直接使用受隐私法规、机构审查和数据使用协议限制，合成数据被视为可行替代方案。然而既有研究往往只评估单一生成器、单一数据集或狭窄下游任务，难以判断合成数据何时能支持模型开发、何时会丢失任务关键信号，缺乏统一、可复现的评估框架。

Method: 构建CoMedBench基准，在统一的临床有效性框架和共享的训练/评估引擎下，跨静态表格与时序两类模态评估一组生成器。覆盖20个静态表格和17个时序ICU任务共37个数据集-任务对，数据来自MIMIC-III、MIMIC-IV、eICU及UCI、BRFSS、NHANES、pycox等七个公开来源。评估同时关注统计保真度和任务效用，通过比较在真实与合成数据上训练并测试的模型性能来衡量（合成/真实性能比）。

Result: 表格任务上合成训练数据保留大部分下游信号：CoMed-CTGAN平均AUROC效用达90.6%，最强生成器CoMed-TVAE达97.3%。时序ICU任务更难且对生成器更敏感：CoMed-CTGAN保留81.6%的AUROC，但在对不平衡敏感的AUPRC下仅保留64.0%，而CoMed-TVAE在AUROC上仍保留约95%。

Conclusion: 合成数据在表格型临床任务中可较好支持模型开发，但时序ICU任务中效用显著下降且高度依赖生成器选择，提示在该场景下需谨慎评估生成器与任务信号保真能力。

Abstract: Access to clinical data is essential for developing reliable healthcare machine learning systems, but direct use of electronic health records is constrained by privacy regulation, institutional review, data-use agreements, and the risk of re-identification. Synthetic data promises a practical alternative: it can preserve useful statistical and clinical structure while reducing exposure of sensitive patient records. Prior studies often evaluate a single generator, one dataset, or a narrow downstream task, making it difficult to know when synthetic data can support model development and when it fails to preserve task-critical signal. We introduce CoMedBench, a reproducible benchmark that evaluates a family of generators under a common clinical-validity framework and one shared training and evaluation engine, spanning static tabular and temporal downstream tasks on established critical-care datasets. In total the benchmark spans 37 dataset-task pairs across two modalities consists of 20 static tabular and 17 temporal ICU time-series-drawn from seven public data sources: three intensive-care databases (MIMIC-III, MIMIC-IV, and eICU) together with the UCI Machine Learning Repository, the CDC BRFSS diabetes cohort (2015), NHANES (1999-2014), and the pycox survival datasets (GBSG and METABRIC). The benchmark evaluates both statistical fidelity and task utility by comparing models trained and tested across real and synthetic data. In these settings, synthetic training data preserves most of the downstream signal: on tabular tasks the reference generator CoMed-CTGAN retains a mean AUROC utility (the synthetic-to-real performance ratio) of 90.6%, rising to 97.3% for the strongest generator, CoMed-TVAE. Temporal ICU tasks are harder and more generator-sensitive: CoMed-CTGAN retains 81.6% (AUROC) and only 64.0% under the imbalance-sensitive AUPRC, whereas CoMed-TVAE still retains ~95% (AUROC).

</details>


### [123] [HiRoute: Hierarchical Routed Prompt Tuning for Safety Alignment of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12821)
*Fangzhou Chen,Shiji Zhao,Mengyang Wang,Qihui Zhu,Ranjie Duan,Maoxun Yuan,Xingxing Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiRoute是一个输入自适应的层次化提示调优框架，用于大语言模型的安全对齐。它通过轻量级层次路由器检测有害意图并预测多标签风险，然后结合共享粗粒度提示和路由加权混合的风险特定提示专家，在推理时对良性输入绕过安全分支、对风险输入动态组合安全提示，从而在保持高安全率的同时减少过度拒绝并维护通用性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示调优的参数高效安全对齐方法依赖于单一全局提示或外部选择的提示模块，这种静态设计难以在跨类别的安全边界与针对特定风险生成建设性响应之间取得平衡，也容易对良性输入产生过度拒绝。作者希望解决这一局限性，实现既安全又不牺牲有用性的动态安全对齐。

Method: HiRoute包含三个核心步骤：1) 在冻结的LLM上训练一个轻量级层次路由器，用于联合检测有害意图并预测多标签风险分数；2) 冻结骨干模型和路由器，通过带交替梯度更新的偏好优化学习一个共享粗粒度提示和一组细粒度风险特定提示专家（作为连续嵌入）；3) 推理时，良性输入绕过安全分支，风险输入使用共享提示与路由器加权混合的风险特定提示专家进行处理。

Result: 在三个指令微调模型上的实验表明，HiRoute在多个安全基准上实现了高安全率，同时保持了安全响应的有用性，减少了过度拒绝，并在通用任务上保持了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: HiRoute通过将类别无关的安全控制与类别特定的响应引导相分离，证明了输入自适应的层次化提示调优是参数高效安全对齐的有效方案，能够在安全性与有用性之间实现更好的权衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to harmful requests and jailbreak attacks. Parameter-efficient safety alignment methods based on prompt tuning typically rely on a single global prompt or externally selected prompt modules. Such static designs struggle to maintain a cross-category safety boundary while generating constructive responses tailored to specific risks and avoiding over-refusal of benign inputs. To address these limitations, we propose HiRoute, an input-adaptive hierarchical prompt-tuning framework that separates category-agnostic safety control from category-specific response guidance. HiRoute first trains a lightweight hierarchical router on representations extracted from a frozen LLM to jointly detect harmful intent and predict multi-label risk scores. It then freezes both the backbone model and the router and uses preference optimization with alternating gradient updates to learn a shared coarse-grained prompt and a set of fine-grained prompt experts as continuous embeddings. At inference time, benign inputs bypass the safety branch, whereas risky inputs are processed using the shared prompt together with a router-weighted mixture of risk-specific prompt experts. Experiments across three instruction-tuned models show that HiRoute achieves high safety rates across multiple safety benchmarks while preserving safe-response helpfulness, reducing over-refusal, and maintaining competitive performance on general-purpose tasks.

</details>


### [124] [Fast A/B/n Testing: Exact Multi-Policy Comparison via Tree-Coupled Feedback Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12831)
*Yuxiao Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Tree-Coupled A/B Testing（TCAB），一种精确的反馈共享设计，用于任意基于历史的自适应上下文bandit策略的对比测试。通过可预测的树连接各策略历史，最大化耦合父子上下文-动作律，并在匹配树边内共享奖励，使每个策略保留其独立的有限时域轨迹分布。奖励查询次数满足路径恒等式N(T)=T+ΣD_{e,t}，对固定J可实现次线性伪遗憾和N(T)=T+o(T)的期望代价（对比无关联运行的JT）。实验表明在成本-精度前沿上显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线平台需要比较多种自适应决策策略（排序系统、推荐算法、定价规则、语言模型agent），但每次奖励交互可能昂贵或有风险。传统A/B/n设计为每个策略分配独立的horizon-T轨迹，总共需要JT次结果，成本过高。需要一种更高效的反馈共享机制来降低评估成本。

Method: TCAB在每个回合通过一棵可预测的树连接当前各策略的历史；对每条父子边进行最大化耦合（maximally coupled），并在匹配树边的同一组件内共享奖励。策略之间虽被刻意制造依赖，但每个策略仍精确保持其独立运行的有限时域轨迹分布。每次树边不匹配时记录D_{e,t}，奖励查询数满足路径恒等式N(T)=T+ΣD_{e,t}。对固定J，次线性伪遗憾与oracle动作几乎必然唯一意味着E[N(T)]=T+o(T)。同时提供成对策略对比的有限样本方差界。

Result: TCAB的设计在给定树上条件最优（精确edge-local设计），当前回合最小生成树在树设计中是遗忘最优的。对于固定J，在次线性伪遗憾和oracle动作几乎必然唯一条件下，期望奖励查询数为T+o(T)而非独立运行的JT。在奖励模型评估、多选语言模型评估和自适应搜索策略等实验中，显著提升了成本-精度前沿。

Conclusion: TCAB提出了一种新颖的、理论完备的反馈共享A/B测试框架，在保留每个策略独立轨迹分布的同时大幅降低交互成本，为大规模在线自适应策略对比提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Online platforms increasingly compare many adaptive decision policies---ranking systems, recommendation algorithms, pricing rules, and language-model agents---while each reward-bearing interaction can be costly or risky. A direct A/B/n design gives each of $J$ policies its own horizon-$T$ trajectory and therefore uses $JT$ outcomes. We introduce Tree-Coupled A/B Testing (\TCAB), an exact feedback-sharing design for arbitrary history-dependent contextual-bandit policies. At each round, a predictable tree connects the current policy histories; every parent--child context--action law is maximally coupled, and one reward is shared within each component of matched tree edges. Every policy retains exactly its standalone finite-horizon trajectory law, even though the policies are deliberately dependent. If $D_{e,t}$ records a mismatch on tree edge $e$ at round $t$, the number of reward queries satisfies the pathwise identity $N(T)=T+\sum_{t,e}D_{e,t}$ and hence equals $T$ plus cumulative tree-edge total variation in expectation. This cost is conditionally optimal among exact edge-local designs on the selected tree, and a current-round minimum-spanning tree is myopically optimal among tree designs. For fixed $J$, sublinear pseudo-regret of every policy and almost-sure uniqueness of the oracle action imply $\mathbb{E}[N(T)]=T+o(T)$, versus $JT$ for independent runs. We also obtain finite-sample variance bounds for pairwise policy contrasts. Experiments on reward-model evaluation, multiple-choice language-model evaluation, and adaptive search policies demonstrate substantial improvements in the cost--precision frontier.

</details>


### [125] [A Compositional Theory of Curvature in Probabilistic Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12869)
*Hrithik Suresh,Sahil Sidheekh,Shelar Parth Vijay,Yasir Z,Sriraam Natarajan,Narayanan Chatapuram Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对概率电路（PCs）中的锐度正则化问题进行理论分析，证明了每个求和节点对Hessian迹的贡献可以精确分解为电路流（节点使用频率）与局部锐度项的乘积，揭示了全局锐度正则化存在深度偏差并可能导致欠拟合的问题，进而提出了一种自适应锐度感知正则化方法，在保持EM闭式更新的同时恢复全局正则化所牺牲的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率电路（PCs）作为支持精确推理的生成模型，其损失曲面曲率（对数似然的Hessian迹）可以精确计算。近期工作通过全局正则化该迹来引导学习更平坦、泛化更好的最优点，但PCs的曲率本质上是组合式的，全局锐度正则化存在设定不匹配（misspecification）的问题，需要更细粒度的理论理解与针对性的正则化策略。

Method: 作者首先从理论上证明了每个求和节点对Hessian迹的贡献可以精确分解为两个因子的乘积：电路流（circuit flow，衡量节点被使用的频繁程度）和由节点输出分布决定的局部锐度项。基于这一分解，分析了全局锐度正则化的深度偏差问题。随后提出了一种自适应的锐度感知正则化方法，它根据节点固有的局部曲率进行针对性惩罚，并保持了EM更新的闭式形式。

Result: 实验结果显示，与全局正则化相比，这种针对性的正则化方法能够恢复全局正则化所牺牲的泛化性能，同时保留了锐度感知学习的鲁棒性和其他优势。

Conclusion: 全局锐度正则化在PCs中因曲率的组合式结构而存在深度偏差问题，可能导致欠拟合；基于Hessian迹的精确因子化分解所设计的自适应局部锐度正则化方法更为合适，能在保持泛化能力的同时保留锐度感知学习的优势。

Abstract: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) are generative models that support exact inference and, unlike deep neural networks, admit an exact and tractable measure of loss-surface curvature: the trace of the Hessian of the log-likelihood. Recent work regularizes this trace globally to bias learning toward flatter, better generalizing optima. We show that treating sharpness as a global regularizer can be misspecified for PCs, whose curvature is inherently compositional. We prove that each sum node's contribution to the Hessian trace factorizes exactly into its circuit flow, which measures how heavily the node is used, and a local sharpness term determined by its output distribution. This decomposition provides insights into why global sharpness regularization is depth biased and can lead to underfitting. Building on it, we introduce an adaptive sharpness aware regularizer that penalizes nodes based on intrinsic local curvature and preserves closed form EM updates. We also show that empirically, this targeted regularization recovers the generalization that global regularization sacrifices while retaining the robustness and benefits of sharpness aware learning.

</details>


### [126] [Sustaining Plasticity via Learnable Wavelet Activations in Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12874)
*Zeyang Zhang,Tieliang Gong,Junyan Lu,Weizhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可学习的小波激活函数，将激活分解为低频和高频分量以对抗谱偏差，并结合动态小波注入与正则化策略，在持续学习中同时提升可塑性与稳定性，理论上提供了严格的数学保证，实验上在多个持续学习基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中可塑性丧失严重阻碍顺序任务的获取。现有固定形式激活函数存在对低频变化的固有谱偏差，而可学习变体又允许无约束更新导致灾难性遗忘，亟需一种能同时兼顾可塑性与稳定性的激活函数设计。

Method: 提出一种可学习小波激活函数，将激活函数分解为低频和高频分量以显式对抗谱偏差；采用动态小波注入机制自适应增强新任务的可塑性；引入正则化策略保证先前知识稳定性。理论方面，证明了混合小波架构对高效L2逼近的结构必要性，展示了解耦学习率机制恢复高频信息网络可塑性的能力，并形式化推导了损失驱动注入触发机制。

Result: 大量实证评估表明，该方法在整个学习过程中保持优越的可训练性和泛化能力，并在多个持续学习基准上取得state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 通过小波分解激活函数同时解决频谱偏差导致的低可塑性与无约束更新导致的灾难性遗忘两大问题，结合动态注入与正则化策略，在理论与实验两个层面验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Plasticity loss has emerged as a critical challenge in continual learning that significantly hinders the acquisition of sequential tasks. While optimizing activation designs offers a potential solution, current fixed-form functions suffer from an inherent spectral bias towards low-frequency variations, whereas learnable variants permit unconstrained updates that induce catastrophic forgetting. To address these limitations, we propose a novel learnable wavelet activation that decomposes the activation function into low-frequency and high-frequency components to explicitly counter spectral bias. Furthermore, we employ dynamic wavelet injection to adaptively enhance plasticity for new tasks, alongside a regularization strategy to ensure the stability of previous learned knowledge. Theoretically, we provide rigorous mathematical guarantees for the proposed framework, proving the structural necessity of the hybrid wavelet architecture for efficient $L^2$ approximation and demonstrating that the decoupled learning rate mechanism successfully restores network plasticity for high-frequency information. Additionally, we provide a formal derivation of the loss-driven injection trigger mechanism to precisely guide the injection. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that our approach maintains superior trainability and generalization throughout the learning process and achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse continual learning benchmarks.

</details>


### [127] [Robust data-driven discovery of fractional differential equations via weak formulations and Pareto-based subset selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12879)
*Pongpisit Thanasutives,Yoshinobu Kawahara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Weak-Pareto方法，通过伴随一致的弱形式和Pareto子集选择，从含噪数据中稳健地发现分数阶偏微分方程，克服了分数阶微分放大高频噪声和导数阶数未知两大挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶偏微分方程描述了非局部动力学，但从含噪数据中发现它们非常困难，原因有二：一是分数阶微分会放大高频测量噪声，二是导数阶数是未知的。现有方法在含噪环境下难以稳健地恢复方程结构。

Method: Weak-Pareto结合了两个关键思想：(1) 对分数阶项采用伴随一致的弱形式，将分数阶算子从测量场转移到光滑测试函数上，用平滑积分替代对噪声敏感的逐点微分；对于非线性项，这种噪声抑制效果虽不彻底但仍有帮助。(2) 在离散项类型和连续分数阶上执行Pareto子集选择。系数通过岭回归拟合，并在分支感知的差分进化搜索中优化分数阶，最后在验证误差-复杂度拐点处选择支撑集大小。

Result: 理论分析表明，固定线性右端项弱特征的方差在网格细化下趋于零，而逐点分数阶特征的噪声放大随导数阶数增加。在分数阶对流-扩散、反应-扩散和Burgers基准上，Weak-Pareto能从干净和含噪测量中恢复简约结构。在受控对比中，算法在每个测试的乘性噪声水平下都保留正确的支撑集，而未正则化的强形式方法在引入噪声后基本失效；这一优势在加性高斯噪声下依然存在。消融实验证明弱库驱动噪声鲁棒性，连续阶Pareto搜索避免了密集固定字典的支撑选择失败。在对流-扩散基准上，Weak-Pareto比当代神经基线实现更一致的操作符恢复和大幅更低的实际运行时间。

Conclusion: Weak-Pareto为含噪环境下的分数阶偏微分方程发现提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案，其弱形式策略和连续阶Pareto搜索的组合在噪声鲁棒性、支持集恢复和计算效率方面均优于强形式基线和神经基线。

Abstract: Fractional partial differential equations describe nonlocal dynamics, but discovering them from noisy data is difficult because fractional differentiation amplifies high-frequency measurement noise and the derivative orders are unknown. We propose Weak-Pareto, which combines an adjoint-consistent weak formulation of fractional terms with Pareto-based subset selection over discrete term types and continuous fractional orders. For linear right-hand-side terms, the adjoint transfers fractional operators from measured fields to smooth test functions, replacing noise-sensitive pointwise differentiation with smoothing integration; for nonlinear terms, the noise-suppression effect is partial yet useful. Coefficients are fitted by ridge regression within a branch-aware differential-evolution search over the orders. The support size is then selected at the validation-error-complexity elbow. We show that the variance of fixed linear right-hand-side weak features vanishes under grid refinement, whereas noise amplification in pointwise fractional features increases with derivative order. Across fractional advection-diffusion, reaction-diffusion, and Burgers benchmarks, Weak-Pareto recovers parsimonious structures from clean and noisy measurements. In controlled advection-diffusion and Burgers comparisons, it retains the correct support at every tested multiplicative-noise level, whereas the unregularised strong-form counterpart largely fails once noise is introduced; this advantage persists under additive Gaussian noise. Ablations show that the weak library drives noise robustness and that continuous-order Pareto search avoids the support-selection failure of a dense fixed dictionary. On the advection-diffusion benchmark, Weak-Pareto yields more consistent operator recovery and substantially lower measured runtime than a contemporary neural baseline.

</details>


### [128] [Adaptive $k$ Nearest Neighbors Classifier via Granular Ball Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12903)
*Xiaoyu Lian,Shuyin Xia,Hongxuan He,Lifeng Shen,Guoyin Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于粒球计算的自适应高效KNN方法（Adaptive-GBKNN），通过两个阶段实现：训练阶段利用Fisher准则进行粒球的粗划分与多粒度表示，预测阶段通过加权距离定位最近粒球并动态确定自适应邻域和有效k值。实验表明该方法在准确率和效率上均优于现有KNN变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统KNN算法的k值选择对性能影响显著，但现有方法往往难以自适应地确定最优k值，导致在复杂数据分布下鲁棒性和效率不足。

Method: 该方法包含两个阶段：（1）训练阶段：先用粗粒度划分降低粒球内数据分布的复杂度，再引入Fisher准则控制粒球的划分与停止，生成多粒度粒球表示；（2）预测阶段：通过加权距离机制定位最近粒球，并在测试样本周围构建自适应邻域，将邻域内实际样本数作为动态有效k值。由最近粒球诱导的邻域提供了更稳定的局部组信息，增强了对噪声和局部扰动的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验结果表明，所提出的方法在准确率和效率两方面均优于现有的KNN变体方法。

Conclusion: 基于粒球计算的自适应KNN方法解决了k值选择问题，通过多粒度粒球表示和自适应邻域构建实现了更鲁棒、高效的分类性能，且代码已开源以供复现。

Abstract: The $k$-Nearest Neighbor~(KNN) algorithm is widely used across various tasks. The selection of the $k$ value is a key issue because it significantly impacts performance. In this paper, an adaptive and efficient KNN approach via granular-ball computing is proposed. The method consists of two stages. \textcolor{black}{In the training stage, the dataset is first coarsely partitioned to reduce the complexity of data distributions within a granular ball, and then the Fisher criterion is introduced to control ball splitting and stopping, yielding a multi-granularity granular ball representation. In the prediction stage, the nearest granular ball is first located through a weighted distance mechanism, and an adaptive neighborhood is then constructed around the test sample. The effective $k$ value is dynamically determined by the actual number of samples contained in this neighborhood. The neighborhood induced by the nearest granular ball provides more stable local group information, thereby improving robustness against noise and local perturbations.} Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms existing KNN variants across multiple datasets in terms of both accuracy and efficiency. The code has been open-sourced for reproducibility: https://github.com/lianxiaoyu724/Adaptive-GBKNN.

</details>


### [129] [EGRL: Edge generation-guided relation-aware learning for RNA-protein interaction prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12906)
*Danyu Li,Ling Zhou,Rubing Huang,Xian Zhong,Bin Zou,Kui Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EGRL框架，用于RNA-蛋白质相互作用预测。该框架通过隐式元路径学习、多关系感知注意力机制、图生成器（预测软边以支持冷启动节点）和多特征融合预测器，在四个基准数据集上取得了具有竞争力的整体性能，并在冷启动（未知分子）场景下表现优异（AUROC提升8.6%，AUPR提升5.0%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统湿实验检测RNA-蛋白质相互作用成本高且耗时，深度学习提供了高效的计算替代方案。虽然图神经网络天然适合建模RPI网络，但现有GNN方法往往依赖同质图或预定义元路径，限制了其对数据稀疏性的处理能力，且难以泛化到涉及未知分子的冷启动场景。为此需要一种能够自动学习关系语义并支持冷启动预测的新方法。

Method: EGRL包含四个关键组件：(1) 隐式元路径学习，无需手工设计路径即可捕捉关系语义；(2) 多关系感知注意力机制，自适应融合交互模式；(3) 图生成器，预测潜在软边以支持冷启动节点；(4) 多特征融合预测器，用于最终交互评分。模型通过主任务损失和辅助生成器损失联合训练。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上，EGRL取得了具有竞争力的整体性能。在冷启动（未知分子）场景下表现尤为突出，AUROC达0.867，AUPR达0.861，较先前最优方法分别提升了8.6%（AUROC）和5.0%（AUPR）。

Conclusion: EGRL通过隐式元路径学习和图生成机制，有效解决了现有GNN方法在处理数据稀疏性和冷启动场景时的局限性，显著提升了RNA-蛋白质相互作用预测中对未知分子的泛化能力，验证了其作为计算工具的实用价值。

Abstract: RNA-Protein Interactions (RPIs) are critical for regulating cellular functions. While traditional wet-lab experiments for RPI detection are costly and time-consuming, Deep Learning (DL) methods provide an efficient computational alternative for RPI Prediction (RPIP). In particular, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) are promising, as they naturally model RPI networks. However, existing GNN-based methods often rely on homogeneous graphs or predefined meta-paths, which limit their ability to handle data sparsity and to generalize to cold-start scenarios involving unknown molecules. To address these limitations, we propose Edge Generation-guided Relation-aware Learning (EGRL), a novel framework with several key components: implicit meta-path learning to capture relational semantics without handcrafted paths; a multi-relation-aware attention mechanism for adaptive fusion of interaction patterns; a graph generator that predicts potential ("soft") edges to support cold-start nodes; and a multi-feature fusion predictor for final interaction scoring. EGRL is jointly trained with a primary task loss and an auxiliary generator loss. Comprehensive evaluations on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that EGRL achieves competitive overall performance. More importantly, it exhibits superior generalization in cold-start settings, achieving an Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic curve (AUROC) of 0.867 and an Area Under the Precision-Recall curve (AUPR) of 0.861 on unknown molecules, corresponding to improvements of 8.6% in AUROC and 5.0% in AUPR over prior state-of-the-art methods. The code will be released soon.

</details>


### [130] [Revisiting Overestimation Bias Problem of Q-learning: Settling Large Discrete Action Space via Action Intersection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12912)
*Pu Li,Tao Tan,Hong Xie,Xiaoyu Shi,Mingsheng Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了动作交集（action intersection）策略，通过让两个Q函数共享部分轨迹数据，在耦合与解耦两种范式之间建立半解耦机制，从而同时缓解大规模动作空间下Q-learning的过估计和欠估计偏差问题。该策略支持在欠估计到过估计之间进行精细的偏差控制，在深度强化学习和表格实验中均取得了优于SOTA基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模动作空间场景下，Q-learning的过估计偏差问题更为严重。现有两大范式各有瓶颈：耦合范式（用同一个Q函数估计最优动作及其Q值）因随机性总是产生正偏差（过估计），解耦范式（用两个独立Q函数分别估计）则总是产生负偏差（欠估计）。作者希望找到一种简单而有效的策略来缓解这两类偏差。

Method: 提出动作交集（action intersection）策略，实现半解耦设计：(1) 允许两个Q函数共享一定比例的轨迹数据；(2) 共享的数据样本采用耦合范式更新Q函数，非共享数据则采用解耦范式更新。通过调节数据共享比例和动作交集大小，实现偏差的连续控制和精细调节。

Result: 在深度强化学习实验中，该方法大幅优于多个SOTA基线；表格实验揭示了方法取得优越性能的内在原因。验证了动作交集策略具有两个关键特性：可获得从欠估计到过估计的大偏差范围，以及可任意细化交集大小以实现精细控制。

Conclusion: 动作交集是一种简单而强大的策略，通过半解耦机制在大规模动作空间中有效缓解Q-learning的偏差问题，兼具大偏差调节范围和精细控制粒度，为现有方法提供了重要的性能突破。

Abstract: This paper considers the overestimation bias problem of Q-learning in the setting of a large action space, for the purpose of relieving the bottleneck of existing methods. We find that the large action space increases the randomness in Q-value estimation. The randomness makes two paradigms that drive the major literature on the overestimation problem have their own bottlenecks: the coupling paradigm, i.e., the optimal action and its Q-value are estimated with the same Q-function, always has a positive bias. This is because randomness leads to some actions having abnormally high estimated values than their true values, and the coupling methods prefer these actions. The decoupling paradigm, i.e., the optimal action and its Q-value are estimated with two independent Q-functions, always has a negative bias. This is because randomness increases the estimation gap between the two independent Q-tables for the same action. This paper shows that action intersection can be a simple yet powerful strategy to relieve these bottlenecks. The action intersection strategy enables semi-decoupling via two designs: (1) it allows two Q-functions to share a certain fraction of trajectory data; (2) if a data sample is shared, each Q-function is updated using the coupling paradigm; otherwise, using the decoupling paradigm. Two properties make the action intersection strategy powerful: (1) attaining a large bias range, i.e., varying the data sharing fraction, the estimation bias varies from underestimating to overestimating; (2) fine granularity: the action intersection size can be made arbitrarily finer to enable finer control. We consider two experiment settings, i.e., tabular and deep RL, deep RL experiments show that our method outperforms several SOTA baselines drastically; tabular experiments reveal why our method can achieve superior performance.

</details>


### [131] [Towards Socially Compliant Navigation in Deep Reinforcement Learning via Proxemics-Based Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12917)
*Takieddine Soualhi,Jacques Saraydaryan,Laetitia Matignon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于就近学（proxemics）理论的稠密奖励函数，用于深度强化学习（DRL）社会导航，通过将人类个人空间建模为径向高斯混合场，在保持导航效率的同时提升社交合规性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于DRL的机器人导航方法在拥挤环境中主要关注任务导向目标（如到达效率），而忽视社会合规目标（如保持适当人际距离），导致机器人与人交互时缺乏社交意识。作者希望引入一种既能提供稠密、可解释的学习信号，又不牺牲导航效率的社交奖励机制。

Method: 1) 基于Hall的就近学理论，将每个人的个人空间建模为径向高斯混合场；2) 计算机器人视野范围内的局部代价（cost）；3) 将该代价整合为DRL导航方法的稠密奖励；4) 在多种人群场景、奖励基线和人群密度下进行仿真实验，同时使用导航指标和社会指标进行评估。

Result: 在仿真实验中，与对比的奖励模型相比，所提出的奖励方法一致性地改善了社会性指标，同时保持了具有竞争力的导航性能。

Conclusion: 提出的就近学奖励公式可以在不牺牲导航效率的前提下，有效提升DRL社交导航中的社交合规性，为社会导航提供了一种稠密、可解释的奖励设计范式。

Abstract: Developing effective robot navigation methods in crowded environments is essential for real-world applications. Although recent deep reinforcement learning (DRL) methods have improved navigation performance in crowded environments, they often focus primarily on task-centric objectives and underrepresent social compliance objectives. In this paper, we introduce a novel proxemics-based reward formulation for DRL social navigation that provides a dense, interpretable social learning signal while maintaining navigation efficiency. Our approach models each human's personal space as a radial Gaussian-mixture field derived from Hall's proxemics theory and computes a robot-centric local cost over the robot's field of view. We integrate the proposed reward into established DRL navigation methods and evaluate it in simulation across multiple crowd scenarios, reward baselines, and crowd densities using both navigation metrics and social metrics. Results show that the proposed reward consistently improves social metrics in simulation while maintaining competitive navigation performance relative to the compared reward models.

</details>


### [132] [Momentum as Residual-Driven Multiplier Correction for Deep Learning Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12925)
*Zhixin Ren,Yau Lyu,Congrong Li,Liping Zhang,Shengbo Eben Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AIM框架，从ADMM式残差惩罚变量分裂的角度解释动量优化器，并基于此提出RADAR优化器，融合相对论自适应几何、解耦残差校正与二阶动量滤波，在视觉、语言和强化学习任务上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管动量优化器在深度学习中被广泛使用，但动量递归、更新几何与加速之间的内在关系仍未被充分理解。作者希望从ADMM的视角统一解释这些看似独立的概念，揭示动量本质上是基于分裂残差的乘子式修正。

Method: 提出AIM框架，基于残差惩罚变量分裂将动量解释为ADMM风格乘子更新（等价于梯度指数移动平均），并区分两个纠缠机制：残差惩罚决定更新几何，目标相关子问题的近似决定加速形式。在此基础上提出RADAR优化器，结合相对论自适应几何、解耦残差校正与二阶动量滤波，并通过方差扰动Lyapunov漂移分析建立随机收敛性。

Result: 在监督视觉学习、语言建模和强化学习任务上，RADAR相对于强自适应优化基线的性能提升保持一致。

Conclusion: AIM框架为动量优化器提供了统一的理论视角，RADAR通过分离几何与加速机制、改进动量估计，在实际任务中展现了优于现有自适应优化器的表现。

Abstract: Momentum-based optimizers are widely used in modern deep learning, yet the relations among momentum recursion, update geometry, and acceleration remain only partially understood. We develop an $\textbf{A}$DMM-$\textbf{I}$nspired $\textbf{M}$omentum (AIM) framework based on residual-penalty variable splitting, which interprets momentum as a multiplier-like correction driven by the splitting residual. AIM recovers the exponential moving average of gradients from an ADMM-style multiplier update and separates two mechanisms that are usually intertwined in practical optimizers: the residual penalty determines the update geometry, whereas the approximation of the objective-related subproblem determines the acceleration form. Building on AIM, we propose $\textbf{R}$elativistic $\textbf{A}$daptive gradient $\textbf{D}$escent with $\textbf{A}$ccelerated $\textbf{R}$esidual (RADAR), which combines relativistic adaptive geometry, decoupled residual correction, and second-order momentum filtering to improve the update direction and momentum estimation. We establish stochastic convergence through a variance-perturbed Lyapunov drift analysis. Experiments on supervised vision learning, language modeling, and reinforcement learning show that RADAR achieves consistent improvements over strong adaptive optimizer baselines.

</details>


### [133] [H-VAEP and H-xT: Valuing Offensive On-the-Ball Actions in Handball by Estimating Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12926)
*Julius Broermann,Oliver Müller,Michael Döring,Jochen Baumeister*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将足球领域中基于事件的行动估值框架xT（期望威胁）和VAEP（基于概率估计的行动估值）系统性地适配到手球领域，并利用德国手球甲级联赛五个赛季的追踪事件数据，提出了Handball-xT（H-xT）和Handball-VAEP（H-VAEP）。H-xT采用手球原生球场分区布局，在模拟中比标准矩形网格更稳健；H-VAEP通过定制特征空间和选择上下文长度来避免球队身份泄露。评估显示H-VAEP能产生稳定、有区分度且直观的球员评分，突出构建进攻的价值，并开源了全部代码供职业俱乐部部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统手球球员评估依赖基础框式统计或启发式指标，无法体现多人进攻构建链条的价值。尽管足球分析领域已广泛采用xT和VAEP等事件级行动估值框架，但这些方法尚未被适配到手球运动中。作者旨在填补这一空白，为手球提供更精细、更能捕捉比赛构建环节的球员评估工具。

Method: 基于德国手球甲级联赛（Handball Bundesliga）五个赛季的追踪衍生事件数据，作者首先将xT框架适配到手球场景（H-xT），核心创新在于采用手球原生球场区域划分布局而非标准矩形网格，并通过仿真验证其稳健性更优。其次，作者对VAEP框架进行定制化适配（H-VAEP），通过针对手球运动的特征工程设计并选择恰当的上下文长度，以限制模型中学到的球队身份泄露（team-identity leakage）。

Result: 仿真结果表明H-xT在稳健性上系统性优于标准矩形网格分区方案。评估显示H-VAEP生成的球员评分极其稳定、具有高区分度且解释性强，能够突出体现球员在进攻构建（build-up）阶段的价值。作者同时发布了完整代码仓库供职业俱乐部使用。

Conclusion: 该工作证明xT和VAEP这两个足球行动估值框架可以成功迁移到手球领域，为手球球员评估提供了超越传统框式统计的更精细、更有效的工具。研究成果开源，有望直接推动职业手球俱乐部在球员分析和决策中的实际部署。

Abstract: Traditional player evaluation in professional handball relies on basic box-score metrics or heuristic indices, which fail to credit the multi-player build-up chain. While football (soccer) analytics has adopted Expected Threat (xT) and Valuing Actions by Estimating Probabilities (VAEP), these event-based action valuation frameworks have not yet been adapted to handball. In this paper, we present the first comprehensive adaptation and evaluation of xT and VAEP for handball, utilizing five seasons of tracking-derived event data from the Handball Bundesliga. We develop Handball-xT (H-xT) using a handball-native court zoning layout, demonstrating via simulations that it is systematically more robust than standard rectangular grids. We optimize Handball-VAEP (H-VAEP) by tailoring its feature space and selecting the context length to limit team-identity leakage. Our evaluation shows that H-VAEP yields exceptionally stable, discriminative, and intuitive player ratings that highlight build-up play. Finally, we release our complete code repository to help professional clubs deploy these models.

</details>


### [134] [Multi-perspective Imbalance-Conscious 6G Beamforming Optimization and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12929)
*Chukwunonso Henry Nwokoye,Blessing Oluchi Iloka,Chikwue V. Umeugoji,Christopher Anene Egemba,Nnenna D. Duroha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性地研究了6G-IoT波束赋形优化（6GBO）问题，采用有监督和无监督机器学习方法，比较了网络、环境、设备和视觉四类特征组的预测能力，并进行了聚类分析和可解释性分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G-IoT场景下的波束赋形优化面临复杂多变的网络环境，传统优化方法难以应对。需要系统评估不同特征维度（网络、环境、设备、视觉）对波束赋形优化的预测能力，并探索无监督方法在场景聚类与理解中的辅助作用。

Method: 采用有监督（预测不同特征组的性能）和无监督（K-means、DBSCAN、层次聚类）两类方法；通过不平衡感知实验评估特征组预测能力（采用Recall、F1-score、ROC-AUC指标）；聚类质量通过Elbow法、Silhouette Score和Davies-Bouldin Index评估；并进行了全局特征重要性分析。

Result: 网络特征组的预测能力优于设备、环境和视觉特征组；聚类分析表明部署环境和设备类型是影响聚类的主要因素而非移动性属性；可解释性分析显示带宽、IoT传感器数量和移动性具有更高的全局特征重要性。

Conclusion: 网络特征是6GBO最优的预测特征来源，未来将引入深度学习和强化学习技术来预测吞吐量/时延或基于SNR增强等性能指标优化奖励函数。

Abstract: The study presents a systematic machine learning (ML) study of 6G-IoT beamforming optimization (6GBO) using supervised and unsupervised approaches. We compared the predictive power of network, environmental, device, and vision feature groups for 6GBO. Additionally, it addressed other unsupervised perspectives that can enhance 6GBO, including clustering network scenarios using methods such as K-means, DBSCAN, and hierarchical clustering. Several imbalance-aware experiments revealed that network features possess better prediction power than device, environmental, and vision feature groups, as evidenced by their recall, F1-score and ROC-AUC values. For unsupervised ML exploration (assessed using Elbow, Silhouette score, and Davies-Bouldin Index methods), the results indicate that the deployment environment and type of device primarily influence clustering, rather than mobility-based attributes. Furthermore, the explainability analysis showed that bandwidth, IoT sensors, and mobility possess higher global feature importance across the feature groups. In the future, we would apply deep and reinforcement learning techniques to predict throughput/latency or to optimize rewards determined by performance indicators like SNR enhancement

</details>


### [135] [Diagnosing JEPA World Models with Action-Conditioned Predictive Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12939)
*Guo An,Zijing Wu,Honghua Dong,Yuhao Yan,Zixuan Gui,Haochong Chen,Shanzhao Ruan,Xiang Wang,Yurong Ling,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出动作条件预测一致性（ACPC）诊断指标，用于衡量联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在视觉扰动下预测的鲁棒性。通过不变半径（IR）和分离率（SR）两个互补度量，证明ACPC能界定扰动导致的预测误差与规划代价变化，并在四个视觉控制任务及多种扰动下验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: JEPA在潜空间中预测世界模型，虽减少了像素级外观建模压力，但无法保证对视觉扰动的鲁棒性——扰动仍可能改变编码表示并影响后续动作条件预测。双模拟（bisimulation）思想要求：两个观测仅在动作条件后果一致时才应视为相同状态。基于此准则，需要一种能衡量并预测扰动影响的诊断方法。

Method: 提出ACPC诊断，量化干净历史与其视觉扰动视图在同一动作序列滚动后的发散程度。证明该发散量界定了扰动引发的多步预测误差和规划器代价变化。在成对ACPC基础上定义两个互补度量：不变半径（IR）概括干净-扰动滚动扩散程度；分离率（SR）检验不同状态滚动后是否仍可区分。在四个视觉控制任务上，对LeWM和PLDM两种架构进行实验验证。

Result: 实验表明成对ACPC能预测扰动引起的预测误差和代价变化。在LeWM上，IR-SR筛查可在任务间迁移，联合诊断在模糊和尺寸变换扰动下仍保持信息量。PLDM在不同架构下表现出类似的诊断趋势。

Conclusion: ACPC以及IR-SR提供了一套有理论保障的诊断框架，能够有效评估和预测JEPA类世界模型在视觉扰动下的表现，且诊断结果具有跨任务、跨扰动类型和跨架构的泛化能力，为JEPA模型的鲁棒性验证提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn world models that predict in a compact latent space rather than in pixels, reducing the pressure to model nuisance appearance. Yet this provides no guarantee against visual perturbations: they can still alter the encoded representation and affect subsequent action-conditioned predictions. Bisimulation captures this requirement precisely: two observations should be treated as the same state only when their action-conditioned consequences agree. Guided by this criterion, we introduce Action-Conditioned Predictive Consistency (ACPC), a diagnostic that measures how far a clean history and a visually perturbed view of it diverge after being rolled forward under the same action sequence. We prove that this divergence bounds the perturbation-induced change in multi-step prediction error and planner cost. Building on pairwise ACPC, we define two complementary measures: the Invariance Radius (IR) summarizes clean-perturbed rollout spread, while the Separation Rate (SR) checks whether different states remain distinguishable after rollout. Experiments on four visual control tasks show that pairwise ACPC predicts perturbation-induced prediction and cost changes. On LeWM, the IR-SR screen transfers across tasks, and the joint diagnostic remains informative under blur and resize. PLDM exhibits similar diagnostic trends under a different architecture.

</details>


### [136] [I-SDPO: Instance-Level Adaptive Self-Distillation Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12957)
*Yubo Zhang,Xinhong Ma,Zezhong Tan,Ziqiang Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出I-SDPO（实例级自适应自蒸馏策略优化），根据输入实例的成功情况动态决定是否使用特权自蒸馏或GRPO，解决GRPO在全部响应错误时无有效相对信号以及持续自蒸馏导致的教师偏见问题，在SciKnowEval上平均mean@16准确率从56.67%提升到70.31%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在采样组内所有响应均错误时无法获得有效相对奖励信号；而全程使用特权自蒸馏虽然能提供密集token监督，但教师是一个有偏的低方差奖励替代，在策略已能生成成功轨迹后，持续模仿会阻碍奖励提升的更新。

Method: I-SDPO按输入实例做一次路由决策并在该实例的采样组内共享：全错误组使用特权自蒸馏目标，有success的组保留GRPO。局部理论分析刻画了教师与奖励方向何时对齐，并证明非消失的有偏蒸馏权重会产生优化偏差底线；路由规则随成功率上升自动降低期望蒸馏率，无需手工设计调度。

Result: 在SciKnowEval上，I-SDPO在全部四个科学领域取得最佳结果，平均mean@16准确率从GRPO的56.67%提升至70.31%，最大领域增益为18.24个百分点。

Conclusion: I-SDPO通过将教师依赖作为能力依赖的变量，仅在组相对奖励不具信息量时使用模仿学习，实现了自适应的教师影响退出机制，有效结合了GRPO和自蒸馏的优势。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within a rollout group, but receives no useful relative signal when every sampled response is incorrect. Privileged self-distillation can fill this gap with dense token supervision, yet applying it throughout training creates a different failure mode: the teacher is a biased, low-variance surrogate for the reward objective, so persistent imitation can oppose reward-improving updates after the policy becomes capable of producing successful trajectories. We introduce I-SDPO (Instance-Level Adaptive Self-Distillation Policy Optimization), which treats teacher reliance as capability-dependent. I-SDPO makes one routing decision per input instance and shares it across that instance's rollout group: all-incorrect groups use a privileged self-distillation objective, whereas any-success groups remain intact for GRPO. This design uses imitation only where group-relative rewards are uninformative. A local analysis characterizes when teacher and reward directions align and shows that a non-vanishing biased distillation weight induces an optimization bias floor. The routing rule automatically reduces the expected distillation rate as success probability rises, withdrawing teacher influence without a hand-designed schedule. On SciKnowEval, I-SDPO obtains the best result in all four scientific domains and improves average mean@16 accuracy from 56.67% with GRPO to 70.31%, with a maximum domain gain of 18.24 points.

</details>


### [137] [The Objective Is the Bottleneck: Latent World Models Encode What Their Planners Cannot Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12959)
*Joyjeet Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过复现LeWorldModel在TwoRoom环境上的实验，证明潜伏世界模型规划在长时域上失败的根本原因不是预测器退化，而是规划器的目标函数（平方潜伏距离）存在缺陷。该目标与真实距离相关性弱、早熟饱和且非单调，导致远离目标反而可能降低代价。仅替换优化目标（无需重训或GPU），即可将offset 100的目标达成率从26.0%提升至98.0%，使规划不再受时域限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜伏世界模型通常以预测能力衡量其质量，因此当长期规划失败时，直觉归因于预测器退化。作者旨在质疑这一共识，验证真正瓶颈是否在规划器的目标函数而非预测器本身。

Method: 在TwoRoom环境上复现LeWorldModel，分别评估：1）预测器在75步后的误差（与实际冻结世界假设对比）；2）交叉熵方法规划所使用的平方潜伏距离目标与真实距离的相关性、饱和性与单调性；3）从冻结嵌入中通过岭探针恢复位置的能力；4)对比作者发布权重及四个检查点，验证病理是否普遍存在；5）仅替换目标函数（不重训、不用GPU），比较不同目标（包括从帧间隔学习的头）的规划性能。

Result: 预测器75步前的想象状态误差仅为冻结世界假设的0.189，且规划从未超过25步，预测器并非瓶颈。平方潜伏距离与真实距离的相关系数仅r=0.426，约80个竞技场单位即饱和、120个单位后下降，具有缺陷。岭探针可从冻结嵌入以R^2=0.9922恢复位置，信息完整。该病理在作者发布权重及四个检查点中一致存在：长时域成功率与度量质量正相关、与预测准确率反相关。仅替换目标后，offset 100达成率从26.0%升至98.0%，offset 25达到98.0%，三分之一预算下达92.0%。从帧分离学习的头预测空间距离不如位置探针（r=0.819对0.9897），但规划更好，穿越环境分界墙多计价24%（平方潜伏距离少计价4%），说明其学到的是可达性而非邻近性。

Conclusion: 长期规划失败的主要约束是规划目标函数的设计缺陷，而非世界模型预测器的退化。最优的代价度量不一定是误差最精确的度量——学到可达性（而非邻近性）的目标能带来显著更好的规划表现，且改进无需重新训练模型。

Abstract: Latent world models are judged by how well they predict, so when planning fails at long horizons the natural reading is that the predictor degrades. On a reproduction of LeWorldModel on TwoRoom we show the binding constraint is the planner's objective instead.
  The predictor is not the limit: its imagined state seventy-five environment steps ahead is still only 0.189 as wrong as assuming the world froze, while the planner never imagines beyond twenty-five. The objective is. Cross-entropy-method planning minimises squared latent distance, which tracks true distance at r = 0.426, saturates by about eighty arena units and decreases beyond a hundred and twenty, so moving away from the goal can lower the cost. The information is present throughout: a ridge probe recovers position from the frozen embedding at R^2 0.9922.
  The pathology is the method's, not one reimplementation's. It is present in the authors' released weights, and across four checkpoints long-horizon success rank-orders exactly with metric quality and inversely with prediction accuracy.
  Replacing only the objective, with nothing retrained and no GPU, lifts goals reached at offset 100 from 26.0% to 98.0%, equals the 98.0% at offset 25, and reaches 92.0% under a third of the budget: planning stops depending on the horizon. The best cost is not the most accurate. A head learned from frame separation alone predicts spatial distance worse than a position probe (r = 0.819 against 0.9897) yet plans better, charging 24% more to cross the environment's dividing wall where squared latent distance charges 4% less. It has learned reachability, not proximity.

</details>


### [138] [Understanding Backdoor Vulnerabilities in Vertical Federated Learning: The Gap Between Research and Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12962)
*Ziqi Zhao,Jialin Lu,Junjie Shan,Junyuan Zhang,Shuya Yang,Ka-Ho Chow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地揭示了纵向联邦学习（VFL）中后门攻击现有研究在现实条件下失效的根本性差距，重新定义了现实约束下的威胁模型，提出了实用的后门攻击流程，并构建了以VFL后门为中心的基准测试平台BVBench，配备了最先进的基线方法，为该领域的公平、实用和全面评估奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纵向联邦学习中的不对称信息结构（发起方保有任务信息，参与方保有私有数据）使得后门攻击既能在训练阶段投毒又能在推理阶段激活。虽然已有研究声称达到近乎100%的攻击成功率并提出了有效防御，但这些发现在现实条件下大多不成立，暴露了研究与实际之间的根本性差距。现有方法忽视了关键的实际约束，依赖不切实际的先验知识，且这些问题因文献中糟糕的评估实践而被掩盖。

Method: 本文采用系统性、面向实践的研究方法：首先揭示现有后门攻击与防御在方法论设计和评估实践两方面的缺陷；其次在现实约束下重新定义威胁模型；然后提出实用的后门攻击工作流；最终构建BVBench基准测试平台，集成最先进的基线方法，实现公平、实用、全面的评估。

Result: BVBench提供了强有力的证据，表明当前对VFL后门风险的理解是脆弱的。现有研究中的后门攻击和防御方法在现实条件下大多失效，证明了此前报道的攻击成功率和防御有效性存在严重夸大。

Conclusion: 当前VFL后门研究缺乏现实性和公平性，BVBench的建立为引导研究走向发现实际漏洞和开发更有意义的防御方案奠定了重要基础，填补了研究与实际应用之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Vertical Federated Learning (VFL) enables organizations holding complementary features of shared entities to collaborate and train models. In this setting, the initiator can withhold information about the learning task, while other contributors participate without exposing their local datasets, creating an asymmetric information structure aligned with growing privacy demands. However, this asymmetry is a double-edged sword. Among various threats, backdoor attacks are particularly concerning because VFL not only enables malicious contributors to poison the model during training, but also allows them to activate the backdoor at inference time to manipulate predictions. Although prior work has reported near-perfect attack success rates and proposed effective defenses, we find that most findings fail to hold under realistic conditions, exposing a fundamental gap between research and practice. In this paper, we present a systematic, practice-oriented study of backdoor vulnerabilities in VFL, revealing this gap in both methodological design and evaluation practices. We show that existing approaches overlook key practical constraints and therefore rely on unrealistic prior knowledge. Furthermore, these limitations have remained hidden due to poorly designed evaluation practices in the literature. To bridge this gap, we redefine threat models under realistic constraints, propose practical backdoor workflows, and introduce BVBench, a backdoor-centric benchmark that enables fair, practical, and comprehensive evaluation, preloaded with state-of-the-art baselines. BVBench provides strong evidence of the fragility of the current understanding of VFL backdoor risks and establishes a foundation for steering research toward uncovering practical vulnerabilities and developing more meaningful defenses.

</details>


### [139] [Comment on "Modeling rapid language learning by distilling Bayesian priors into artificial neural networks"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12974)
*Orr Well,Idan Tarshish,Nur Lan,Roni Katzir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文批判性审视了McCoy & Griffiths (2025)提出的"通过MAML将贝叶斯先验蒸馏进人工神经网络"的主张。作者指出，M&G的程序在标准先验解释下并未真正灌输先验，只是有利地初始化了网络权重；在更宽松的解释下也面临重大挑战；且实验表明M&G的模型过拟合、泛化能力差，远不如真正的贝叶斯学习器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对McCoy & Griffiths (2025)提出的通过MAML将贝叶斯先验蒸馏进ANN的强主张，作者希望检验这一说法在理论和实证层面是否真正成立，澄清"先验"的正确解释，并评估该方法的实际学习效果。

Method: 作者从理论分析入手，区分了两种先验解释：(1)标准解释——先验应体现在目标函数中，而M&G仅初始化权重、不改目标函数，故未真正灌输先验；(2)更宽松解释——系统整体可视为实现贝叶斯学习器，作者指出该解释面临非平凡的挑战。随后通过实证对比，评估MAML对贝叶斯学习经验结果的逼近程度，检验模型的泛化能力。

Result: 发现M&G的程序在标准解释下并未真正灌输贝叶斯先验；在更宽松的解释下也存在根本性挑战；实证结果显示，与真正的贝叶斯学习器不同，M&G的MAML模型出现过拟合，对未见数据的泛化表现较差，尽管在形式上具有一定的语言学习能力。

Conclusion: M&G关于"MAML可蒸馏贝叶斯先验"的核心主张缺乏充分的理论与实证支撑。先验并非仅通过权重初始化即可传递，MAML模型的泛化能力也远不及真正的贝叶斯学习器，因此该主张需要被谨慎看待或修正。

Abstract: McCoy & Griffiths (2025, henceforth M&G) suggest that a Bayesian prior can be distilled into Artificial Neural Networks (ANNs) through Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (MAML, Finn et al., 2017). They support this empirically by showing that meta-trained networks demonstrate formal language learning abilities comparable to Yang & Piantadosi (2023)'s Bayesian learner, significantly outperforming standard ANNs. We point out that under the standard interpretation of a prior, M&G's procedure does not actually instill one; it merely initializes network weights favorably, leaving the objective function unchanged. We then consider a more permissive interpretation, where the system as a whole can be seen as implementing a Bayesian learner even without an explicit prior in the objective. We show that this interpretation faces nontrivial challenges. Finally, we assess how well MAML approximates the empirical results of Bayesian learning, showing that unlike genuine Bayesian learners, M&G's model overfits and generalizes poorly to unseen data.

</details>


### [140] [Learning the Mathematical Property for Designing Low Mutual Coherence Binary Sensing Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12982)
*Rekha,Santosh Singh,S. K. Neogy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的基于学习的传感矩阵构造方法，该方法无需训练数据集和特定应用场景，仅利用数学性质（互相关性）作为神经网络的损失函数来构造具有低互相关的二值传感矩阵，实现了信号完美恢复，同时大幅降低计算成本和存储需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩感知中传感矩阵的构造是核心问题，传统方法依赖RIP、NSP、SP等性质，但这些均为NP难问题，计算上极具挑战。现有学习式方法需要大规模数据集且针对特定应用，缺乏普适性。因此需要一种既高效又具通用性的传感矩阵构造方案。

Method: 采用神经网络框架构造传感矩阵，核心创新在于：(1) 不使用任何数据集或特定应用；(2) 利用数学性质（低互相关性）直接定义损失函数；(3) 构造二值传感矩阵（条目通过共享底层规则生成）以实现低互相关性，从而满足完美信号恢复条件。

Result: 所提架构简单，无需大规模训练数据，首次在文献中利用数学性质定义损失函数。该方法大幅降低计算成本，并首次将互相关性作为损失函数嵌入神经网络框架，带来泛化性、鲁棒性以及存储需求的显著减少。

Conclusion: 通过与传统NP难的RIP/NSP/SP性质约束方法对比，该基于互相关数学性质的无数据、无特定应用的神经网络方法为传感矩阵构造提供了更高效、更通用且存储友好的新途径，有望推动压缩感知在实际应用中的广泛部署。

Abstract: In this research work, we are constructing the sensing matrix, which is essential for the success of the compressive sensing technique. We have chosen a learning-based technique for the construction of the sensing matrix. The novelty and uniqueness of the proposed technique is that it does not use any data set and also does not use a specific application. It uses the mathematical property/constraint for the construction of the sensing matrix for the perfect recovery of the signal. The perfect recovery of signals is an old and still very challenging problem in real-world applications. In late 2000, compressive sensing became a popular mathematical tool for the perfect recovery of sparse signals. The core of the compressive technique is the construction of the sensing matrix, which satisfies certain special properties such as restricted isometry property (RIP), null space property (NSP), and spark property (SP). All these properties are NP-hard problems and hence computationally challenging to solve. For all practical purposes, the construction of the sensing matrix needs to achieve low mutual coherence to achieve the perfect recovery of the signals. We have used a neural network for the construction of the sensing matrix, and this framework constructs a binary sensing matrix with low mutual coherence. The entries in the matrix are generated through a shared underlying rule. The proposed architecture is simple and does not use large-scale training data sets. Such uniqueness and novelty bring a drastic reduction in computational cost, and also, for the first time in literature, the use of a mathematical property for defining the loss function. In this proposed research work, the mutual coherence property has been used in the neural network framework. Such a neural network framework brings generality, robustness, and reduces storage requirements.

</details>


### [141] [Balanced Adaptive Prototype Selection for Scalable TabPFN Inference on Large-Scale Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12989)
*Mahboobe Jadid,Melika Rezaye Garkani,Ali Mousavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BAPS（平衡自适应原型选择）框架，通过在不修改预训练模型的情况下构建紧凑且信息保持的上下文，将TabPFN扩展到百万级表格数据集。在HIGGS和SUSY数据集上，512个原型即可保持强预测性能和可靠校准，实现约1953倍的上下文压缩，且仅需CPU即可运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练表格基础模型（如TabPFN）虽有强大预测能力，但其推理上下文长度有限，难以直接应用于大规模数据集。现有方法缺乏在不重训模型的前提下构建紧凑且信息丰富的上下文的有效手段。

Method: 提出BAPS框架，联合优化五个维度以选择代表性原型：代表性结构、信息性决策边界、局部密度、类别平衡和特征空间多样性。BAPS不对预训练模型做任何修改或重训，仅通过精心选择的数据原型构建紧凑上下文。

Result: 在百万行HIGGS和SUSY数据集上，512个原型保持强预测性能和可靠校准，实现约1953倍的上下文压缩；实验在Intel Core i7 CPU（16GB RAM）上完成，无需GPU加速。

Conclusion: 有效构建上下文是扩展预训练表格基础模型到百万级数据集的实际可行机制，为大规模表格数据推理提供了低成本、无需重训的解决方案。

Abstract: Pretrained tabular foundation models have demonstrated strong predictive capability; however, their application to large-scale datasets remains constrained by the limited inference context. This paper introduces Balanced Adaptive Prototype Selection (BAPS), a framework for constructing compact, information-preserving contexts for scalable TabPFN inference. Without modifying or retraining the pretrained model, BAPS jointly preserves representative structure, informative decision boundaries, local density, class balance, and feature-space diversity. Experiments on the million-row HIGGS and SUSY datasets show that 512 prototypes retain strong predictive performance and reliable calibration, corresponding to an approximately 1,953-fold context compression. All experiments were conducted on an Intel Core i7 CPU with 16 GB RAM and no GPU acceleration. These findings establish effective context construction as a practical mechanism for extending pretrained tabular foundation models to million-scale datasets.

</details>


### [142] [Incremental Evaluation and Training in Relational Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13023)
*Jakub Peleška,Gustav Šír*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种增量式、多回合的评估与训练范式，用于评估和提升关系深度学习（RDL）模型在时序数据演变下的鲁棒性与适应性，并证明增量微调训练优于从头训练的标准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RDL评估依赖于静态、单回合的数据集快照，忽视了真实数据库随时间持续演变的特性，导致现有基准无法捕捉模型性能在新数据不断累积过程中的变化，进而无法衡量模型的时序鲁棒性与适应性。

Method: 提出增量式、多回合（multi-episode）的评估与训练范式；利用大规模公开数据集研究数据演变与模型训练动态；设计多种增量微调（incremental fine-tuning）训练机制；提出一种优先考虑近期预测准确率的新的时序评估指标。

Result: 在多数预测任务中观察到时序概念漂移（temporal concept drift）现象；所提出的增量微调模型在时序评估指标下始终优于从头训练（from-scratch）的昂贵标准基线。

Conclusion: 迁移学习在RDL场景中是可行且高效的；增量微调训练是应对数据库时序演变的更优策略，能够以更低成本获得更好的近未来预测性能。

Abstract: Relational Deep Learning (RDL) models multi-tabular databases as temporal heterogeneous graphs to enable end-to-end representation learning. However, prevailing RDL evaluation practices rely on static, single-episode dataset snapshots, overlooking the continuous, time-evolving nature of real-world databases. Consequently, current RDL benchmarks fail to capture how model performance changes as new data accumulates over time. To address this limitation, we introduce an incremental, multi-episode evaluation and training paradigm to assess and improve the temporal robustness and adaptability of state-of-the-art RDL models. Using established large-scale datasets, we examine data evolution and model training dynamics, demonstrating that temporal concept drifts occur in the majority of predictive tasks. We present multiple incremental training regimes for fine-tuning the models and demonstrate that transfer learning is both feasible and highly effective in the RDL setting. Alongside a new temporal evaluation metric that prioritizes near-future accuracy, we show that our incrementally fine-tuned models consistently outperform the standard, expensive, from-scratch trained baselines.

</details>


### [143] [On the global feature importance for interpretable and trustworthy heat demand forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13039)
*Milan Zdravković*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"事前可解释AI"（ante-hoc XAI）方法论，用于评估区域供热系统智能控制中热需求预测机器学习模型的全局特征重要性。该方法使用梯度提升的固有可解释性以及偏依赖图（PDP）、累积局部效应（ALE）和SHAP三种事后方法，且这些方法均不依赖特征排列或扰动，避免了引入不真实数据实例带来的偏差。结果讨论了各方法的互补性及在区域供热过程背景下的具体解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是提升供热需求预测机器学习模型的可解释性和可信度，从而应对社区标准合规、客户满意度和责任风险等挑战。

Method: 采用四种方法评估全局特征重要性：梯度提升的固有可解释性，以及偏依赖（PDP）、累积局部效应（ALE）和SHAP三种事后方法。所有方法均避免使用特征排列或扰动，以防止随机不真实数据实例引入偏差。

Result: 提供了结果讨论，包括各方法在可行处的互补性评估，并结合区域供热工艺背景给出了具体解释。

Conclusion: 该ante-hoc XAI框架可有效提升区域供热热需求预测模型的可解释性和可信度，助力智能控制决策的透明化与责任归属。

Abstract: The paper introduces the ante-hoc Explainable AI methodology to assess the global feature importance of the Machine Learning models used for heat demand forecasting in intelligent control of District Heating Systems, with motivation to facilitate their interpretability and trustworthiness, hence addressing the challenges related to adherence to communal standards, customer satisfaction and liability risks. Methodology includes use of four different approaches, namely intrinsic interpretability of Gradient Boosting method and selected post-hoc methods, namely Partial Dependence, Accumulated Local Effects and SHAP. None of the selected methods assume feature permutation or perturbations which can introduce bias due to introduction of random unrealistic values of data instances. Discussion of results is provided, including the assessment of complementarities where applicable, with specific interpretations in context of the district heating processes.

</details>


### [144] [Latent On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13040)
*Guibin Zhang,Jiayang Lyu,Ran Sun,Xinlei Yu,Haoyu Zhao,Qibing Ren,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在在策略自蒸馏（LOPD），通过将教师模型的特权上下文本身变为可端到端学习的潜在token，替代人工设计特权工件（如答案、反馈、技能、轨迹），实现代理从经验中持续自我改进。LOPD在智能体工具使用和代码生成任务上优于RLVR及OPSD、SDPO、Skill-SD等代表方法，并以不足30%的采样预算超越GRPO和Skill-SD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略自蒸馏（OPSD）通过特权自我教师提供密集监督，是自进化AI的有效途径，但现有方法依赖设计者指定的人工特权工件（如答案、反馈、技能或轨迹），限制了端到端可学习性和可扩展性，难以支撑持续自我改进所需的规模化演进。

Method: LOPD将教师特权上下文本身变为端到端可学习的：首先检索相关经验并将其组合为连续潜在token，用于条件化自我教师；学生则从任务与交互历史中生成轨迹，并在每个访问的序列前缀处接收密集的token级监督。此外引入特权边际（privileged-margin）目标函数来稳定并调控潜在上下文的学习过程。

Result: 实验表明LOPD：(I) 在智能体工具使用和代码生成任务上均优于RLVR及OPSD、SDPO、Skill-SD等OPSD代表方法；(II) 以不足GRPO和Skill-SD 30%的采样预算即实现更优性能，学习效率显著更高。消融实验证实使特权上下文可学习是实现上述增益的必要条件。

Conclusion: LOPD通过将特权上下文从人工指定的固定形态转变为可端到端学习的潜在表示，降低了OPSD对设计者专家的依赖，提升了可扩展性与自监督能力，为更可扩展、更自驱动的智能体进化范式提供了方向性进展。

Abstract: Enabling agents to learn from experience and internalize it into their policy has become a central problem in self-evolving AI. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) offers an effective pathway by using a privileged self-teacher to provide dense supervision on the student's own trajectories; however, existing methods still rely heavily on designer-specified privileged artifacts (e.g., answers, feedback, skills, or trajectories), limiting the end-to-end learnability and scalability required for continual self-improvement. In this work, we introduce Latent On-Policy Self-Distillation (LOPD), which, rather than proposing another hand-crafted OPSD variant with a newly prescribed form of privileged context, makes the teacher's privileged context itself learnable end-to-end from experience. Technically, LOPD retrieves relevant experiences and composes them into continuous latent tokens that condition a self-teacher, while the student generates trajectories from the task and interaction history and receives dense token-level supervision at every visited prefix. We further introduce a privileged-margin objective to stabilize and regulate the learning of latent context. Empirically, LOPD demonstrates (I) strong performance, outperforming RLVR and representative OPSD methods including OPSD, SDPO, and Skill-SD across both agentic tool use and code generation; and (II) high learning efficiency, surpassing GRPO and Skill-SD with less than 30% of their rollout budget. Ablation studies further provide direct evidence that making privileged context learnable is necessary for realizing these gains. Together, these results position LOPD as a step toward a more scalable and self-directed paradigm for agent evolution.

</details>


### [145] [A Multispectral Framework for the Detection of Calcium Carbide-Induced Ripening and Shelf-Life Estimation in Climacteric Fruits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13073)
*Gurbhit Chaurakoti,Harshit Kumar,Hani Kumar,Anurag Singh,Ram Asrey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多光谱传感的非侵入性框架，利用AS7265x光谱三合一传感器在410-940nm范围内的18个离散波长，结合PCA降维和XGBoost算法，用于区分自然成熟、乙烯利催熟和碳化钙(CaC2)催熟的芒果与香蕉，同时估算成熟进度百分比和剩余货架期。芒果分类准确率达95%，香蕉达81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级碳化钙被非法但普遍用于催熟芒果、香蕉等跃变型水果，会残留砷和磷等有害物质，对人体健康构成显著风险。现有检测方法缺乏非侵入性、快速且能同时判断催熟方式和成熟状态的解决方案，因此需要开发一种有效的非破坏性检测框架。

Method: 研究采用AS7265x光谱三合一传感器获取芒果和香蕉在18个离散波长（410-940nm）的可见-近红外光谱数据。特征工程整合了方法间光谱方差、特定波长的强度比值以及温度和湿度等环境参数。通过主成分分析(PCA)进行降维，保留前5-7个主成分（>90%光谱方差）。随后训练三个独立的基于极端梯度提升(XGBoost)的学习算法，分别用于催熟方法分类、剩余货架期定量估计和成熟进度定量估计。

Result: 芒果样本的分类准确率为95%，碳化钙类别的召回率为0.67；香蕉模型准确率为81%，碳化钙类别召回率为0.74。CaC2处理样本在可见光区域表现出更剧烈的光谱强度下降，与叶绿素加速降解和类胡萝卜素发育一致。

Conclusion: 该仪器与数据驱动相结合的方法证明了所提出的非侵入性多光谱框架在区分安全催熟与碳化钙催熟水果方面的有效性，同时也能估计成熟进度和剩余货架期，具有实际食品安全检测应用潜力。
