A band-tail model with adjusted band width is applied to fit the sub-threshold swings at all temperatures. The calibrated parameters are expected to be applicable to other technologies because TCAD models are not technology-specific. It is also found that, except for saturation velocity, no new temperature-dependent model is needed. The calibration process and fitting strategies are detailed and are expected to be applicable to calibrate other transistor types.

</details>


### [177] [A discrete duality finite volume method with harmonic average for semiconductor drift-diffusion equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12808)
*Shuya Liu,Zhicheng Liu,Bo Lin,Chijie Zhuang,Qingyuan Shi,Weizhu Bao,Rong Zeng*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将调和平均稳定化技术融入离散对偶有限体积法（DDFV-HA）的新方案，用于解决经典FVSG方法在不规则或畸变网格上性能退化的问题。实验表明DDFV-HA在高质量网格上与FVSG等效，在低质量网格上更可靠准确，且在实际晶闸管仿真中表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体电荷输运建模中广泛使用的经典FVSG方法依赖Voronoi图，在高质量Delaunay网格上表现良好，但在不规则或畸变网格上性能退化。实际工程中复杂、不规则域内难以生成高质量网格，因此需要一种不受网格质量限制的稳健数值方案。

Method: 将调和平均稳定化（harmonic average stabilization）融入离散对偶有限体积法（DDFV），形成新的DDFV-HA格式。该方法不依赖Voronoi图，能够直接在任意网格上工作。通过在高质量和低质量网格上与FVSG进行对比实验验证方案的有效性。

Result: 实验结果表明DDFV-HA在高质量网格上与FVSG结果一致；在低质量网格上，DDFV-HA比FVSG更可靠、更准确。将DDFV-HA应用于真实晶闸管仿真进一步证实其适用于复杂不规则域中的半导体仿真。

Conclusion: DDFV-HA是一种克服网格质量限制的稳健半导体仿真方法，在高质量网格上与经典FVSG相当，在低质量网格上具有明显优势，适用于复杂不规则工程域的电荷输运仿真。

Abstract: The stationary drift-diffusion model is widely used to model charge transport in semiconductor devices. Classical methods, such as the finite volume Scharfetter--Gummel (FVSG) method, perform well on high-quality Delaunay meshes but struggle on irregular or distorted meshes due to their reliance on Voronoi diagrams. To overcome this mesh limitation, this article introduces a new approach that integrates harmonic average stabilization into the discrete duality finite volume method (DDFV-HA). To validate our scheme, we compare DDFV-HA and FVSG for semiconductor simulations on both high- and low-quality meshes. Experiments show that DDFV-HA matches FVSG on high-quality meshes and is more reliable and accurate on low-quality meshes. Applying DDFV-HA to a real-world thyristor further confirms that it is well-suited for semiconductor simulations in complex, irregular domains where high-quality meshes are not easy to generate.

</details>


### [178] [Using Diffusion Models to Estimate Uncertainties in Analytic Continuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13123)
*Sagi Meir,Daniel Freedman,Barak Hirshberg*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的生成式框架，用于虚时关联函数（iTCF）的解析延拓问题。该方法通过学习与给定iTCF相容的谱分布，不仅能够重构功率谱，还能直接量化不确定性，并引入"不确定度伪体积"指标来评估每个反演问题的内在难度。在液态仲氢的路径积分分子动力学模拟数据上验证，成功给出了自扩散系数的误差棒并标记了可能的伪峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析延拓是ill-posed的反问题，存在多个谱与同一iTCF匹配的模糊性。传统的回归方法（包括现有的神经网络方法）只输出单一解，近似于对真实解空间的平均，无法捕捉完整的不确定度分布，因此在物理上缺乏可靠性保证。

Method: 提出一个基于扩散模型（diffusion-based）的生成式框架，学习与给定iTCF相容的谱的条件分布。通过从该分布中采样，实现：（1）直接从习得分布量化不确定性；（2）定义"不确定度伪体积"（uncertainty pseudo-volume）指标来定量评估每个反演问题本身的固有难度。

Result: 将该框架应用于液态仲氢的路径积分分子动力学模拟得到的iTCF，成功获得了带误差棒的自扩散系数，并标记了一个可能为伪影的次级高频峰。相比以往的不确定度量化尝试，该生成式方法建立在具体概率论基础上，在理论上更为严谨。

Conclusion: 生成式扩散模型为解析延拓这一ill-posed问题提供了一种有理论根基的不确定性量化方案，能够区分问题本身的固有难度，并为重构谱提供更可靠的置信度度量。

Abstract: Inverse problems are ubiquitous in physics, chemistry, and engineering, arising when reconstructing hidden quantities from indirect measurements. A key example is the analytic continuation of imaginary-time correlation functions (iTCFs) to the real-frequency domain. This process requires an inverse Laplace transform, which is inherently ill-posed and highly sensitive to small input variations. Recent neural network (NN)-based methods have shown promising results by learning mappings from imaginary-time to real-frequency spectra, often outperforming traditional techniques such as maximum entropy. However, because the problem is ill-posed, many spectra fit the same iTCF. Regression-based approaches output a single solution, which approximates an average over the true solution space, and therefore fail to capture the full distribution of plausible power spectra. To address this issue, we introduce a diffusion-based framework for analytic continuation that learns the distribution of spectra consistent with a given iTCF. It offers two key advantages. First, it quantifies uncertainty directly from the learned distribution. Second, by analyzing the spread and structure of this distribution, we can quantitatively assess the intrinsic hardness of each inversion problem. We measure this hardness with a new metric, the uncertainty pseudo-volume. Applying the framework to an iTCF from a path-integral molecular dynamics simulation of liquid parahydrogen, we obtain the self-diffusion coefficient with an error bar and flag a secondary high-frequency peak as a possible spurious artifact. In contrast to previous attempts at uncertainty quantification, our generative approach rests on a concrete probabilistic basis, providing a more theoretically grounded measure of confidence in the reconstructed power spectra.

</details>


### [179] [Modeling Bond-Dependent Kitaev-like interaction in 2D Edge-Sharing Tetrahedral Magnets: FeX (X=Te, Se)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13427)
*Mengdong Li,Can Huangb,Bingjie Liu,Zhixin Liu,Yanfei Pan,Jiyu Fan,Chunlan Ma,Daning Shi,Yan Zhua*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了单层FeTe和FeSe的Kitaev类模型，首次在共边四面体配位环境中发现了由硫族介导的自旋轨道耦合与四面体晶体场几何共同诱导的键依赖伊辛型相互作用。通过第一性原理磁性各向异性能映射，揭示了Kitaev类相互作用在FeTe中主导磁各向异性，而在FeSe中与单离子各向异性激烈竞争，导致非共线局域各向异性轴并产生本征单自旋阻挫，为理解磁性无序提供了超越各向同性交换模型的微观机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为键依赖磁相互作用（如Kitaev模型）主要存在于共边八面体配位体系中，而共边四面体环境（与铁基超导体母体化合物相关）中是否存在类似相互作用尚属未知，作者希望探索这一开放问题。

Method: 构建FeTe和FeSe单层的Kitaev类模型，通过第一性原理计算获取磁性各向异性能，并将其映射到代表性线性磁序上，从而将键依赖贡献与单离子各向异性区分开。

Result: 发现Kitaev类相互作用在FeTe中主导磁各向异性，在FeSe中则与符号相反的单离子各向异性激烈竞争，产生非共线局域各向异性轴并引发本征单自旋阻挫。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了共边四面体磁体作为键依赖相互作用的新平台，将Kitaev物理的适用范围从八面体配位扩展到四面体环境。

Abstract: Bond-dependent magnetic interactions, exemplified by the Kitaev model, are known to arise from the interplay between spin-orbit coupling(SOC) and specific coordination geometries, yet their existence has so far been predominantly associated with edge-sharing octahedral systems. Whether analogous interactions survive in edge- sharing tetrahedral environments - relevant to iron-based superconductor parent compounds - remains an open question. Here, we construct a Kitaev-like model for monolayer FeTe and FeSe and demonstrate the presence of a previously unrecognized bond-dependent Ising-type interaction, induced jointly by chalcogen-mediated SOC and the tetrahedral crystal-field geometry. By mapping the first-principles calculations derived magnetic anisotropy energy mapping across representative linear magnetic orders, we disentangle the bond-dependent contributions from single-ion anisotropy. We reveal that the Kitaev-like interaction dominates the magnetic anisotropy in FeTe, whereas in FeSe, it fiercely competes with a single-ion anisotropy of opposite sign. The resulting noncollinear local anisotropy axes generate intrinsic single-site spin frustration, providing a microscopic mechanism for magnetic disorder beyond isotropic exchange models. Our results establish edge-sharing tetrahedral magnets as a new setting for bond-dependent interactions and extend the scope of Kitaev physics beyond octahedral coordination.

</details>


### [180] [Characteristic Mode Analysis of Plasmonic Nanostructures Using Hydrodynamic Volume Integral Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13435)
*Meruyert Khamitova,Ran Zhao,Doolos Aibek Uulu,Sebastian Celis Sierra,Hakan Bagci*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了将特征模式分析扩展到等离激元纳米结构的新框架。通过将流体动力学与体积积分方程耦合，简化为单一的水动力学体积积分方程，并将其离散化为广义特征值问题，从而实现了对金属纳米结构本征共振的激励无关分析。该方法不仅能识别出给定激励下未被激发的共振，还能捕捉到非局域响应带来的、局域模型不具备的额外共振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属纳米结构在亚波长尺度下具有等离激元纳米天线的应用前景，但其非局域响应需要流体动力学模型来描述。然而，现有的求解器只能给出对特定激励的响应，无法直接揭示结构的内在共振特性（本征模式），缺乏一种激励无关的模态分析方法来理解和设计等离激元纳米结构。

Method: 本研究将特征模式分析框架扩展到等离激元纳米结构：首先将耦合的流体动力学方程与体积积分方程化简为关于感应电流的单一水动力学体积积分方程；然后将该方程离散化，并在特征模式分析框架中构造广义特征值问题；求解该特征值问题即得到特征模式电流和模态显著性曲线，从而获得结构的激励无关共振特性。

Result: 通过对三种金属纳米结构（纳米球、纳米棒和纳米二聚体）的验证，该方法成功识别了每个结构的本征共振，包括给定激励下未被激发的共振。更重要的是，该方法捕捉到了由非局域响应引起的额外共振，这些共振在局域模型中不存在，展示了方法在揭示非局域效应方面的独特优势。

Conclusion: 该框架为等离激元纳米结构的模态机制提供了物理洞察，并可作为分析和设计等离激元纳米结构的实用工具，弥补了传统方法只能分析特定激励响应的不足。

Abstract: Metallic nanostructures confine electromagnetic fields at subwavelength scales, making them attractive as plasmonic nanoantennas. At these scales, the response of metals becomes nonlocal, and the hydrodynamic model is widely used to capture this response. However, existing solvers provide only the response to a prescribed excitation and do not directly reveal the intrinsic resonances of the structure. This work extends the characteristic mode analysis to plasmonic nanostructures to enable excitation-independent modal analysis of their resonant behavior. For simple metals, the coupled hydrodynamic and volume integral equations are reduced to a single hydrodynamic volume integral equation in terms of the induced current. The equation is discretized and cast as a generalized eigenvalue problem within the characteristic mode analysis framework, whose solution yields the characteristic mode currents and modal significance curves of the structure. The proposed framework is validated through three metallic nanostructures: a nanosphere, a nanorod, and a nanodimer. The results show that the method identifies the intrinsic resonances of each structure, including resonances not excited by a given source and additional resonances arising from the nonlocal response, which are absent in local models. The proposed framework provides physical insight into the modal mechanisms of plasmonic nanostructures and serves as a practical tool for their analysis and design.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Wave-Assisted Propulsion in Bimodal Sea States: Hydrodynamic Performance and Hydroelastic Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12488)
*Avinash Kumar Pandey,Jung-Hee Seo,Rajat Mittal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了浸没式拍动水翼在海浪与风浪共存的双峰海况下的波浪辅助推进（WAP）性能，通过高保真流固耦合模拟发现推进性能遵循与单色波和单峰波一致的有效峰值频率缩放规律，展示了该缩放框架的稳健性，并指出最优归一化调谐比范围狭窄但刚性弹簧需随海况自适应调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往对波浪辅助推进系统（WAP）的研究主要集中于单色波或单峰波浪条件，对真实海洋环境中WAP性能的探索存在空白。本文旨在填补这一空缺，研究双峰海况下（涌浪与风浪共存）WAP系统的水动力与水弹性响应，以评估其在实际海洋环境中的推进性能与设计指导意义。

Method: 采用高保真流固耦合（FSI）模拟方法，对浸没式拍动水翼在平静、过渡和风暴三种典型双峰海况下的水动力与水弹性响应进行研究。水翼通过扭转弹簧实现被动俯仰运动，系统性地分析不同海况下推进性能与弹性调谐参数之间的关系。

Result: 尽管双峰波浪激励增加复杂性，推进性能仍遵循先前在单色波和单峰波中建立的有效峰值频率缩放规律，证明了该缩放框架在多种海况下的鲁棒性。最优归一化调谐比保持在较窄范围内，但量纲上的扭转弹簧刚度随海况特征而变化，需要自适应水弹性调谐来最大化推力。

Conclusion: WAP系统在真实海洋条件下能够稳健地产生波浪驱动推力，为波浪驱动海洋推进系统的改进设计提供了实践指导，同时强调了自适应水弹性调谐在最大化推进性能方面的重要性。

Abstract: Wave-assisted propulsion (WAP) systems harvest ocean wave energy to generate propulsive thrust, offering a promising approach for improving endurance and energy efficiency of marine vehicles. Previous studies have focused primarily on monochromatic or unimodal wave conditions, leaving WAP performance in realistic ocean environments largely unexplored. This study investigates the hydrodynamic and hydroelastic response of a submerged flapping hydrofoil operating in bimodal sea states generated by the coexistence of swell and wind-sea wave systems. High-fidelity fluid--structure interaction simulations are performed for representative calm, transitional, and storm conditions, with passive pitching provided through a torsional spring. Simulations show that, despite increased complexity of bimodal wave forcing, propulsion performance follows the same effective peak frequency scaling previously established for monochromatic and unimodal waves, demonstrating the robustness of this scaling framework across a broad range of sea states. The findings further reveal that while the optimal normalized tuning ratio remains within a narrow range, dimensional torsional spring stiffness varies with sea state characteristics, highlighting the need for adaptive hydroelastic tuning to maximize thrust. Overall, the results demonstrate that WAP systems provide a robust means of generating wave-powered thrust under realistic ocean conditions while providing practical guidance for improved design of wave-powered marine propulsion systems.

</details>


### [182] [Dispersion and clustering of deformable droplets in turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12554)
*Yushu Lin,John Palmore*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文研究了湍流（均匀各向同性湍流，HIT）中非球形（变形）液滴的弥散与聚集行为，发现液滴变形对其弥散和聚集的影响依赖于惯性区间：弱惯性液滴变形削弱弥散与优先聚集，强惯性液滴变形增强聚集但削弱弥散；变形液滴需更高的Stokes数才能达到相同聚集水平，且变形的非稳态形状动力学引入了有效惯性效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空喷雾燃烧应用中，传统LPT方法将喷雾视为球形离散粒子，忽略了液滴变形对喷雾动力学的影响。以往研究虽然指出非球形对蒸发、燃烧和阻力系数的重要作用，但仅限于孤立液滴在均匀流中的理想构型。为了在更真实的构型中研究液滴变形效应，本文以均匀各向同性湍流为框架，探究变形对液滴弥散的影响。

Method: 采用均匀各向同性湍流（HIT）作为研究框架，使用全非稳态TAB（Taylor Analogy Breakup）模型模拟液滴变形与形状振荡，并结合LPT方法追踪液滴运动。研究了不同Stokes数的液滴，以考察变形与惯性之间的相互作用，并对比了全非稳态TAB模型与其稳态极限，以区分稳态变形与瞬态形状振荡的各自贡献。

Result: 液滴变形对弥散和聚集的影响取决于惯性区间：弱惯性液滴的变形同时削弱弥散和优先聚集；强惯性液滴的变形增强优先聚集但削弱弥散。变形液滴需要更高的Stokes数才能达到相同的聚集水平。对于无惯性液滴，变形会诱导出有效惯性效应。全非稳态TAB模型与其稳态极限的对比表明，非稳态形状动力学影响时间相关统计，但不改变平均聚集模式。

Conclusion: 变形及非稳态形状振荡对液滴弥散和聚集预测至关重要，考虑液滴变形及其非稳态形状振荡是准确预测湍流中液滴弥散与聚集的必要条件，对航空喷雾燃烧建模具有重要意义。

Abstract: Motivated by the application of spray combustion in aviation industry, this work investigates the dispersion of non-spherical droplets in turbulence. The most common strategy for modeling sprays relies on LPT method, which represents the spray as a discrete collection of spherical particles. One limitation of LPT is that it neglects the influence of droplet deformation on spray dynamics. Prior studies have highlighted the importance of non-sphericity in droplet vaporization, combustion and drag coefficient. However, these works are restricted to idealized configurations such as an isolated droplet in a uniform flow. To study droplet deformation in a more realistic configuration, we adopt homogeneous isotropic turbulence (HIT) as the framework to investigate its effect on droplet dispersion. Droplets of various Stokes number are studied to investigate the interplay between deformation and inertia. Analysis of droplet statistics reveals that the impact of droplet deformation on both dispersion and clustering is dependent on the inertia regime. For weakly-inertial droplets, deformation weakens both dispersion and preferential concentration, whereas for strongly-inertial droplets, deformation tends to enhance preferential concentration while weakening dispersion. The results also suggest that to achieve the same level of clustering, deformed droplets require a higher Stokes number. Interestingly, for non-inertial droplets, the deformation seems to induce an effective inertia. This is verified by a comparison between the full unsteady TAB model and its steady-state limit, which suggests that unsteady shape dynamics affect temporal correlation statistics, but leave the mean clustering pattern unchanged. These findings demonstrate that accounting for droplet deformation and its unsteady shape oscillation is essential for accurately predicting droplet dispersion and clustering in turbulence.

</details>


### [183] [Infrared imaging of thermally-driven jets and eddies in planetary-style laboratory turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12694)
*Cy S. David,Remy Monville,Daphne Lemasquerier,Lvcian Vltava,Jonathan M. Aurnou*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文是2025年美国物理学会流体动力学分部（APS DFD）Milton van Dyke奖的海报获奖作品，相关工作已在DFD流体运动画廊展出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作旨在展示流体动力学中具有美学与科学价值的流动现象，并通过画廊形式传播流体运动之美，获得Milton van Dyke奖的认可。

Method: 该论文作为获奖海报的配套说明，其具体方法依赖于原始海报内容，此处仅提供获奖信息及在线画廊链接。

Result: 该工作获得了2025年APS DFD Milton van Dyke Award（海报类奖项），并被收录于DFD流体运动画廊。

Conclusion: 该论文是流体运动画廊中的获奖海报作品，代表了流体动力学可视化领域的高水平贡献。

Abstract: This paper is associated with a poster winner of a 2025 American Physical Society's Division of Fluid Dynamics (DFD) Milton van Dyke Award for work presented at the DFD Gallery of Fluid Motion. The original poster is available online at the Gallery of Fluid Motion, at https://doi.org/10.1103/APS.DFD.2025.GFM.P012

</details>


### [184] [Bayesian optimization and topographic exploration of drag-reducing dimples for aerodynamic surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12826)
*Sangjoon Lee,M. Erden Yildizdag,Haris M. Sheikh*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究将凹坑设计视为包含凹坑类型、深度、平面尺度和流向拉伸四个变量的混合变量优化问题，采用贝叶斯优化器MixMOBO结合浸没边界大涡模拟，在摩擦雷诺数180的通道流中系统探索设计空间。最优解为相对较深、完全密排、流向拉长的菱形凹坑，实现了13.2%的减阻效果，显著高于此前报道值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凹坑有望降低气动表面阻力，但其净收益对地形几何高度敏感，而此前缺乏对综合设计空间的系统映射与探索，难以确定最优凹坑构型及主导因素。

Method: 采用混合变量贝叶斯优化器MixMOBO优化四个设计变量（凹坑类型、深度、平面尺度、流向拉伸），耦合浸没边界大涡模拟，对恒定流量通道流（对应摩擦雷诺数180的平坦通道）进行数值评估；并通过高斯过程元模型敏感性分析及近壁流动分析揭示主导因素与减阻机理。

Result: 最优设计（相对深的、完全密排的流向伸长菱形凹坑）实现13.2%减阻，优于以往报道值。敏感性分析显示凹坑拓扑结构为主导因素，覆盖率与伸长主要通过交互作用影响，深度本身无普遍性符号效应。近壁流动分析表明，最优设计产生完全贴附的沟槽状流动，而较差设计则产生局部流动分离，招致不利压差阻力。

Conclusion: 凹坑拓扑是减阻性能的首要决定因素，覆盖率与伸长通过交互作用发挥影响，深度不存在普遍最优趋势。最佳减阻凹坑应具备完全贴附的沟槽状流态、避免流动分离，据此提供了关键的设计指导原则。

Abstract: Dimples offer a promising route to reducing drag on aerodynamic surfaces. However, whether such shallow concavities yield a net benefit depends sensitively on their topography, which demands systematic mapping and exploration of their comprehensive design space. In this study, dimple design is examined as a mixed-variable optimization over four design variables: dimple type, depth, in-plane scale, and streamwise stretch. The design space is explored using MixMOBO, a Bayesian optimizer, coupled with immersed-boundary large eddy simulations of channel flows at a constant flow rate corresponding to a flat channel at a friction Reynolds number of 180. The optimal solution, a relatively deep, fully packed, streamwise-elongated diamond dimple, attains a 13.2% drag reduction, notably above previously reported values. A Gaussian process metamodel sensitivity analysis identifies dimple topology as the dominant factor, with coverage and elongation acting mainly through interactions, and depth itself carrying no universal sign. A near-wall flow analysis links the leading designs to fully attached, groove-like flow, whereas poorer designs tend to produce local flow separation that incurs adverse form drag. From these findings, key design insights for drag-reducing dimples are provided.

</details>


### [185] [Controlling the dynamics of an electric-field-driven droplet on a lubricant-infused micropillar surface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12868)
*Geng Wang,Junyu Yang,Timan Lei,Jin Chen,Halim Kusumaatmaja,Kai Li,Kai H. Luo*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种格子玻尔兹曼方案，用于研究电场驱动润滑液注入表面（LIS）上微液滴的动力学行为，建立了运动液滴所受阻力和电力的定量表达式，并通过可编程操控实验验证了模型的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电场驱动的液滴动力学在LIS上具有广泛的应用前景（如药物制造和3D打印），但由于LIS上存在多种可能的润滑状态，液滴动力学机制尚未被充分理解，现有研究缺乏可靠的理论模型。

Method: 开发了一种格子玻尔兹曼（lattice Boltzmann）数值方案，完整耦合界面流动与电流体动力学，用于模拟微柱LIS上电场驱动液滴的动力学过程；同时结合模拟与解析计算，建立了运动液滴所受阻力和电力的定量表达式。

Result: 建立的定量模型能够准确捕捉可编程操控过程中的液滴动力学，包括周期性运动和长距离输运行为。

Conclusion: 所建立的理论模型可为液滴动力学的精确控制提供可靠基础，消除了对试错试验的依赖，具有显著的应用潜力。

Abstract: As a non-contact control approach, electric field (EF) can be utilised to drive droplet dynamics on a lubricant-infused surface (LIS), with numerous potential applications ranging from drug manufacturing to 3D printing. However, the resulting droplet dynamics remain poorly understood, especially as there are several possible droplet lubrication states on LIS. Here, we develop a lattice Boltzmann scheme that fully captures the interplay between the interfacial flows and electrohydrodynamics and harness it to investigate EF driven droplets on micropillar LIS. Combining simulations and analytical calculations, we establish quantitative expressions for the drag force and the electric force acting on a moving droplet. We demonstrate that the models can accurately capture droplet dynamics during programmable manipulation, including periodic motion and long-distance transport. Such reliable theoretical models can potentially transform precision control of droplet dynamics by removing the reliance on trial and error tests.

</details>


### [186] [Second-order stochastic modeling of particle resuspension: macroscopic degeneracy and anomalous pre-detachment transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12941)
*David Ben-Shlomo,Ronen Berkovich,Eyal Fattal*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种二阶马尔可夫拉格朗日随机模型，通过引入有限相关的切向加速度来增强角速度状态，用于粒子再悬浮建模。该模型与传统一阶模型在宏观悬浮分数上几乎完全重叠，揭示了不同随机描述之间存在宏观简并性，而多尺度轨迹统计能够打破这种简并性，揭示出加速增广公式带来的短时正则性变化、角速度相关维持、更强的方向不对称性以及雷诺数依赖的异常输运窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统粒子再悬浮模型通常仅用宏观悬浮分数来评估，但这种积分观测量可能掩盖导致颗粒脱离的时序动态。需要寻找能够区分不同随机描述模型的判别量，以更深入地理解颗粒从附着到脱离的动力学过程。

Method: 通过将角速度状态与有限相关切向加速度耦合，构建二阶马尔可夫拉格朗日随机模型。在湍流通道流动（Re_τ∈[60,430]）中检验该模型，并与成熟的一阶公式进行比较。采用多尺度轨迹统计（包括短时正则性、角速度相关性、方向不对称性、增量尾部、幸存者条件下的均方位移）作为判别工具。

Result: 两个模型在宏观悬浮分数上几乎完全重叠，但多尺度轨迹统计能清晰地区分它们。加速度增广模型将短时正则性从S_2(τ)∝τ改变为S_2(τ)∝τ^2，维持角速度相关性，产生更强的方向不对称性和更重的增量尾部。幸存者条件均方位移揭示了雷诺数依赖的异常输运窗口，其中附着颗粒系综增长速度快于扩散参考，局部接近弹道和超弹道标度。在Re_τ≈60处，轨迹进入一种独特的低雷诺数统计状态。

Conclusion: 多尺度轨迹统计是区分随机再悬浮模型的关键判别量，有限加速度相关包含了超越宏观脱离动力学的额外动力学信息。宏观悬浮分数不足以全面评估再悬浮模型，需要多尺度轨迹级验证。

Abstract: Particle resuspension models are commonly evaluated using the macroscopic resuspended fraction, although this integrated observable may conceal the temporal dynamics leading to detachment. Here, a second-order Markovian Lagrangian stochastic model is developed by augmenting the angular-velocity state with a finite-correlated tangential acceleration. The model is examined over turbulent channel flows with ($Re_τ\in [60, 430]$) and compared with an established first-order formulation. The two models produce nearly overlapping resuspended fractions, revealing a macroscopic degeneracy between distinct stochastic descriptions. Multiscale trajectory statistics break this degeneracy. The acceleration-augmented formulation changes the short-time regularity from $S_2(τ) \propto τ$ to $S_2(τ) \propto τ^2$, sustains angular-velocity correlation, and produces stronger directional asymmetry and heavier increment tails. The survivor-conditioned mean square displacement exposes a Reynolds-number-dependent anomalous-transport window, in which the attached-particle ensemble grows more rapidly than the diffusive reference and locally approaches ballistic and super-ballistic scaling before crossing toward diffusion-like transport. This finite-time pathway records how particles approach detachment and is compressed out of the macroscopic resuspended fraction. At $Re_τ\approx 60$, the trajectories enter a distinct low-Reynolds-number statistical state characterized by converging velocity and acceleration decorrelation and near-Gaussian increments. These results establish multiscale trajectory statistics as essential discriminants between stochastic resuspension models and identify finite acceleration correlation as a source of dynamical information beyond macroscopic detachment kinetics.

</details>


### [187] [Intermittent Vortex Merging and Extreme Drag in Transitional Airfoil Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13398)
*Shishir Gautam,Chitrarth Prasad*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过二维直接数值模拟研究过渡翼型流动中罕见的剧烈阻力波动事件，发现这些极端事件源于分离剪切层中的次级涡量喷发和主涡成簇释放机制，并提出了通过控制次级涡动力学来抑制极端气动载荷的可能途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过渡翼型流动中，偏离名义Kármán涡街的间歇性事件可引发罕见而显著的阻力突变，理解这类极端事件的物理机制对于气动载荷预测与流动控制至关重要。现有研究对正常涡脱落的理解较为成熟，但对极端事件的触发机制和演化路径缺乏系统性认知。

Method: 采用NACA0012翼型的二维直接数值模拟（DNS），攻角5°，来流马赫数0.4，弦长雷诺数分别为5×10⁴和5×10⁵。通过事件分辨分析（event-resolved analysis）解析主涡释放的物理过程，追踪次级涡量喷发、涡簇形成及其在近尾缘区域的相互作用。

Result: 在低雷诺数下，单个主涡通过壁面产生的反向次级涡量喷发从分离剪切层释放；正常脱落时每个涡仅经历单次释放并保持孤立。极端事件则由短时间间隔内的多次次级涡喷发产生紧凑的主涡群，其初始流向间距较小，经差异对流进一步压缩后在近尾缘区域发生强相互作用（包括变形、丝化及核心合并），产生局部吸力峰和尖锐的阻力跃升。在Re=5×10⁵下也观察到类似的紧凑涡组织和近尾缘相互作用，表明该事件通路在不同雷诺数下具有普适性。

Conclusion: 极端气动载荷事件由成簇涡释放而非单个涡释放触发，通过次级涡量动力学控制涡释放时机可能是破坏成簇释放并缓解极端气动载荷的有效途径。

Abstract: Intermittent departures from nominal Kelvin--Helmholtz shedding can produce rare and pronounced drag excursions in transitional airfoil flow. We examine these events using two-dimensional direct numerical simulations of flow over a NACA0012 airfoil at an angle of attack of $5^\circ$, a freestream Mach number of $0.4$, and chord-based Reynolds numbers of $5\times10^4$ and $5\times10^5$. At the lower Reynolds number, event-resolved analysis shows that individual primary vortices are released from the separated shear layer through the eruption of wall-generated, opposite-signed secondary vorticity. Each eruption interrupts the connection between a developing primary vortex and its feeding shear layer, releasing the vortex downstream. During nominal shedding, the vortex reaching the trailing-edge region is associated with a single such release and remains sufficiently isolated to pass the trailing edge without strong collective interaction. Extreme events instead arise through clustered vortex release, in which several secondary-vorticity eruptions occur within a short interval and produce a compact group of primary vortices with small initial streamwise spacing. Differential convection further reduces their spacing and promotes strong near-trailing-edge interactions, where the combined pressure footprint of these vortices produces a localized suction peak and a sharp increase in drag. These interactions range from prolonged deformation and filamentation to rapid core coalescence. Similar compact vortex organization and near-trailing-edge interactions are recovered at $Re=5\times10^5$, indicating that the downstream event pathway persists despite the smaller vortical scales.These findings suggest that controlling vortex-release timing through secondary-vorticity dynamics may provide a route to disrupt clustered release and mitigate extreme aerodynamic loading.

</details>


### [188] [Modern aerodynamics models do not capture important unsteady forces--the failure of the quasi-steady approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13424)
*Victoria M. Malarczyk,Marcus Hultmark*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文通过高增压风洞实验，研究了大型风力机叶片在非定常气动条件下的准定常近似有效性，发现该近似在大范围通常会应用的频率区间内不成立，从而导致传统模型在接近失速条件下严重低估叶片载荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代风力机转子直径已超过200米，运行在缺乏经验数据和模型验证的条件下。准定常近似常用于简化非定常气动响应的建模，但其在大尺度叶片条件下的有效性尚未得到充分验证，可能导致载荷预测不准确。

Method: 使用高增压风洞模拟大型风力机所经历的实际流动条件，系统地评估准定常近似在不同频率范围内的适用性，并与真实非定常气动响应进行对比。

Result: 发现准定常近似在其通常被应用的很大频率范围内并不成立，这意味着传统模型会显著低估风力机叶片载荷，尤其在接近失速条件下更为严重。

Conclusion: 准定常近似在大型风力机实际运行条件下存在严重局限性，需要重新审视其适用边界；鉴于现代风力机常在近失速工况下运行，现有载荷预测模型可能导致设计安全裕度不足，亟需开发更可靠的非定常气动模型。

Abstract: Aerodynamic unsteadiness is inherent to the operation of many engineering applications, especially those that involve large-scale rotating blades, such as modern wind turbines. Over the past few decades, wind turbine rotors have grown rapidly and are now exceeding 200 meters in diameter, causing them to operate in conditions where limited empirical data are available, and where models have not been validated. Here, we use a highly pressurized wind tunnel to probe these conditions and evaluate the quasi-steady approximation, often used to simplify the modeling of the unsteady aerodynamic response of an airfoil to slow changes in inflow conditions. We find that the quasi-steady approximation is not valid for a large range of frequencies where it is normally applied. This finding suggests that the loading on wind turbine blades will be significantly underestimated when using conventional models, especially near stalling conditions, which modern wind turbines typically encounter.

</details>


### [189] [Lattice Boltzmann Method for Compressible Navier-Stokes-Fourier Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13437)
*Fedor Bukreev,Adrian Kummerländer,Mathias J. Krause*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 一篇通过符号编译器自动从声明的三维可压缩Navier-Stokes-Fourier方程组推导出的格子Boltzmann方案，在D3Q7格子上以单精度运行，通过精确解和参考数据验证，在超音速Taylor-Green涡（M0=1.25）512^3分辨率下，其膨胀耗散精度比七种对比求解器中最优者高出30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统格子Boltzmann方法在可压缩流动中面临数值不稳定和精度不足的问题，本文旨在通过符号编译器自动生成满足热力学一致性（含粘性应力与热通量作为输运状态量）的三维可压缩N-S-F求解方案，并评估其超越现有求解器的能力。

Method: 该方法基于符号编译器从可声明的三维可压缩N-S-F方程组自动推导格子Boltzmann格式；系统将粘性应力和热通量作为输运状态量；在D3Q7格子上以单精度离散化；包含自动生成的碰撞算子、本构闭包以及附加的激波传感器，所有算子均为局部（只读取所在胞元），数据仅通过沿格子特征线流传播递。

Result: 对Sod和Becker精确解进行验证时，捕获的激波厚度具有一阶收敛性。在M0=1.25的超音速Taylor-Green涡、512^3分辨率下，该方案对参考膨胀耗散的匹配程度比七种对比求解器中最优者还高出30%。

Conclusion: 该自动派生的格子Boltzmann方案在激波捕获上具有一阶收敛特性，并在超音速湍流的耗散耗散预测上显著优于现有的多种对比求解器，同时保持了算子的局域性和数据流的流送特征，展示了符号编译器驱动自动生成高精度可压缩格子Boltzmann求解器的潜力。

Abstract: A lattice Boltzmann scheme for the three-dimensional compressible Navier--Stokes--Fourier equations, derived automatically from the declared system by a symbolic compiler, is validated against exact solutions and published reference data. The declared system carries the viscous stress and the heat flux as transported state, and is discretized on a D3Q7 lattice in single precision. Against the exact Sod and Becker solutions the captured shock thickness converges at first order. On the supersonic Taylor-Green vortex at $M_0 = 1.25$ the scheme at $512^3$ matches the reference dilatational dissipation more closely than seven compared solvers, by thirty percent over the next best. Every operator in this solver, the generated collision and constitutive closure and the added shock sensor alike, reads only the cell it acts on, and data reaches a neighbor only by streaming along the lattice characteristics.

</details>


### [190] [Air-water cavity in multi-plunging jet impacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13479)
*Narendra Dev,Hèléne Scolan,J. John Soundar Jerome,Jean-Philippe Matas*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文报道了紧密排列的垂直射流冲击液面时，在冲击点形成的一种新型穹顶状气-水空腔。通过高速成像、激光诱导荧光和光学相位检测等手段，揭示了该空腔由多个空气茎和空气片组成，其拉伸、收缩和夹断过程产生气泡，并描述了从几微米到约5毫米的宽广气泡尺寸分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射流冲击液面产生的气泡和空腔现象在许多工业和自然过程中具有重要意义，但此前研究主要针对单一射流或稀疏射流阵列。紧密排列的多射流（类似淋浴头构型）冲击液面时形成的空腔结构及其气泡产生机制尚不清楚，理解该过程对于气液混合、充气等工程应用具有价值。

Method: 采用多环同心圆形射流阵列（类淋浴头构型）作为注入装置，结合高速成像、激光诱导荧光以及光学相位检测探针三种测量手段，对空腔形态演化进行可视化观测；同时系统测量了不同冲击速度、射流数量和射流间距下空腔尺寸的变化。

Result: 发现空腔由多个空气茎和空气片组成，这些结构在拉伸、回缩和夹断过程中产生气泡，并观察到多种气泡产生机制以及气泡云中的聚并与分裂现象。气泡尺寸跨度从几微米到约5毫米。空腔尺寸随冲击速度、射流数量和间距变化而变化。

Conclusion: 提出该独特的穹顶状气-水空腔是由连续的射流环逐渐与池水混合时的液体卷吸作用所产生的，为理解多射流冲击液面的气泡生成机理提供了新的认识。

Abstract: We report the formation of an original dome-shaped air-water cavity at the impact site of closely-packed plunging water jets. The injector assembly consists of concentric rings of circular jets, similar to a shower-head configuration. We infer from high-speed imaging, LASER-induced fluorescence, and optical phase detection probes that the cavity consists of multiple stems and sheets of air that stretch, retract and pinch off to produce bubbles. We also illustrate various bubble production mechanisms in the cavity, along with coalescence and bubble breakup scenarios in the subsequent bubble cloud. Thereby, we describe the wide range of bubble sizes generated by such jets, from a few micrometers up to about $5$~mm. Measurements of the cavity size are carried out for varying impact velocity, number of jets, and jet spacing. We propose that this distinctive air-water cavity is generated by liquid entrainment when successive rings of plunging jets progressively mix with the pool water.

</details>


### [191] [DD-RNO: A Domain-Decomposed Routed Neural Operator for Airfoil Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13490)
*T. A. Mehta,P. S. Bhati,H. D. Akolekar*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出DD-RNO（域分解路由神经算子），一种用于翼型RANS流场预测的深度学习代理模型。它通过可微域路由机制将流场划分为无黏、边界层和尾迹区域并分派到专门的解码器，同时引入学习规范化求积（LCQ）用流场条件化的学习积分权重直接从表面压力预测升力和阻力。在AirfRANS基准上，速度场MSE较最强基线降低17倍（ux）和12倍（uy），分布外雷诺数外推下扩大到23倍；LCQ将阻力MSE降低7.5倍，阻力秩相关系数从0.250提升到0.997。消除任一组件都会导致性能大幅退化。推理速度约144毫秒/样本，较传统RANS求解器快10000倍，适合实时气动设计与优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习代理在翼型RANS流场预测中面临两大瓶颈：(1)单一神经架构无法同时精确解析近壁尖锐边界层和光滑远场势流；(2)从连续场近似计算壁面法向速度梯度的数值不稳定性损害了力的预测精度。

Method: DD-RNO结合谱几何编码器与两项物理引导的创新：(a)可微域路由机制，将流场划分为无黏、边界层和尾迹区域，将查询点分派到专门的区域解码器；(b)学习规范化求积（LCQ），用流场条件化的学习积分权重替代不稳定的压力积分，直接从表面压力预测升力和阻力。

Result: 在AirfRANS基准上，速度场MSE较最强基线降低17倍（ux）和12倍（uy），分布外雷诺数外推下扩大至23倍；LCQ将阻力MSE降低7.5倍，阻力秩相关从ρ=0.250提升到ρ=0.997；消融实验表明去除域路由会使速度误差增加8.2倍，去除LCQ会使相对阻力误差增加40余倍；推理约144毫秒/样本，较传统RANS求解器快10000倍。

Conclusion: DD-RNO通过域分解与学习积分两大物理引导创新，同时解决了边界层/远场多尺度建模和力预测数值不稳定两大瓶颈。路由机制能随所编码的物理规律泛化而非仅拟合训练分布，两个组件缺一不可。其精度与速度足以支撑实时气动设计与优化闭环。

Abstract: Deep learning surrogates for RANS flow prediction around airfoils face two persistent bottlenecks. A single neural architecture cannot simultaneously resolve sharp near-wall boundary layers and smooth far-field potential flow. Additionally, force prediction is undermined by the numerical instability of computing wall-normal velocity gradients from continuous-field approximations.
  Both of these points are addressed with a DD-RNO (domain-decomposed routed neural operator), combining a spectral geometry encoder with two physics-guided innovations: (a) a differentiable domain routing mechanism that partitions the flow field into inviscid, boundary-layer, and wake regimes---dispatching query points to specialized regional decoders, and (b) learned canonical quadrature (LCQ), which replaces unstable pressure integration with flow-conditioned, learned integration weights that predict lift and drag directly from surface pressure.
  On the AirfRANS benchmark, DD-RNO cuts velocity field mean-square error (MSE) by 17$x$ ($u_x$) and 12$x$ ($u_y$) over the strongest baseline, widening to 23$x$ under out-of-distribution Reynolds extrapolation---evidence that the routing mechanism generalizes with the physics it encodes rather than merely fitting the training distribution. LCQ reduces drag MSE by 7.5$x$ relative to conventional pressure integration and raises drag rank correlation from $ρ= 0.250$ to $ρ= 0.997$. Ablations confirm that both components are indispensable to performance: removing domain routing increases velocity error by 8.2$x$, and removing LCQ increases relative drag error more than 40-fold. At ~144 ms per sample---a 10,000$x$ speedup over conventional RANS solvers---DD-RNO offers a surrogate accurate and fast enough for real-time aerodynamic design and optimization loops.

</details>
